To systematically characterize this effect, we reformulate synthetic medical image detection as an audit of multimodal robustness at the image-record interface and introduce a paired benchmark that holds the image fixed while swapping controlled metadata variants. Across multiple imaging modalities, we evaluate diverse open-weight and frontier API VLMs and quantify how metadata alone shifts authenticity predictions. Our benchmark provides a standardized tool for assessing and improving multimodal robustness beyond image-only settings. The code is available at https://github.com/chiuhaohao/Beyond-Visual-Forensics.

</details>


### [36] [Transferable Attack against Face Swapping in an Extended Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25376)
*Mingzhi Lyu,Yi Huang,Jun Xie,Zihao Zhao,Hong Xu,Adams Wai-Kin Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AIR，一种无需替代换脸模型的可迁移攻击方法，通过重照明与加性扰动联合误导主体无关换脸模型的身份提取模块，在攻击成功率与视觉质量上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度换脸模型虽有娱乐应用价值，但带来隐私与安全风险。现有防护方法如深度伪造检测偏被动，对抗扰动方法对未知的主体无关换脸模型迁移性不足，因此需要一种更主动、可迁移且视觉自然的保护/攻击机制。

Method: AIR基于重照明函数构造Additive Identity attack，同时利用重照明扰动和加性扰动扩大攻击空间，以误导主体无关换脸模型中的身份提取模块。方法还引入自适应平移不变操作与光照控制方案，以提升迁移性、保持攻击效果并改善图像视觉质量；同时给出攻击空间扩展的数学证明。

Result: 在1000组图像对和多种先进主体无关换脸模型上进行实验，包括GAN与扩散模型类型的换脸方法。结果显示，AIR在攻击成功率和图像质量方面均超过现有攻击方法。

Conclusion: AIR证明了结合重照明与加性扰动能够有效提升针对未知主体无关换脸模型的可迁移攻击能力，并在不依赖替代换脸模型的情况下实现较强效果与较自然视觉表现。

Abstract: Although deep Face Swapping (FS) models may benefit the entertainment industry, they pose severe threats to privacy and security. Existing protections, including deepfake detection and adversarial perturbation, are either passive responses or ineffective to unseen subject-agnostic FS models. In this paper, we propose a transferable attack against subject-agnostic FS models named Additive Identity attack based on a Relighting function (AIR). AIR leverages reillumination and additive perturbations to mislead the identity extraction modules in subject-agnostic FS models. By using these two types of perturbations simultaneously, the attack space is extended such that stronger but more visually natural adversarial examples can be identified. To further enhance the visual quality while preserving the effectiveness of the attack, an adaptive translation-invariant operation and an illumination control scheme are designed for AIR. Unlike other methods, AIR does not require a surrogate FS model to achieve high transferability. In addition, a mathematical proof is given for the extension of the attack space. Extensive experiments using 1000 image pairs across various state-of-the-art subject-agnostic FS models, including GAN and diffusion-based FS models, show that AIR surpasses all existing attacks in terms of both attack success rate and image quality.

</details>


### [37] [Anatomically-conditioned Latent Diffusion Model for Data-Efficient Few-Shot Cross-Domain 3D Glioma MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25390)
*Salman Shaik,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ALDM用于少样本3D MRI合成，以缓解跨中心域偏移和标注数据稀缺问题，并提升弥漫性胶质瘤分类的数据增强效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥漫性胶质瘤的准确分类受限于不同医疗中心之间的成像域偏移，以及目标域大规模高质量标注数据不足；现有生成方法在极少样本3D体数据场景下难以同时保证结构一致性、病灶边界清晰度与跨模态一致性。

Method: ALDM采用两阶段框架：首先用3D变分自编码器在数据丰富的源域学习解剖先验；随后在潜空间中训练条件扩散模型，并通过ControlNet引入肿瘤掩膜条件，以生成目标域中结构一致的3D MRI体数据。

Result: 在仅有16张目标域图像的极端少样本设置下，ALDM优于GAN和混合式基线方法，取得FID 85.40，并在下游分类任务中达到AUC 0.987；定性结果显示其能保持清晰病理边界和跨模态一致性，训练过程中视觉质量逐步改善。

Conclusion: ALDM能够在低资源目标域中捕获关键诊断特征并生成高质量3D MRI数据，可作为临床医学影像分类任务中的有效数据增强工具，尤其适用于数据稀缺和存在域偏移的场景。

Abstract: Accurate classification of diffuse gliomas is often hindered by domain shifts across centers and a lack of large, annotated datasets. We propose the Anatomically-conditioned Latent Diffusion Model (ALDM), a novel framework for data-efficient, few-shot 3D volumetric MRI synthesis. ALDM utilizes a two-stage approach: a 3D variational autoencoder learns anatomical priors from a data-rich source domain, while a conditional latent diffusion model, guided by tumor masks via a ControlNet, generates structurally coherent volumes for a data-scarce target domain. Evaluated in an extreme few-shot setting with only 16 target images, ALDM outperformed GAN and hybrid baselines, achieving a superior Frechet Inception Distance (FID) of 85.40 and a downstream classification AUC of 0.987. Qualitative results confirm that the model preserves sharp pathology boundaries and cross-modal consistency, with visual fidelity improving progressively during training. By capturing essential diagnostic features, ALDM provides a robust tool for clinical data augmentation in low-resource settings. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/Analytics-Everywhere-Lab/anatomically-conditioned-LDM.

</details>


### [38] [Teach-to-Reason: Competition-Guided Reasoning with a Self-Improving Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25407)
*Xiao Han,Hao Liu,Zhimin Bao,Jile Jiao,Yue Wang,Hui Guo,Xiaofeng Mou,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Teach-to-Reason（T2R）框架，用比较式监督改进胸部 X 光视觉问答中的链式推理训练，在多个开放式 CXR VQA 基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的 CXR VQA 训练多依赖答案级奖励，信号过于粗粒度，难以提升 CoT 推理质量；当组级优势退化为零时，奖励监督会失效。

Method: T2R 包含自我提升的 Teacher 和竞争引导的 Reasoner。Teacher 通过自竞争迭代增强，Reasoner 则与逐步变强的 Teacher 生成参考进行比较优化。论文还设计了 case-wise 奖励机制：在原奖励划分有效时保留正/负样本分区；当原奖励退化时，利用竞争分数恢复监督信号。

Result: 在多个胸部 X 光开放式视觉问答基准上，T2R 持续超过强基线，表明该方法能更有效优化医学推理。

Conclusion: 受控且原则化地引入比较式监督，可以为 CXR VQA 的推理优化提供比单纯答案级奖励更稳定、更细粒度、也更有效的训练信号。

Abstract: Chest X-ray visual question answering (CXR VQA) requires models not only to predict correct answers, but also to produce reliable medical reasoning. However, existing reinforcement-learning-based training typically relies on answer-level rewards, which are often too coarse to improve chain-of-thought (CoT) quality and can become ineffective when group-level advantages collapse to zero. We propose \textbf{Teach-to-Reason (T2R)}, a framework that introduces comparison-based supervision into CoT optimization through a self-improving \emph{Teacher} and a competition-guided \emph{Reasoner}. As the Teacher is iteratively strengthened via self-competition, the Reasoner is optimized against progressively stronger Teacher-generated references. We further introduce a case-wise reward design that preserves the original reward-induced positive/negative partition when it is informative, and restores supervision from competition scores when the original reward signal degenerates. Experiments on multiple CXR open-ended VQA benchmarks show that T2R consistently outperforms strong baselines, indicating that comparison-based supervision, when integrated in a controlled and principled manner, provides a more effective training signal for reasoning optimization.

</details>


### [39] [Gastroendoscopy View Synthesis: A New Real Dataset and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25427)
*Masaki Minai,Yusuke Monno,Masatoshi Okutomi,Sho Suzuki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出首个面向新视角合成（NVS）的真实胃镜数据集 GastroNVS，并用若干 3D Gaussian Splatting 方法进行初步评估，以推动胃镜场景下 NVS 研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NeRF 和 3DGS 推动了 NVS 发展，但胃镜领域缺乏可用于评估 NVS 的真实数据集；这限制了扩展内镜视野、构建三维数字孪生、辅助内镜操作训练等应用的发展。

Method: 作者采集真实胃镜检查数据，构建包含胃镜图像、相机位姿和点云的 GastroNVS 数据集；随后在该数据集上评估多种 3DGS 方法，并分析其在胃镜 NVS 场景中的适用性与挑战。

Result: 论文给出了 GastroNVS 数据集，并通过实验展示现有 3DGS 方法在真实胃镜数据上的表现，同时指出该任务仍面临若干技术难点。

Conclusion: GastroNVS 填补了胃镜 NVS 真实数据集的空白，为后续算法评测和方法改进提供基础；但胃镜场景的成像特性和数据采集条件仍对现有 NVS 方法提出挑战。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis (NVS) is an active research topic in computer vision, owing to the success of neural radiance field (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) methods. While NVS opens the door to potential applications in gastroendoscopy, such as extending the field of view of endoscopic images and enabling digital twins for 3D archiving and endoscopist manipulation training, the dataset is insufficient to evaluate NVS for gastroendoscopy. In this paper, we present the first real gastroscopy dataset for NVS, namely the GastroNVS dataset, which contains a set of gastroscopic images, camera poses, and a point cloud for real gastroendoscopy inspection. To assess the suitability of the GastroNVS dataset, we evaluate several 3DGS methods and discuss the challenges for future development. The dataset is available on request from our project page.

</details>


### [40] [PRISM: Feed-Forward Single-Image 3D Reconstruction via Geometric Warp-Residual Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25430)
*Zhijie Zheng,Xinhao Xiang,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM 将单图到多视图/3D重建分解为几何前向投影先验与学习式残差校正，避免推理阶段扩散采样，在保持竞争性质量的同时显著降低耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图三维场景重建在虚拟现实、机器人和内容生成中很重要。现有基于相机控制视频扩散模型的方法质量较高，但依赖迭代扩散采样，推理速度慢、部署成本高。作者观察到，仅使用几何前向 warping 就能覆盖目标视图的大部分内容，模型只需修正较小残差。

Method: 提出 PRISM，一个前馈式框架，将多视图 latent 预测拆分为两部分：无参数的几何先验负责从输入图像直接前向投影到目标视角；可学习的编码器/残差模块负责补全和校正剩余误差。训练上采用两阶段策略：先进行 latent 监督蒸馏以获得几何泛化能力，再进行感知微调以优化外观质量。推理时不需要扩散采样。

Result: 在三个基准上的实验显示，PRISM 的重建质量可与扩散式方法竞争，同时推理时间大幅降低到每个场景约 36 秒。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将显式几何先验与轻量学习式残差校正结合，可以在单图三维重建中有效替代昂贵的扩散采样，实现质量与效率之间更实用的平衡。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D scenes from a single image is a fundamental challenge in computer vision, with broad applications in virtual reality, robotics, and content creation. Recent methods achieve outstanding performance by leveraging camera-controlled video diffusion models, but rely on iterative diffusion sampling, which greatly limits their practical deployment. We observe that geometric forward warping alone can cover the majority of a target view directly from the input image, with only a compact residual left for the encoder to correct. Motivated by this observation, we propose PRISM, a feed-forward framework that decomposes multi-view latent prediction into a parameter-free geometric prior and a learned residual correction, with no diffusion sampling required at inference. To enable generalization from purely synthetic training data, we devise a two-stage training strategy combining latents supervised distillation for geometric generalization and perceptual fine-tuning for appearance quality optimization. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that PRISM achieves competitive reconstruction quality compared with diffusion-based methods, while reducing inference time dramatically to only 36 seconds per scene.

</details>


### [41] [LinStereo: Linear-Complexity Global Attention for Multi-Scale Iterative Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25437)
*Yiran Wang,Oliver Turner,Viorela Ila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LinStereo 基于 Depth Anything V3，通过全局线性注意力、层级语义代价体和单目深度先验初始化，提升迭代式双目匹配在弱纹理/退化光照场景中的鲁棒性，尤其在水下场景取得显著泛化优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉基础模型的迭代式立体匹配方法未充分利用三类信息：多尺度骨干特征常被压缩为单层相关性，初始化阶段缺少几何先验，上下文传播范围局限于局部。这些不足在光度线索退化的场景中更明显，因此需要更强的全局信息传播与先验引导机制。

Method: 提出 LinStereo：以 Depth Anything V3 为基础，引入 Position-Aware Linear Attention（PALA）模块，以线性复杂度实现全局聚合，用可靠匹配区域的信息辅助退化区域估计，同时保持视差结构。配套设计包括 Hierarchical Semantic Cost Volumes（HSCV），利用 VFM 层级特征构建尺度对齐的相关性；以及 Depth Prior Initialization（DPI），将单目深度转换为经过尺度/度量校准的初始视差或深度估计。

Result: 在标准双目基准上达到 state-of-the-art 水平，并展现较强跨域泛化能力。水下数据上表现尤为突出：TartanAir-UW 的 AbsRel 降低 28%，真实水下数据集 SQUID 的 AbsRel 降低 26%，整体精度最佳。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，结合 VFM 多尺度语义特征、单目深度几何先验和线性复杂度的全局上下文传播，可以显著增强立体匹配在光度退化和跨域场景中的鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Existing Vision Foundation Model (VFM)-based iterative stereo pipelines under-exploit three information pathways: multi-scale backbone features are collapsed into single-level correlations, geometric priors remain untapped at initialization, and context propagates only locally. These gaps widen under degraded photometric cues, making underwater scenes a stringent generalization test. To address this, we propose LinStereo, built upon Depth Anything V3, whose core is a Position-Aware Linear Attention (PALA) module that replaces local recurrence with global aggregation at linear cost, propagating reliable estimates from well-matched regions into degraded areas while preserving disparity structure. PALA is made effective by two enabling components: Hierarchical Semantic Cost Volumes (HSCV), which supply scale-aligned correlations from the VFM feature hierarchy, and a Depth Prior Initialization (DPI) that converts monocular depth into a metrically calibrated warm start. LinStereo achieves state-of-the-art-level accuracy on standard benchmarks and strong cross-domain generalization, particularly on underwater scene where severe photometric degradation makes stereo matching particularly challenging, attaining the best overall accuracy with consistent gains 28% lower AbsRel on TartanAir-UW, 26% on SQUID, a real-world underwater dataset).

</details>


### [42] [C3-Bench: A Context-Aware Change Captioning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25445)
*Jae-Woo Kim,Hyeongbeom Kim,Ue-Hwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 C3-Bench，用于系统评估上下文感知变化描述任务，揭示现有变化描述模型和大型多模态模型在真实多样变化场景中的泛化缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Change Captioning 系统虽受到关注，但由于缺乏覆盖真实世界多样变化语境的综合评估框架，其真实性能与失败模式尚未被充分研究。

Method: 构建 C3-Bench：包含 4,996 个人工标注图像对，覆盖四大领域、51 类真实变化上下文；同时提出面向变化描述任务的 LLM-as-Judge 评估框架，从正确性、具体性、流畅性、相关性等细粒度维度评价，并引入可逆性指标衡量模型对对称变化的一致理解能力。

Result: 基于 C3-Bench 评测 32 个模型，包括传统变化描述模型、闭源大型多模态模型以及 2B–90B 开源大型多模态模型。结果显示，传统模型在偏离训练分布的变化场景中显著退化，先进大型多模态模型也存在系统性的领域相关和位置相关错误。

Conclusion: C3-Bench 将变化描述系统中以往隐藏的失效模式显式化、可量化，为构建更具泛化性、可靠性和可信度的变化描述模型提供了新的评测基础与研究方向。

Abstract: While Change Captioning systems have garnered substantial attention to respond to our evolving world, their true performance on diverse real-world change contexts remains largely unexplored due to the lack of comprehensive evaluation frameworks. To fill this gap, we propose C3-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating Context-aware Change Captioning. C3-Bench features: (1) 4,996 human-labeled image pairs of 51 real-world change contexts across four domains (e.g., natural scenes, remote sensing imagery, image editing, and anomalies), each with diverse, carefully curated scenarios derived from multiple change-centric communities; and (2) the first LLM-as-Judge evaluation framework in the change captioning task that measure fine-grained dimensions (e.g., correctness, specificity, fluency, and relevance), along with a novel reversibility metric exploring whether models understand changes with symmetric consistency. Based on C3-Bench, we benchmark 32 models -- including conventional change captioning models, proprietary Large Multimodal Models (LMMs), and 2B-90B open-source LMMs. We reveal a fundamental blind spot in the prevailing change captioning paradigm: Once the change context departs from training-style regimes, conventional models collapse, and even state-of-the-art LMMs such as GPT-5.2 exhibit systematic domain- and position-dependent errors that distort reliable change understanding. By making these hidden failure modes explicit and measurable, we delineate the next frontier for building generalizable and trustworthy change captioning systems. All codes and datasets are publicly available on the project page.

</details>


### [43] [EchoStyle: Unlocking High-Fidelity Video Stylization with Reverse Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25465)
*Huaqiu Li,Jiahao Wang,Sijia Cai,Hualian Sheng,Bing Deng,Jieping Ye,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoStyle 是一个面向任意长度视频的文本驱动视频风格化框架，通过视频到视频架构、大规模反向合成数据集 V-Style20k，以及 init-follow-mode 与滑动窗口推理，提升风格一致性、运动稳定性和长视频适配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频风格化方法多依赖参考图像作为风格先验，容易出现内容泄漏、训练数据不足、对长视频适配性差等问题，导致风格漂移、时序不一致和运动失真。因此需要一种更稳定、可扩展、可处理长视频且支持多样文本风格控制的方法。

Method: 提出 EchoStyle：首先构建视频到视频架构，将视频内容与文本描述的风格进行重新融合；其次设计自动反向合成流程，构建包含 2 万个高质量视频对的大规模风格化数据集 V-Style20k；最后引入 init-follow-mode 机制与滑动窗口推理策略，以支持任意长度视频的连续风格化。

Result: 大量实验表明，EchoStyle 能在多种艺术风格下生成高质量视频风格化结果，在风格表达、时序稳定性和运动保持方面表现优异，效果甚至可与领先的闭源方案相媲美。

Conclusion: 该工作通过架构、数据和推理机制的联合设计，显著缓解了视频风格化中的数据稀缺、风格漂移和长视频不稳定问题，为可扩展的文本驱动视频风格化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While image stylization has been studied extensively, video stylization remains a critical and largely unsolved challenge in the field of intelligent content creation. Existing methods, usually utilizing a reference image as the style prior, suffer from content leakage, data scarcity and limited adaptability to long videos, leading to suboptimal results with severe style drift and motion distortion. For these issues, we present EchoStyle, a scalable text-driven framework to achieve high-quality stylization of videos with arbitrary lengths. To start with, we construct a video-to-video architecture to appropriately re-fuse the video content and the text style. To address data scarcity, we pioneer an automatic reverse-synthesis pipeline to establish V-Style20k, a large-scale stylization dataset of 20k high-quality video pairs. To facilitate long video stylization, we devise an init-follow-mode mechanism along with a sliding-window inference strategy. Extensive experiments demonstrate EchoStyle's excellent performance across a wide range of artistic styles, even comparable to leading closed-source solutions.

</details>


### [44] [Causal-rCM: A Unified Teacher-Forcing and Self-Forcing Open Recipe for Autoregressive Diffusion Distillation in Streaming Video Generation and Interactive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25473)
*Kaiwen Zheng,Guande He,Min Zhao,Jintao Zhang,Huayu Chen,Jianfei Chen,Chen-Hsuan Lin,Ming-Yu Liu,Jun Zhu,Qianli Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将 rCM 扩展到自回归视频扩散，提出 Causal-rCM，将 teacher-forcing 一致性模型作为初始化、self-forcing DMD 作为在线精炼，在流式视频生成与交互式世界模型中取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散与因果扩散 Transformer 已成为实时流式视频生成和动作条件交互式世界模型的重要范式，但如何高效进行扩散蒸馏、减少采样步数并保持生成质量仍是关键问题。作者希望利用 rCM 中前向散度与反向散度互补的思想，构建适用于因果自回归场景的统一训练与蒸馏框架。

Method: 方法上，作者将 rCM 的一致性模型（CM，对应前向散度）与分布匹配蒸馏（DMD，对应反向散度）迁移到自回归视频扩散中：使用 teacher-forcing CM 进行离线因果训练与初始化，再用 self-forcing DMD 进行 on-policy 精炼；同时实现基于 teacher-forcing 的连续时间 CM（如 sCM/MeanFlow），并通过自定义 mask 的 FlashAttention-2 JVP kernel 支持高效训练，最终形成 Causal-rCM 算法与基础设施方案。

Result: 实验显示，teacher-forcing CM 是 self-forcing DMD 的有效初始化策略；连续时间 CM 相比离散时间 CM 收敛速度提升约 10 倍；Causal-rCM 在逐帧与分块流式视频生成设置中达到 SOTA。蒸馏后的 2-step causal Wan2.1-1.3B 模型仅用 1 或 2 个采样步即可获得 VBench-T2V 84.63 分，并可应用于 Cosmos 3 以支持动作条件交互式世界模型。

Conclusion: 本文表明，在自回归视频扩散中结合 teacher-forcing CM 与 self-forcing DMD 能有效兼顾稳定初始化与在线分布匹配精炼，从而显著提升低步数流式视频生成质量与训练效率。Causal-rCM 提供了一个可扩展的统一扩散蒸馏与因果训练方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion with causal diffusion transformers has emerged as a major paradigm for real-time streaming video generation and action-conditioned interactive world models. In this work, we extend rCM, an advanced diffusion distillation framework, to autoregressive video diffusion. The core philosophy of rCM lies in the complementarity between forward and reverse divergences, represented by consistency models (CMs) and distribution matching distillation (DMD), respectively, in diffusion distillation. This philosophy naturally carries over to the autoregressive setting, where teacher-forcing (TF) provides an offline, forward-divergence causal training paradigm, while self-forcing (SF) corresponds to an on-policy, reverse-divergence refinement.
  Our contributions are: (1) through extensive experiments, we show that teacher-forcing CM is currently the best complement to self-forcing DMD as an initialization strategy (2) we present the first implementation of teacher-forcing-based continuous-time CMs (e.g., sCM/MeanFlow) for autoregressive video diffusion, enabled by our custom-mask FlashAttention-2 JVP kernel, achieving 10$\times$ faster convergence compared to discrete-time CMs (dCMs) (3) we introduce Causal-rCM, a leading, unified, and scalable algorithm-infrastructure open recipe for diffusion distillation and causal training (4) we achieve state-of-the-art streaming video generation performance in both frame-wise and chunk-wise settings, using only synthetic data for training.
  Notably, our distilled 2-step causal Wan2.1-1.3B model achieves a VBench-T2V score of 84.63 with only 1 or 2 sampling steps. We further apply Causal-rCM to Cosmos 3, an advanced omnimodal world foundation model for physical AI with action-conditioned generation capability, enabling an interactive world model.

</details>


### [45] [TACO: Towards Task-Consistent Open-Vocabulary Adaptation in Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25478)
*Minghao Zhu,Xiao Lin,Mengxian Hu,Xun Zhou,Liuyi Wang,Xiaoyan Qi,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TACO旨在改进CLIP在开放词汇视频识别中的微调适配，通过保持训练分布之外的表示对齐结构，缓解过度专门化导致的泛化下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过正则化或约束平衡视频任务专门化与CLIP预训练泛化能力，但忽视了微调后表示在训练分布之外发生偏移的问题。作者认为该问题源于微调目标只覆盖已知训练分布，而评估却发生在未见分布上，导致目标不一致。

Method: 提出TACO框架：核心是Relative Structure Distillation，用于约束表示空间的相对几何结构，抑制训练过程中有害的对齐偏移；同时引入轻量级specialization projection，将表示空间与优化空间解耦，使模型能进行任务特定适配，而不直接过度改变测试时使用的表示。

Result: TACO在跨数据集和base-to-novel开放词汇视频识别设置下，在多个基准上取得了当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 保持OOD相关的表示对齐对于CLIP视频适配至关重要；TACO通过相对结构蒸馏和优化/表示空间解耦，有效提升了专门化能力与泛化能力之间的平衡。

Abstract: Adapting CLIP for open-vocabulary video recognition necessitates a delicate balance between newly acquired video knowledge and the pretrained generalization. While existing studies pursue this generalization-specialization trade-off with additional regularizations or constraints, we argue that they overlook the deviation of representations beyond the fine-tuning data distribution, resulting in suboptimal adaptation effects. We believe such deviation is inherited from the inconsistency between the fine-tuning and evaluation objectives, where model optimization is restricted to the known training distribution but evaluated on unseen ones. In this paper, we introduce \emph{TACO}, a simple yet effective framework to mitigate the potential negative effects induced by this inconsistency. Our key insight is that adaptation should preserve OOD-relevant alignment beyond the training distribution. To this end, we propose \emph{Relative Structure Distillation}, which regularizes the relative geometry of the representation space and suppresses harmful alignment shift during training. We further decouple the representation space from the optimization space with a lightweight specialization projection, allowing task-specific adaptation without directly overspecializing the representations used at test time. \emph{TACO} establishes state-of-the-art performance on diverse benchmarks under cross-dataset and base-to-novel settings. Code will be released at https://github.com/ZMHH-H/TACO.

</details>


### [46] [Cross-View Variance Correlation in Path-Traced Stereo:A Hidden Shortcut in Synthetic Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25483)
*Po-Ting Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现路径追踪合成立体数据中，左右相机的蒙特卡洛噪声虽相互独立，但经真值视差对齐后的逐像素方差场高度相关，并可能被模型利用为非真实世界的匹配捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视差估计大量依赖路径追踪生成的合成立体数据。作者关注这类数据中是否存在未被认识的统计结构，尤其是可能导致仿真到真实迁移偏差的训练捷径。

Method: 使用 Mitsuba 3 渲染 20 个场景，分析左右视图 MC 方差场在真值视差 warp 对齐后的 Pearson 相关性；考察不同 SPP、不同材质区域的相关性，并通过基于渲染积分函数的视图无关/视图相关分解解释现象；进一步采用 residual-shuffle 干预破坏跨视图方差对齐但保持干净图像不变，以验证其是否可作为匹配线索。

Result: 在 SPP=512 时，20 个场景的对齐方差场相关性达到 ρ=0.754±0.016；代表性场景中该相关性在 16 倍 SPP 范围内几乎不变，为 ρ=0.778±0.001。Lambertian 区域相关性最强（约 0.78），glass 区域显著较弱（约 0.30）。residual-shuffle 干预使非玻璃区域 GT cost margin 下降 33%，玻璃区域基于方差的 winner-take-all 准确率下降 4.3 倍。

Conclusion: MC 渲染生成的合成立体数据包含一种独特的、经视差对齐后高度相关的方差场结构，该结构可作为视差匹配线索，但真实数据中不存在或不等价，因此可能构成 sim-to-real shortcut。其对实际训练网络性能与泛化的影响仍需进一步量化。

Abstract: Path-traced synthetic stereo data underlie a large fraction of modern disparity-estimation training pipelines. We report a previously unrecognised property of such data: while the Monte Carlo (MC) noise streams of the two cameras are statistically independent, the underlying \emph{variance fields} -- deterministic per-pixel functions of the rendering integrand -- are highly correlated once aligned by the ground-truth disparity warp. Across 20 scenes rendered with Mitsuba~3, the warped Pearson correlation reaches $ρ{=}0.754{\pm}0.016$ across 20 scenes at $\mathrm{SPP}{=}512$, and on a representative scene remains essentially invariant ($ρ{=}0.778{\pm}0.001$) over a $16\times$ range of samples per pixel. The effect is strongest in Lambertian regions ($ρ{\approx}0.78$) and substantially weaker in glass ($ρ{\approx}0.30$), as predicted by an integrand decomposition into view-independent and view-dependent components. A residual-shuffle intervention that breaks the cross-view alignment while preserving the clean image degrades the GT cost margin by $33\%$ on non-glass and the variance-based winner-take-all accuracy on glass by $4.3\times$, confirming the structure functions as a matching cue. This signal is unique to MC-rendered data and constitutes a candidate sim-to-real shortcut whose impact on trained networks remains to be quantified.

</details>


### [47] [HG-Bench: A Benchmark for Multi-Page Handwritten Answer-Region Grounding in Automated Homework Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25491)
*Chuangxin Zhao,Boyan Shi,Yanling Wang,Yijian LU,Canran Xiao,Jiali Chen,Jun Xia,Yan Wang,Ji Qi,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 HG-Bench，用于评估模型在多页手写作业中定位完整答案区域及其有序步骤子区域的能力；现有零样本多模态模型表现不足，微调模型显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动作业评测不仅需要识别学生答案文本，还需要在噪声较多、多页手写作业图像中准确定位每个答案及其中间推理步骤。现有评测缺少面向页面感知、层级化答案区域定位的设置。

Method: 构建 HG-Bench：从 1,489,278 张图像来源池中人工标注 500 份 K-12 作业样本，提供题目级与步骤级边界框，并加入层级包含约束。设计页面感知评测协议，分别衡量完整答案定位 FA 与步骤级分解 FSm。

Result: 前沿闭源 API 与竞争性开源 VLM 在零样本设置下，FA 最高不超过 55.22%，FSm 最高不超过 48.22%；使用约 1 万个领域内样本微调的 GLM-4.6V 9B 参考模型达到 74.97/72.26。

Conclusion: 步骤级手写推理区域定位仍是当前多模态模型的明显能力短板。HG-Bench 提供了可复现的基准、评测协议和微调参考模型，有助于推动自动作业评测中的空间结构理解研究。

Abstract: Automated homework assessment depends not only on recognizing student answers, but also on accurately locating where each answer and each intermediate reasoning step appears in noisy, multi-page handwritten work. This paper addresses the missing evaluation setting of page-aware, two-level answer-region grounding: given a sequence of homework page images, a model must localize complete answer regions and their ordered step-level subregions. We introduce HG-Bench, a benchmark of 500 human-annotated K-12 homework samples curated from a 1,489,278-image source pool, with question-level and step-level boxes linked by a hierarchical containment constraint. HG-Bench is paired with a page-aware evaluation protocol that separately measures complete-answer localization (FA) and step-level decomposition (FSm), revealing whether models truly ground the spatial structure of student reasoning rather than merely parse visible text. Across frontier closed-source APIs and competitive open-weight VLMs, no zero-shot system exceeds 55.22% on FA or 48.22% on FSm, while a GLM-4.6V 9B reference model fine-tuned on ~10k in-domain examples reaches 74.97/72.26. These results identify step-level handwritten grounding as a concrete capability gap and provide a reproducible benchmark, evaluation protocol, and trained reference point for future work on automated homework assessment.

</details>


### [48] [C2RM-Seg: Causal Counterfactual Reasoning with Structural-Semantic Priors for Weakly Supervised Histopathological Tissue Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25508)
*Hualong Zhang,Siyang Feng,Zihan Huan,Yi Qian,Zhenbing Liu,Rushi Lan,Xipeng Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 C²RM-Seg，用因果反事实推理改进弱监督组织病理分割中的 CAM 伪标签，并结合结构-语义双路径网络与不确定性门控损失，提升分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织病理组织分割对计算机辅助诊断很重要，但弱监督方法依赖 CAM 生成伪标签，容易受染色外观等非因果线索干扰，导致定位偏差、结构一致性差和边界不准确。

Method: 方法包含两阶段：分类阶段引入 Causal Counterfactual Reasoning Module，将特征分解为潜在因素，并通过学习到的因果结构矩阵进行反事实干预，以抑制混杂上下文并生成更符合形态学的 CAM；分割阶段采用 Dual-Path Structural-Semantic Architecture，融合 ResNeSt 的细粒度结构特征与冻结 DINOV3 的全局语义先验，并通过跨路径门控机制根据局部结构自适应注入语义信息；同时提出 Uncertainty-Gated Margin loss，依据预测不确定性动态平衡间隔约束与置信学习。

Result: 在两个公开组织病理组织数据集上的实验表明，C²RM-Seg 达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将因果伪标签精炼、结构感知语义增强和不确定性鲁棒训练结合起来，可以有效缓解弱监督组织病理分割中的伪标签噪声与伪相关问题，并提升结构一致性与分割精度。

Abstract: Histopathological tissue segmentation is essential for computer-aided diagnosis, yet weakly supervised methods often suffer from noisy pseudo-labels generated by Class Activation Mapping (CAM). Existing CAM approaches tend to focus on staining-driven appearance cues rather than true causal tissue morphology, resulting in spurious localization and poor structural consistency. To address this issue, we propose C$^2$RM-Seg, a two-stage framework that integrates causal pseudo-label refinement with structure-aware semantic enhancement. For classification, we introduce a Causal Counterfactual Reasoning Module (C$^2$RM) that decomposes features into latent factors and performs counterfactual intervention via a learned causal structure matrix, suppressing confounding context and producing morphology-aligned CAMs. For segmentation, we design a Dual-Path Structural-Semantic Architecture that combines fine-grained structural features from ResNeSt with global semantic priors from a frozen DINOV3 foundation model. A cross-path gating mechanism adaptively regulates semantic injection using local structural cues to preserve boundary fidelity. To further mitigate residual pseudo-label noise, we propose an Uncertainty-Gated Margin (UGM) loss, which dynamically balances margin enforcement and confidence learning based on prediction uncertainty. Extensive experiments on two public histopathological tissue datasets show that C$^2$RM-Seg achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [49] [PatchINR: Patch-Based Implicit Neural Representations for Efficient and Scalable Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25534)
*Jiachen Ren,Wenyong Zhou,Taiqiang Wu,Yuxin Cheng,Xincheng Feng,Zhengwu Liu,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于非重叠 patch 的 INR 推理方法，并在 FPGA 上实现硬件加速，在保持接近像素级重建质量的同时显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统隐式神经表示（INR）通常按像素逐点查询推理，在高分辨率场景中推理次数随像素数量快速增长，带来较高计算成本和延迟，因此需要更高效的推理粒度与硬件实现方案。

Method: 将非重叠图像 patch 作为基本处理单元，使模型一次前向传播预测一个完整像素块，而非单个像素；同时在 FPGA 平台上设计面向 INR 的硬件加速架构，包含可配置流水线并支持双精度计算。

Result: 在 2×2 patch 设置下，patch-based INR 达到 34.97 dB PSNR，重建质量接近像素级 INR；推理延迟降低 75%，参数开销仅增加 0.6%。

Conclusion: 基于 patch 的 INR 能有效减少查询次数和推理延迟，并可通过 FPGA 加速进一步提升部署效率，是面向高分辨率连续信号建模的实用优化方向。

Abstract: Implicit Neural Representation (INR) provides an effective approach for continuous signal modeling, but classical per-pixel inference results in quadratic growth in inference count, leading to dramatically increased computational costs in high-resolution application scenarios. To address this issue, we propose a patch-based approach that treats non-overlapping patches as fundamental processing units and predicts entire pixel patches in a single forward pass, significantly reducing the number of inference queries required. To validate the effectiveness of our approach, we propose a hardware acceleration architecture on the Field Programmable Gate Array (FPGA) platform for the INR model, which features a configurable pipeline and supports dual-precision computation. Our patch-based INR achieves comparable reconstruction quality to pixel-level INR (34.97 dB PSNR with 2 x 2 patches) while reducing inference latency by 75% with only 0.6% parameter overhead.

</details>


### [50] [Spatio-Temporal Mixture-of-Modality-Experts Diffusion for Quantitative DCE-MRI Synthesis from Incomplete MR Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25535)
*Junhyeok Lee,Kyu Sung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ST-MoME：一种条件扩散框架，可在多模态 MRI 输入缺失或组合变化时，从可用模态合成 3D DCE-MRI 定量参数图，并在多种可用性场景下取得较优重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DCE-MRI 定量图对肿瘤评估重要，但造影剂风险、扫描协议差异等因素导致其常不可用。现有预测方法通常依赖固定且完整的输入模态，难以应对真实临床中模态缺失和输入组合不确定的问题。

Method: ST-MoME 使用条件扩散模型直接在图像空间生成 3D DCE 参数图；通过模态专属专家提取特征，并由时空门控网络生成体素级、扩散时间步相关的融合权重，形成条件张量指导去噪；采用 3D patch 训练和基于 Swin 的骨干网络以保持空间结构与定量精度。

Result: 在 386 例临床脑肿瘤队列上，作者评估了 16 种模态可用性场景。ST-MoME 在三个 DCE 参数的平均 NMSE 上最低，在 vp 和 ve 上表现领先，在 Ktrans 上具有竞争力，并在临床关键的肿瘤区域取得最低重建误差。门控动态的事后分析显示出“早期偏结构信息、后期偏生理信息”的融合模式。

Conclusion: ST-MoME 能够鲁棒地利用不同可用模态合成 DCE-MRI 定量图，适合更贴近临床实际的缺失模态环境；其时空门控机制不仅提升了性能，也提供了一定的可解释性。

Abstract: Quantitative maps from dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI (DCE-MRI) are essential for tumor assessment but are often unavailable due to contrast-agent risks and protocol variability. Prior methods predict these maps from other MRI modalities, yet most assume fixed, fully observed inputs and fail under realistic missingness. We present Spatio-Temporal Mixture-of-Modality-Experts (ST-MoME), a conditional diffusion framework that synthesizes 3D DCE parameter maps from diverse subsets of multimodal MRI. ST-MoME fuses modality-specific expert features through a spatio-temporal gating network that produces voxel-wise, timestep-dependent weights, forming a conditioning tensor that guides denoising. To preserve quantitative fidelity, ST-MoME performs diffusion directly in image space with 3D patch-based training and a Swin-based backbone. On a clinical brain-tumor cohort of 386 patients, we evaluate ST-MoME across 16 controlled modality-availability scenarios. It achieves the lowest mean Normalized Mean Square Error (NMSE) aggregated across all three DCE parameters, with leading performance on $v_p$ and $v_e$, competitive results on $K^{\mathrm{trans}}$, and the lowest reconstruction error within the clinically critical tumor region. A post-hoc analysis of the learned gating dynamics shows a structural-early, physiological-late fusion schedule consistent with clinical intuition.

</details>


### [51] [SAC$^2$-Net: Semantic Anchoring and Complementary-Consensus Fusion for Multimodal Micro-Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25542)
*Xuepeng Zheng,Tong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SAC²-Net，用于多模态微表情识别，通过语义锚定对齐光流与运动放大特征，并进行可靠性感知融合，以缓解模态异质性和局部可靠性差异问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微表情识别面临面部运动细微、数据有限以及 AU 与情绪类别关系模糊等挑战。光流与运动放大能从不同角度刻画细微动态，但二者常出现非对称失效：一个模态可能噪声大或失真，另一个模态仍保留有判别力的 AU 相关信息。因此需要一种能处理跨模态异质性并感知空间可靠性的融合方法。

Method: 方法包括两个核心模块：1）Semantic Anchoring Soft Alignment（SASA）：将激活的 AU 转换为文本提示，作为稳定语义锚点，对齐运动放大与光流表示；同时使用层级 AU 感知软标签，保留 AU 重叠或解剖相关样本之间的语义邻近性，避免硬对比学习过于刚性。2）Complementary-Consensus Fusion（CCF）：在对齐后的表示上，先通过互补信息交换修复局部不可靠证据，再通过一致性细化约束两个模态形成共享空间关注区域，实现可靠性感知融合。

Result: 在五个微表情识别基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，覆盖粗粒度、细粒度、大规模和跨数据集评估场景。结果显示 SAC²-Net 达到当前最优或具有高度竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，光流与运动放大在微表情识别中具有显著互补性；通过语义锚定降低跨模态差异，并结合互补-一致性融合处理局部可靠性变化，可以有效提升多模态微表情识别性能。

Abstract: Micro-expression recognition (MER) is challenging due to subtle facial movements, limited data, and the ambiguous relationship between Action Units (AUs) and emotion categories. Optical flow and motion magnification have been widely used to describe subtle facial dynamics from different perspectives: the former captures local motion displacement, while the latter amplifies weak appearance changes. In this work, we observe that these two modalities often exhibit asymmetric failure patterns: one modality may become noisy, distorted, or uninformative, while the other still preserves discriminative AU-related evidence. This phenomenon reveals their complementarity, but also raises two key challenges for fusion: cross-modal heterogeneity and spatially varying modality reliability. Motivated by this observation, we propose SAC$^2$-Net, a Semantic Anchoring and Complementary-Consensus Network for multimodal MER, which first aligns visual modalities with semantic anchors and then performs reliability-aware fusion. To reduce cross-modal heterogeneity before fusion, we introduce Semantic Anchoring Soft Alignment (SASA), which converts activated AUs into textual prompts and uses them as stable semantic anchors to align motion-magnified and optical-flow representations. Unlike hard contrastive learning, SASA constructs hierarchical AU-aware soft labels to preserve semantic proximity among samples with overlapping or anatomically related AU patterns. Based on the aligned representations, Complementary-Consensus Fusion (CCF) first repairs unreliable local evidence through complementary exchange and then enforces a shared spatial focus through consensus refinement. Extensive experiments on five MER benchmarks show that SAC$^2$-Net achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive performance across coarse-grained, fine-grained, large-scale, and cross-dataset evaluation settings.

</details>


### [52] [TensorLDM: A Component-Wise Latent Diffusion Model for Volumetric DTI Reconstruction from Sparse DWIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25545)
*Junhyeok Lee,Kyu Sung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TensorLDM通过组件级潜在扩散建模，从极少量DWI中重建更准确且物理有效的扩散张量，在稀疏DTI采集中提升张量重建与下游纤维追踪表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床DTI需要加速采集，因此必须从稀疏DWI重建扩散张量；但现有深度学习方法常产生解剖不一致或物理上不合理的张量，影响可靠性和下游分析。

Method: 提出TensorLDM：将六个张量分量按对角与非对角元素分别输入两个组特异编码器，并共享DWI条件以保持解剖一致性；采用Anatomy-Conditioned Autoencoder使潜变量聚焦张量属性；在自编码器细化与扩散微调中引入共享Cross-Component Attention建模分量间依赖；使用Mixture-of-Experts DWI conditioner实现分量自适应条件控制。

Result: 在HCP数据集单壳四体积稀疏采集设置下，TensorLDM取得最佳下游纤维追踪精度，张量物理有效性接近真实值，SPD违规率为1.54%而真实值为1.40%；体素级重建精度达到最佳或相当水平，Log-Euclidean Metric测得的测地张量误差也支持其优势。

Conclusion: 组件级潜在扩散结合解剖条件约束、跨分量注意力和MoE条件器，可更可靠地从稀疏DWI重建DTI张量，兼顾解剖一致性、物理有效性和下游可用性。

Abstract: Reconstructing diffusion tensors from sparse DWIs is critical for accelerating Diffusion Tensor Imaging (DTI) in clinical settings, yet current deep learning approaches frequently yield anatomically inconsistent or physically implausible tensors. We introduce TensorLDM, a component-wise latent diffusion model that processes the six tensor components through two group-specific encoders (for diagonal and off-diagonal elements) while maintaining anatomical consistency via shared DWI conditioning. TensorLDM uses an Anatomy-Conditioned Autoencoder that encourages the latent to focus on tensor properties rather than re-encoding structural information. A shared Cross-Component Attention (CCA) mechanism, applied in both autoencoder refinement and diffusion fine-tuning, models inter-component dependencies, while a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) DWI conditioner provides component-adaptive conditioning. On the Human Connectome Project (HCP) dataset under a single-shell, four-volume sparse acquisition, TensorLDM produces the most accurate downstream tractography and tensors with near-ground-truth physical validity (SPD-violation rate 1.54% vs. 1.40%), with the best or comparable voxel-wise reconstruction accuracy. Geodesic tensor error measured by the Log-Euclidean Metric (LEM) corroborates these gains.

</details>


### [53] [Disease-Centric Vision-Language Pretraining with Hybrid Visual Encoding for 3D Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25546)
*Bowen Shi,Weiwei Cao,Ruifeng Yuan,Wanxing Chang,Wenrui Dai,Hongkai Xiong,Ling Zhang,Jianpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向3D CT的医学视觉-语言预训练框架，通过高效3D视觉编码、疾病级语义对齐和诊断感知提示，提高零样本诊断与迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VLP方法在3D CT场景中受限于视觉骨干效率不足、图文语义对齐粒度较粗，难以充分利用放射报告中的细粒度疾病监督，尤其难以区分同一解剖区域内的不同疾病。

Method: 方法包含三部分：CNN-ViT混合编码器，用3D CNN替代ViT patch embedding以捕获局部解剖细节并保留全局注意力；基于可学习query token的疾病级对比学习，从完整报告中动态抽取疾病特异语义并与视觉特征对齐；诊断感知prompt策略，利用真实临床短语与聚合疾病原型缩小预训练和推理差距。

Result: 模型在CT-RATE上达到84.4% AUC（提升5.1%），在Rad-ChestCT上达到75.4% AUC（提升5.4%），在60疾病困难基准上提升更显著（+9.8% AUC），并在放射报告生成任务中表现出较强迁移性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结构设计和细粒度语义对齐提升了3D CT医学VLP的诊断可靠性、泛化性和临床实用潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language pre-training (VLP) holds great promise for general-purpose medical AI by leveraging radiology reports as rich textual supervision, yet existing methods struggle with 3D CT imaging due to inefficient visual backbones and coarse semantic alignment. To address these issues, we propose a tailored VLP framework featuring three key components: (1) a CNN-ViT hybrid encoder that replaces ViT's patch embedding with a 3D CNN backbone to efficiently capture local anatomical details while preserving global attention and compatibility with pre-trained cross-modal priors; (2) a disease-level contrastive learning mechanism using learnable query tokens to dynamically extract disease-specific semantics from full reports and align them with corresponding visual features, thereby disentangling distinct diseases within the same anatomical region; and (3) a diagnosis-aware prompt strategy that employs real clinical phrases and aggregated disease prototypes to bridge the pre-training-inference gap and enhance zero-shot diagnostic reliability. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on CT-RATE (84.4% AUC, +5.1%) and Rad-ChestCT (75.4% AUC, +5.4%), with even larger gains (+9.8% AUC) on a challenging 60-disease benchmark, and demonstrates strong transferability to radiology report generation, underscoring the generality and clinical utility of our approach.

</details>


### [54] [Efficient Cross-Scale Invertible Hiding Network with Spatial-Frequency Collaboration and Non-Invertible Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25547)
*Junxue Yang,Xin Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CrosInv，一种用于图像隐藏的跨尺度可逆网络，通过空间-频率协同特征与非可逆机制提升隐藏与恢复图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可逆神经网络的图像隐藏方法受网络结构限制，通常只进行单尺度、单域特征提取，且非线性表达能力不足，导致隐写图像和恢复图像质量不理想。

Method: 方法构建高效的跨尺度可逆隐藏网络 CrosInv。其核心是跨尺度可逆模块，将输入双射映射到跨尺度表征；模块结合 pixel shuffle、Haar 小波变换及其逆操作实现尺度变换，并融合空间域与频率域信息；同时引入非可逆交叉密集模块以增强网络非线性表示能力。

Result: 综合实验验证了 CrosInv 的有效性和优越性，表明其相较已有方法能够获得更好的图像隐藏与恢复性能。

Conclusion: CrosInv 通过跨尺度建模、空间-频率协同以及非可逆增强机制，有效缓解了传统 INN 图像隐藏方法的结构性缺陷，是一种更具表达能力和性能优势的图像隐藏框架。

Abstract: Image hiding aims to conceal image-level messages within cover images at the same resolution. Invertible neural networks (INN)-based image hiding has emerged as an important branch. It treats concealing and revealing as a pair of inverse problems on image domain transformation and uses INN's forward and backward processes to address them. Due to architectural constraints, existing INN-based methods suffer from single-scale and single-domain feature extraction and limited nonlinear representation capability, resulting in inferior image quality. To mitigate these limitations, we propose an efficient cross-scale invertible hiding network with the spatial-frequency collaboration and the non-invertible mechanism, termed CrosInv. CrosInv exploits cross-scale and spatial-frequency collaborative features while enhancing nonlinear representation. Specifically, we introduce a cross-scale invertible module that bijectively maps inputs to cross-scale representations. To effectively integrate spatial and frequency information, the cross-scale invertible module employs pixel shuffle, Haar wavelet transformation, and their inverse operations for scale transformation. Furthermore, a non-invertible cross dense module is integrated to enhance the nonlinearity. Comprehensive experiments verify the effectiveness and superiority of the proposed CrosInv.

</details>


### [55] [Concept Removal for Frontier Image Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25548)
*Aditya Kumar,Pierre Joly,Adam Dziedzic,Franziska Boenisch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向前沿图像生成模型的概念移除方法，通过替换模型内部瓶颈层为可结构化激活特征的 transcoder，实现对特定视觉概念信号的选择性关闭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模、未充分筛选的互联网训练数据会使图像生成模型学习到不希望出现的视觉概念；现有方法难以在高效移除这些概念的同时保持图像质量，并且可能在白盒访问或对抗提示下失效。

Method: 将现代扩散模型和图像自回归模型中的内部瓶颈层原位替换为一个 transcoder。该 transcoder 训练目标是复现原层功能，同时将内部表示组织为可区分的激活特征，从而允许禁用与目标概念相关的特征信号，而保留其他模型行为。

Result: 实验表明，该方法可用于 SD3.5、Flux、Infinity 等模型，在概念移除效果上达到先进水平，同时保持视觉生成质量，对对抗提示具有鲁棒性，并支持多种概念的顺序移除。

Conclusion: 该方法通过修改模型主干而非外接过滤模块，实现了持久、鲁棒且质量损失较小的概念移除，是前沿图像生成模型中较实用的概念控制方案。

Abstract: Image generative models are trained on massive, largely uncurated internet-scale datasets that contain undesirable visual concepts. Efficiently removing such concepts from the model generations without degrading the quality of output images remains challenging. We introduce a novel concept removal method for frontier diffusion and image autoregressive models, such as SD3.5, Flux, and Infinity. Our intervention replaces the internal bottleneck layer present in all these modern models with a transcoder that is trained to replicate the original layer while structuring it into distinct activation features. This in-place substitution creates an integrated filter through which concept-specific signals can be selectively disabled while preserving the rest of the model's behavior. Since the intervention modifies the model backbone rather than attaching an external component, it remains persistent under white-box access. Empirically, the approach achieves state-of-the-art concept removal performance across modern diffusion and autoregressive models, maintains visual generation quality, provides robustness against adversarial prompts, and supports sequential removal of diverse concepts. This positions our method as a practical approach for concept removal in frontier image generative models.

</details>


### [56] [H-Adapter: Pose-Robust Hairstyle Transfer via Attention-Derived, Source-Aligned Hair Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25578)
*Seulgi Jeong,Yunseong Cho,Sanghun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: H-Adapter 面向大姿态差异下的发型迁移，通过区域特异性损失诱导空间解耦的跨注意力，并生成源图对齐的头发编辑掩码来指导扩散修复，从而提升发型一致性与姿态鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有发型迁移方法在源图与参考图头部姿态差异较大时容易失败，难以同时保持参考发型细节、源图非头发区域身份/外观一致性以及生成图像质量。虚拟试戴等应用需要更稳健、可控的发型编辑能力。

Method: 提出 H-Adapter：训练时使用区域特异性损失，将头发区域目标与非头发区域目标解耦；该训练机制促使跨注意力在空间上解耦；进一步从解耦注意力中推导源图对齐的头发编辑掩码，并用于指导扩散模型的图像修复式发型迁移。此外支持文本生成、提示词控制发色以及与身份保持型 IP-Adapter 变体结合。

Result: 在姿态无关与姿态差异子集上的实验显示，H-Adapter 在姿态差异场景下取得最佳 FID、FID_CLIP 和 CLIP-I，同时保持有竞争力的非头发区域保真度；定性结果表明其能更好恢复参考发型的细粒度细节。VLM-as-a-judge 评估也显示其在发型忠实度、非头发保持和伪影质量方面有一致提升。

Conclusion: H-Adapter 有效缓解了大姿态差异下发型迁移的核心困难，在发型细节迁移、非编辑区域保护和生成质量之间取得较好平衡，并具备较强扩展性和实用价值。

Abstract: Hairstyle transfer has practical applications such as virtual try-on, yet remains challenging when the source and reference exhibit large head-pose discrepancies. We propose H-Adapter, which improves pose robustness by training with a region-specific loss that disentangles hair and non-hair objectives and thereby induces spatially disentangled cross-attention, from which a source-aligned hair edit mask is derived to guide diffusion-based inpainting. Experiments on pose-agnostic and pose-different subsets demonstrate strong quantitative results, including the best FID, $\mathrm{FID}_{\mathrm{CLIP}}$, and CLIP-I under pose differences, while maintaining competitive non-hair preservation and improving qualitative fidelity to fine-grained reference hairstyle details. Beyond source-conditioned transfer, H-Adapter supports practical extensions including text-to-image generation, auxiliary prompt-based hair color control, and compatibility with an identity-preserving IP-Adapter variant. We also introduce a VLM-as-a-judge protocol and observe consistent gains in hairstyle faithfulness, non-hair preservation, and artifact quality.

</details>


### [57] [FeVOS: Foresight Expression Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25585)
*Kehan Lan,Kaining Ying,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出前瞻表达视频目标分割任务，要求根据已观察视频帧推理未来事件并在当前帧中分割相关对象；同时发布 FeVOS 数据集并训练 FeVOS-R1 模型，取得领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代表达视频目标分割主要依据已观察帧中的事件、动作或外观进行对象定位，缺乏对需要预判未来事件的时空推理场景的评测，限制了其在具身/第一视角等决策前感知任务中的应用。

Method: 作者定义 Foresight Expression Video Object Segmentation 任务：输入关于未来事件的表达，输出已观察帧中对应对象的掩码。构建 FeVOS 数据集，包含 968 个视频片段、14,525 条前瞻表达和 2,904 条链式思维标注；并提出基于多模态大语言模型的 FeVOS-R1，通过监督微调与强化学习两阶段训练。

Result: FeVOS-R1 在 FeVOS 数据集上达到当前最优表现，并且在已有 RVOS 基准上表现出较强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作将视频目标分割从当前/过去可见语义扩展到面向未来事件的预测性时空推理，为可解释视频感知和决策前理解提供了新任务、数据集和基线模型。

Abstract: Existing Referring Video Object Segmentation tasks focus on referring expressions describing events, actions or appearances of relevant objects within the observed frames, lacking evaluation in scenarios that require pre-decisive spatio-temporal reasoning, thereby limiting their applicability. To address this, we propose Foresight Expression Video Object Segmentation, a task that queries future events in upcoming video segments and requires masks of the objects in the observed frames as visual answers. For example, in ego-centric scenes, the question "What tool will be used?" demands reasoning over spatio-temporal cues to predict the masks of the next tool to be used, which helps with the understanding of future actions and decisions. To support this task, we introduce FeVOS, a dataset with 968 video clips, 14,525 foresight expressions, and 2,904 chain-of-thought annotations to provide explicit and interpretable reasoning steps. We further develop FeVOS-R1, an MLLM-based model trained on our dataset via a two-stage pipeline of supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. FeVOS-R1 not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on FeVOS, but also demonstrates strong generalization to existing RVOS benchmarks. We hope this work can inspire more research on predictive reasoning in video perception.

</details>


### [58] [Expresso-AI: Explainable Video-Based Deep Learning Models for Depression Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25606)
*Felipe Moreno,Sharifa Alghowinem,Hae Won Park,Cynthia Breazeal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要提出一个面向视频人脸的抑郁严重程度自动诊断可解释框架，通过分析深度模型的显著性图，解释模型关注的面部区域与时间表情语义，并提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症早期诊断需要客观、可解释且便于医护人员理解的自动化指标；现有方法虽有进展，但通常缺乏情感特异性与可解释性，尤其是基于视频的深度模型对时间活动的解释不足。

Method: 作者将预训练于动作识别数据集的深度卷积神经网络在AVEC抑郁数据集的人脸视频上进行微调，用于抑郁严重程度预测；同时基于显著性图分析模型决策，结合面部区域定位与时间维度上的表情语义生成视觉和量化解释。

Result: 该框架不仅能够为模型决策提供视觉化和定量化解释，还在视觉抑郁诊断任务上优于既有单人脸基准方法，获得更好的预测性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，面向人脸视频的可解释深度学习框架可以同时提升抑郁严重程度预测能力，并为临床相关假设生成与模型可信分析提供支持。

Abstract: Given the widespread prevalence of depression and its consequential impact on individuals and society, it is crucial to obtain objective measures for early diagnosis and intervention. As a multidisciplinary topic, these objective measures should be interpretable and accessible to health care professionals, ensuring effective collaboration and treatment planning in the realm of mental health care. Even though current automated depression diagnosis approaches improved over the last decade, a critical gap exists as they often lack affect-specificity and interpretability, limiting their practical application and potential impact on mental health care. In particular, interpretability from temporal activities from videos when deep models are used is not fully explored. In this study, we present a novel framework for analyzing Deep Neural Networks' decisions when trained on facial videos, specifically focusing on automatic depression severity diagnosis. By fine-tuning Deep Convolutional Neural Networks (DCNN) pre-trained on Action Recognition datasets on depression severity facial videos from AVEC depression dataset, our framework is able to interpret the model's saliency maps by examining face regions and temporal expression semantics. Our approach generates both visual and quantitative explanations for the model's decisions, providing greater insight into its reasoning. In addition to this interpretability, our video-based modeling has improved upon previous single-face benchmarks for visual depression diagnosis, resulting in enhanced predictive performance. Overall, our work demonstrates the successful development of a framework capable of generating hypotheses from a facial model's decisions while simultaneously improving depression's predictive capabilities.

</details>


### [59] [VPA-Guard: Defending and Benchmarking Image-to-Video Generation Against Visual Prompt Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25592)
*Yining Sun,Haoyu Kang,Jiajun Wu,Heng Zhang,Danyang Zhang,Zhenjun Zhao,Haochen Han,Fangming Liu,Wai Kin Victor Chan,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 VVA-Bench，用于系统评估图像到视频生成中由静态视觉线索触发的隐式视觉提示攻击，并提出 VPA-Guard 防御框架以降低攻击成功率和有害性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成安全评测主要关注文本提示或显式图像内容风险，忽视了箭头、草图、表情符号等静态视觉提示可能被 I2V 模型解释为时间性动作指令，从而诱导生成不安全视频的风险。

Method: 作者构建了首个面向视觉中心提示攻击的系统基准 VVA-Bench，对攻击类型进行分类并评测多个先进 I2V 模型；同时提出 VPA-Guard，一种检索增强、自进化的防御框架，利用少样本推理识别潜在恶意意图，并在保持正常编辑能力的同时拦截风险输入。

Result: 实验显示，先进模型对该类攻击高度脆弱，部分模型攻击成功率可达 100.0%，另一模型达到 74.8%。VPA-Guard 平均降低攻击成功率 44.2%，降低有害性评分 73.4%，同时维持合法用户编辑的可用性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了 I2V 生成中隐式视觉提示攻击这一被低估的安全问题，并通过基准与防御框架为更安全、负责任的多模态生成提供了评测与缓解方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Image-to-Video (I2V) generation have transformed input images from simple appearance references into interactive control interfaces where visual cues such as arrows, sketches, and emojis orchestrate complex video dynamics with unprecedented controllability. However, these seemingly innocuous static cues can be interpreted by models as executable temporal instructions, unfolding into harmful actions in the generated videos. Despite the severity of this threat, existing safety benchmarks remain predominantly focused on text-based and content-only image-based jailbreaks, leaving implicit visual prompt attacks insufficiently explored. To bridge this gap, we present VVA-Bench, the first systematic benchmark for evaluating video generation safety under categorized vision-centric prompt attacks. Extensive experiments on VVA-Bench demonstrate that state-of-the-art models are highly susceptible to such attacks, with Attack Success Rates (ASR) reaching 100.0\% on Wan 2.7 and 74.8\% on Veo 3.1. To mitigate these risks, we propose VPA-Guard, a retrieval-augmented and self-evolving defense framework. By leveraging few-shot reasoning to identify latent malicious intents, our method reduces the attack ASR by 44.2\% and the harmfulness score by 73.4\% on average, while maintaining the model's utility for legitimate user edits. Our work provides both a rigorous benchmark and an effective defense strategy to advance safe and socially responsible multimodal generation.

</details>


### [60] [ScaleHP: Estimating Hand Pose in Metric Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25619)
*Ruitao Jing,Xingyu Chen,Hongyang Li,Qing Jiang,Yukai Shi,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScaleHP利用手骨比例等人体测量先验与尺度token，在单阶段框架中恢复相机坐标系下的绝对度量手部姿态，并在多个数据集上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部姿态估计方法多输出根节点相对坐标，难以恢复真实度量尺度；依赖外部深度估计模块又容易带来误差和不稳定性。因此，论文希望利用手部内部骨骼比例中稳定的形态学约束，直接推断绝对尺度与相机坐标系姿态。

Method: 提出端到端单阶段框架ScaleHP。该方法使用基于Transformer的解码器，并引入scale token融合多尺度形态与外观特征；进一步通过透视约束的最小二乘求解，将预测结果转换为相机坐标系中的度量三维坐标。

Result: ScaleHP在FreiHand上达到35.8 CS-MPJPE，在DexYCB和HO3Dv3上分别达到4.6/5.9 PA-MPJPE，表现为当前先进水平。

Conclusion: 手部骨骼的内部生物学比例约束能够有效降低相对几何误差与绝对尺度误差。ScaleHP避免了脆弱的外部深度模块，为真实场景中的泛化手部跟踪提供了稳健方案。

Abstract: Accurate metric-space hand pose estimation (HPE) is essential for immersive human-computer interaction and robotics. However, most existing methods predict poses in a root-relative coordinate system and cannot estimate the hand in absolute metric scale. In this work, we observe that the intrinsic proportional relationships among human hand bones encode stable anthropometric priors that implicitly correlate with the overall metric size of the hand. Leveraging this insight, we present ScaleHP, an end-to-end one-stage hand pose estimation framework that bypasses fragile extrinsic depth modules to recover the hand in metric space. ScaleHP employs a transformer-based decoder with a novel scale token to fuse multi-scale morphological and appearance features. By solving for metric coordinates through a perspective-constrained least-squares approach, we achieve high-precision pose estimation in the camera coordinate system. ScaleHP delivers state-of-the-art performance, including 35.8 CS-MPJPE on FreiHand and 4.6/5.9 PA-MPJPE on DexYCB and HO3Dv3. These results demonstrate that internal biological constraints significantly reduce relative geometry and absolute metric errors, offering a robust solution for generalized, real-world hand tracking.

</details>


### [61] [Steering Vision-Language Models with Joint Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25657)
*Huizhen Shu,Xuying Li,Hongxu Lin,Wenjie Sun,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出联合稀疏自编码器（JSAE），通过显式跨模态对齐约束，从视觉与语言激活中学习共享、可解释、可干预的概念特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器在语言模型分析中有效，但直接用于视觉-语言模型时，学到的表示往往难以作为可控的跨模态 steering 方向，限制了对多模态特征的干预式分析。

Method: 作者提出 JSAE，对序列池化后的图像与文本激活进行联合因子分解，并加入显式对齐约束，使两种模态映射到共享稀疏特征空间；随后在多个 VLM 的不同层上进行加性 steering 与特征抑制等双向干预实验。

Result: 在 LLaVA 等模型中，JSAE 能恢复食物、动物等可识别跨模态概念。加性 steering 在中后层、接近输出前的层效果最强，在两端较弱；抑制效果则在所测层中大致保持相近，差异处于统计噪声范围内。三个 VLM 上均观察到类似的层局部效应。

Conclusion: 显式对齐的稀疏表示比未加约束的替代方法更适合支持可控的多模态特征干预分析，并且这种可控性主要集中在可识别的特定层范围内。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have shown promise for analyzing language models, but applying them to vision-language models (VLMs) often yields representations that are difficult to use as controllable cross-modal steering directions. We introduce the Joint Sparse Autoencoder (JSAE), which uses an explicit alignment constraint to jointly factorize sequence-pooled vision and language activations into shared, interpretable image/caption-level features. Applied to LLaVA, JSAE recovers cross-modal features for recognizable concepts (e.g., food and animals). Through bidirectional interventions (additive steering and suppression), we observe a layer-dependent asymmetry under our protocol: additive steering peaks at mid-to-late (pre-output) layers and weakens at both ends, whereas suppression scores remain within a comparable range across all probed layers within statistical noise. Experiments on three VLMs, namely LLaVA-v1.6-Mistral-7B, Llama3-LLaVA-8B, and the MoE-based Qwen3-VL-30B, show related layer-localized effects across architectures. Together, these results suggest that explicitly aligned sparse representations support more controllable intervention-based analysis of multimodal features, within an identifiable layer range, than the unconstrained alternatives tested here.

</details>


### [62] [SSMNBench: Diagnosing Image-based Cross-View Human-Object Understanding via Single-View Sufficiency and Multi-View Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25634)
*Tianchen Guo,Chen Liu,Ling Chen,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSMNBench，用于诊断多模态大语言模型在跨视角人类中心场景理解中的真实能力，发现现有模型更多依赖单图语义平均和视角偏好，而非真正的跨视角证据融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角评测通常采用固定的“帧集合”输入，难以区分模型对视觉干扰的鲁棒性与其融合跨视角碎片化证据的能力；而 MLLM 在复杂跨视角人类及人-物场景中的推理能力尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 作者构建了包含 3,300 个精选问答对的诊断基准 SSMNBench，面向跨视角人类理解与人-物理解，并将任务划分为单视角充分（SVS）和多视角必要（MVN）两类；同时系统扰动可用视角，在 17 个先进 MLLM 上进行评估。

Result: 实验揭示了两类关键缺陷：在 SVS 场景中，冗余视角会导致模型出现明显的“干扰退化”；在 MVN 场景中，模型难以整合来自不同相机的碎片化几何证据。整体上，现代 MLLM 倾向于多张单图语义平均和视角偏好。

Conclusion: SSMNBench 作为严格的诊断框架，揭示了当前 MLLM 在跨视角理解中的根本脆弱性，可推动未来具备跨视角感知与融合能力的多模态架构发展。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown remarkable progress in single-image perception, yet their ability to reason about complex cross-view human-centric scenes remains largely unverified. Current multi-view benchmarks evaluate models using a fixed "bag of frames" and thus conflate a model's robustness to visual distraction with its genuine ability to fuse fragmented cross-view evidence. To address this issue, we introduce SSMNBench, a diagnostic benchmark comprising 3,300 curated QA pairs for cross-view human and human-object understanding. SSMNBench uniquely categorizes tasks into Single-View Sufficiency (SVS) and Multi-View Necessity (MVN). By systematically perturbing view availability across 17 state-of-the-art MLLMs, critical limitations are revealed: models suffer from severe "distraction degradation" when presented with redundant views (SVS), and fail to integrate fragmented geometric evidence across cameras (MVN). Our evaluations demonstrate that modern MLLMs rely on multiple single-image semantic averaging and view preference rather than genuine cross-view synthesis. By exposing these fundamental vulnerabilities, SSMNBench provides a rigorous diagnostic framework to drive the advancement of future cross-view-aware multimodal architectures. The code is available at: $ \href{https://github.com/gtc-gh/SSMNBench}{\text{SSMNBench}} $

</details>


### [63] [Point Cloud Diffusion with Global and Local Reconstruction for Instance-Level 3D Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25740)
*Linchun Wu,Qin Zou,Jiwen Lu,Qingquan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PCDiff 是一个用于点云 3D 异常生成与检测的扩散框架，通过多模态条件生成弱缺陷异常，并用局部-全局联合重建提升检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业制造中的高精度 3D 点云异常检测需要识别极弱缺陷（如划痕），但现有重建式方法难以恢复和检测微小异常偏差；同时正常背景区域在重建中容易出现位置偏移，导致误报。

Method: 论文提出 PCDiff：在生成阶段，将实例级多模态注意力嵌入扩散生成框架，以纹理梯度、图像块、文本和掩码作为条件，生成高质量弱缺陷异常；在检测阶段，引入局部-全局联合重建算法，同时实现局部异常修复和全局几何一致性约束。

Result: 大量实验表明，PCDiff 在 3D 异常生成保真度和重建质量方面显著优于现有先进方法，并带来异常检测准确率的明显提升。

Conclusion: PCDiff 通过结合实例级多模态条件扩散生成与局部-全局一致重建，有效缓解弱缺陷难检测和背景误报问题，是面向工业点云异常检测的有效框架。

Abstract: 3D anomaly detection in point clouds is critical for high-precision industrial manufacturing. Reconstruction-based methods have laid a strong foundation by detecting 3D anomalies through comparisons between defective inputs and their reconstructed normal counterparts. However, existing methods still suffer from two challenges: 1) the foreground weak defective regions such as scratches are hard to reconstruct and detect, where the anomaly deviations in normalized point clouds can be as small as $10^{-3}$; 2) the background non-defective regions are prone to get positional bias in reconstruction, which leads to false positives. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{PCDiff}, a point cloud diffusion framework for instance-level 3D anomaly generation and detection. In the generation phase, an instance-level multi-modal attention is embedded into the generation framework, where anomalies are conditioned with texture gradient, image patch, text and mask. The instance-level condition enables the high-quality generation of weak-defective anomalies. In the detection phase, a joint local-global reconstruction algorithm is introduced to ensure local anomaly restoration and global geometric consistency, which preserves background normal structure while restoring the foreground defect. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed PCDiff significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both 3D anomaly generation fidelity and reconstruction quality, leading to substantial improvements in anomaly detection accuracy.

</details>


### [64] [How Robust is OCR-Reasoning? Evaluating OCR-Reasoning Robustness of Vision-Language Models under Visual Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26041)
*Yuxing Cheng,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OCR-Robust 基准，用于系统评估视觉语言模型在 OCR 推理任务中面对可控视觉扰动时的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在 OCR 基准上表现较强，但其在视觉退化条件下的稳定性仍缺乏系统研究；视觉损坏会导致 OCR 错误和结构失真，从而影响后续推理。

Method: 构建包含 812 个样本的 OCR-Robust，覆盖 OCR1.0（文档、场景文字、收据、手写、数学内容）和 OCR2.0（图表、几何图、表格）。通过对 18 种候选扰动进行先导实验，筛选出 5 类代表性扰动，并设置 3 个严重程度。使用 clean accuracy、RCR、WCR 和 CRI 指标评估 18 个模型，包括闭源系统、开源 VLM 和 OCR+LLM 管线。

Result: 实验显示，干净数据上的高准确率并不必然对应更强鲁棒性；模型在最坏扰动条件下可能显著退化，尤其是在依赖结构信息的 OCR 推理任务中。图表和表格相比文档类输入对扰动更脆弱。

Conclusion: OCR 推理鲁棒性需要独立于干净准确率进行评估；OCR-Robust 为分析不同模型在文本丰富、结构敏感场景下的抗扰动能力提供了系统基准。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on OCR-based benchmarks and increasingly focused on text-rich understanding, but their robustness under controlled visual degradation remains insufficiently understood. This gap is critical for OCR reasoning, where visual corruption can induce OCR errors and structural distortions, thereby introducing uncertainty into the reasoning task. To systematically study this problem, we introduce OCR-Robust, a benchmark designed for evaluating OCR reasoning robustness under visual perturbations. It contains 812 samples across two complementary subsets: OCR1.0, covering documents, scene text, receipts, handwriting, and mathematical content, and OCR2.0, focusing on charts, geometry diagrams, and tables. To enable efficient yet informative evaluation, we conduct a pilot study over 18 candidate perturbations and select 5 representative types at 3 severity levels each based on their impact and cross-model discriminability. We evaluate robustness using clean accuracy, Relative Corruption Retention (RCR), Worst-Case Retention (WCR), and a composite Corruption Robustness Index (CRI), and benchmark 18 models spanning proprietary systems, open-source VLMs, and OCR+LLM pipelines. Our results show that higher clean accuracy does not necessarily imply stronger robustness, and that models can suffer pronounced degradation in the worst case on OCR tasks that are sensitive to structure, and charts and tables are substantially more fragile than document-like inputs under perturbation.

</details>


### [65] [Auto-Labelling-Based Domain Transfer for 3D Object Detection on a Bicycle-Mounted LiDAR Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25652)
*Mario Finkbeiner,Max A. Buettner,Kanak Mazumder,Fabian B. Flohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个来自骑行者视角的3D目标检测基准，并验证了车辆端预训练LiDAR检测器在该场景下的泛化能力与自动标注微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶与城市交通安全需要可靠感知行人、骑行者等弱势道路使用者；但骑行者视角的3D标注数据稀缺，现有LiDAR检测器多基于车辆视角数据训练，其跨域泛化能力尚未系统评估。

Method: 作者基于FUSE-Bike平台采集并标注1,027帧LiDAR关键帧，包含超过18,000个3D框；选取4个在nuScenes上预训练的3D检测器进行零样本评估，并使用面向VRU的自动标注流程生成训练标签进行微调，再用1,854个人工验证GT框评估性能。

Result: 零样本条件下的域差异主要集中在VRU类别；经过自动标签微调后，mAP最高提升23.4个百分点，行人和骑行者类别增益最大，且微调后的检测器性能超过其训练所用自动标签质量。

Conclusion: 该基准为VRU中心的3D检测提供了可复现评测基础，并表明在将车辆端检测器适配到自行车平台时，自动标签可以作为人工标注的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Reliable 3D perception of vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as cyclists and pedestrians is essential for their safety in urban traffic and a core requirement for autonomous driving (AD). Alongside advances in vehicle-based perception, research increasingly equips bicycles with sensors to study traffic from a perspective native to VRUs. Such platforms still rely on LiDAR detectors originally trained on vehicle data, yet annotated 3D data from a cyclist's perspective is scarce. How well these detectors generalise to this setting has not been evaluated. We present a 3D object detection benchmark of 1,027 annotated LiDAR keyframes (over 18,000 3D bounding boxes) from the FUSE-Bike platform in urban Munich. We evaluate four nuScenes-pre-trained detectors against 1,854 human-verified ground-truth (GT) boxes both in their original form and after finetuning on training labels produced by a VRU-dedicated auto-labelling pipeline that requires no manual annotation. The zero-shot domain gap is concentrated on the VRU classes. Finetuning recovers most of it, improving mean average precision (mAP) by up to 23.4 points with the largest gains on pedestrians and cyclists, and the adapted detectors even surpass the quality of the auto-labels they were trained on. The benchmark provides a reproducible baseline for VRU-centric 3D detection and shows that auto-labels are a viable substitute for manual annotation when adapting vehicle-trained detectors to a cyclist platform.

</details>


### [66] [Towards a Dynamic and Fixed-budget Memory Bank for Efficient Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25658)
*Baiyang Song,Yuli Lin,Qiong Wu,Tao Chen,Jun Peng,Xiao Chen,Yiyi Zhou,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CausalMem，一种无需训练的固定预算动态记忆机制，用于提升多模态大语言模型的流式视频理解能力，在有限视觉 token 存储下保留关键语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型难以处理流式视频理解：视频帧持续增长、未来内容不可预知、用户指令也可能变化，因此需要一种在线、动态且固定预算的视觉记忆机制来高效保存已观测视频中的重要信息。

Method: CausalMem 构建动态视觉记忆库，通过在线语义基来建模已观测视频流的主要语义，并估计视觉 token 的冗余度，据此更新固定容量记忆库；该方法无需额外训练，可接入 LLaVA-OneVision 和 Qwen2.5-VL 等 MLLM。

Result: 在流式与离线视频理解基准上均优于现有方法，平均准确率分别提升约 3.2% 和 3.0%；在 12k token 预算下可记忆小时级流式视频，实现超过 20 倍视觉 token 压缩，存储占用约 82 MB。

Conclusion: CausalMem 证明了通过因果在线更新和语义冗余压缩，可以在固定内存预算下有效保留长视频关键语义，从而提升 MLLM 的流式与离线视频理解性能。

Abstract: Currently, streaming video understanding is still a daunting task for existing \emph{multimodal large language models} (MLLMs). Its difficulties not only lie in handling the ever-increasing video frames, but also in the unpredictability of future video content and input instructions. In this paper, we study this task from the perspective of constructing a dynamic but fixed-budget memory bank, and propose a novel and training-free approach termed \emph{\textbf{CausalMem}}. CausalMem is dedicated to constructing a dynamic visual memory update mechanism, thereby maximizing the amount of information in streaming video within a limited memory space, much like the human brain. In practice, CausalMem estimates the redundancy of visual tokens and updates the memory bank via an online semantic basis, which models the principal semantics of the observed video stream. To validate CausalMem, we apply it to two representative MLLMs, namely LLaVA-OneVision and Qwen2.5-VL respectively, and conduct extensive experiments on both streaming and offline video understanding benchmarks. The experimental results not only show the great advantages than existing methods under both streaming and offline settings, \emph{e.g.}, $+3.2\%$ and $+3.0\%$ average accuracy gains respectively, but also witness the superior semantic preservation for streaming videos, \emph{e.g.}, using 12$k$ token budgets to memorize hour-long streaming videos, which achieves more than \textbf{20$\times$} visual token compression ratio and only occupies about \textbf{82 MB} storage. \textbf{Our code} is given in \href{https://github.com/hktk07/CausalMem}{CausalMem}.

</details>


### [67] [Edges Before Embeddings: A Confidence-Aware Blur Gate for Vision-Language Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25838)
*Duy Tran Thanh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MagikaDocumentFromPixel：一种轻量级、CPU 友好的图像清晰度质量门控器，可在约 7 ms 内将单张图像分类为清晰、模糊或不确定，用于在生产视觉流水线中提前拦截低质量输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级视觉流水线在遇到模糊图像时常会静默退化，后续 OCR、检索和视觉语言模型调用难以恢复有效结果，却仍消耗大量计算资源。因此需要一个快速、低成本且可置信度感知的前置质量判断模块。

Method: 作者通过 46 种配置、8 轮经验搜索确定最佳训练方案，发现输入分辨率是主要影响因素，模型容量在 384 px 及以上才明显受益。最终采用 MobileNetV3-Large，在 384×384 输入下训练，并加入 Edge Prior Module（EPM）：将拉普拉斯幅值作为辅助输入通道，使网络直接利用经典模糊检测中的边缘/频谱证据。同时引入基于选择性预测的置信度感知路由形式，并在测试时使用 5 尺度增强。

Result: 最终模型在 paired GoPro Large 帧上训练，达到 F1=0.9803、AUC=0.9989，ONNX 模型大小为 17 MB，单 CPU 核推理约 7 ms。与相同硬件上的固定尺度基线 F1=0.9672 相比提升 +1.31 个百分点；EPM 在匹配环境比较中带来 +1.3 F1 提升。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，轻量级图像质量门控可以有效减少低质量输入导致的下游无效计算；输入分辨率、边缘先验和置信度感知路由是关键设计因素。其局限包括仅在单一运动模糊分布上验证、结果来自单随机种子，以及校准评估偏定性而非定量。

Abstract: Production vision pipelines silently degrade on blurry input, wasting compute on downstream OCR, retrieval, and vision-language model (VLM) calls that cannot recover a usable output. We present MagikaDocumentFromPixel, a lightweight, CPU-friendly image quality gate that classifies a single image as sharp, blurred, or uncertain in roughly 7 ms on a single CPU core. The contributions are (i) a recipe selected from a 46-configuration, 8-sweep empirical search that isolates input resolution as the dominant lever and shows architecture capacity only pays off at >= 384 px; (ii) a confidence-aware routing formalism grounded in classical selective prediction; (iii) the Edge Prior Module (EPM), a Laplacian-magnitude auxiliary input channel that gives the network direct access to the spectral evidence that classical blur heuristics rely on and that lifts test F1 by +1.3 points in a matched-env comparison; and (iv) an observation that the gate is one instance of a recurring design pattern that appears independently in Magika content-type detection, risk-controlled OCR with VLMs, and DocVLM. The final recipe MobileNetV3-Large with the EPM trained at 384x384 on paired GoPro Large frames, evaluated with 5-scale test-time augmentation reaches F1 = 0.9803 (AUC 0.9989) with a 17 MB ONNX artifact, improving over our fixed-scale baseline on the same hardware (F1 = 0.9672) by +1.31 points. We are explicit about limitations: results are on a single motion-blur distribution, numbers are from a single seed, and calibration is qualitative rather than measured.

</details>


### [68] [Falcon: Functional Assembly and Language for Compositional Reasoning in X-ray](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25701)
*Yonathan Michael,Mohamad Alansari,Natnael Takele,Andreas Henschel,Naoufel Werghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Conventional vision-language models are largely object-centric, focusing on detecting and describing individual entities. In safety-critical X-ray baggage screening, however, threat often emerges not from a single object but from the functional compatibility of spatially dispersed components, such as batteries, detonators, and explosive charges. We formalize this setting as \emph{compositional threat reasoning}, where risk is modeled as a relational property of grounded regions rather than an independent detection outcome. We introduce \textbf{Falcon}, a multimodal framework that abstracts segmentation-aware region features into a structured safety state capturing component presence, pairwise functional compatibility, and scene-level risk. This structured representation is injected into the language model as an explicit intermediate interface, encouraging relationally consistent and safety-aware reasoning. To evaluate this problem, we present \textbf{Falcon-X}, a benchmark that unifies dense grounding with structured supervision over component completeness and risk inference in cluttered X-ray imagery. Experiments show that while existing multimodal models adapt to appearance, they struggle with compositional safety reasoning. Falcon improves functional grounding and produces more coherent threat assessments, establishing compositional safety reasoning as a distinct evaluation paradigm for multimodal systems.

</details>


### [69] [Enhancing Brain MRI Anomaly Detection and Reasoning with ROI Rethink and Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25894)
*Shangkun Li,Jie Xu,Yi Guo,Zeju Li,Yuanyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrReMark 通过在脑 MRI 诊断中引入显式 ROI 框选与假设-验证流程，提高了模型的可解释性、定位能力，并降低了异常幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型多采用单次推理生成诊断，缺乏对支持诊断结论的图像区域进行空间定位，导致临床可审计性不足，并可能在正常扫描中产生幻觉性发现。

Method: 提出 BrReMark 框架：先生成潜在异常假设并用边界框显式标注相关 ROI，再基于标注证据重新检查并验证诊断结论。训练上结合结构化推理轨迹的监督微调，以及基于定位准确性和诊断推理的复合奖励强化学习；同时采用基于领域随机化的病灶合成增强以提升 OOD 泛化。

Result: 在内部基准上，相比基础模型，mAP50 从 0.74% 提升到 37.54%，并达到 21.57% Clinical F1 和 45.26% 诊断准确率；在 NOVA OOD 基准上整体表现有竞争力，相比 SOTA 假阳性降低 45.7%。

Conclusion: 显式的假设生成、ROI 定位与证据再验证机制有助于提升开放式脑 MRI 诊断的可信度、空间可解释性和 OOD 场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models typically generate diagnoses through single-pass inference without indicating which image regions support their conclusions. This lack of spatial grounding limits clinical utility: outputs cannot be audited, and models may hallucinate findings on normal scans. We present BrReMark (Brain Rethink via ROI Marking), a framework that introduces explicit region marking into brain MRI diagnosis. The model first generates hypotheses about potential abnormalities and grounds them through explicit bounding box marking, then verifies conclusions by re-examining the marked evidence. Training combines supervised fine-tuning on structured reasoning trajectories with reinforcement learning using a composite reward over localization accuracy and diagnostic reasoning. Furthermore, we integrate a domain randomization-based pathology synthesis augmentation strategy to improve the model's generalizability to out-of-distribution (OOD) data. On internal benchmark, BrReMark improves mAP50 from 0.74% to 37.54% compared to the base model, while achieving 21.57% Clinical F1 and 45.26% diagnostic accuracy. On NOVA OOD benchmark, it also achieves competitive overall performance with a 45.7% reduction in false positives compared to the state-of-the-art, indicating reduced hallucination on rare pathologies. These findings suggest that explicit hypothesis-verification grounding is a practical path toward trustworthy open-ended brain MRI diagnosis across both in-distribution and OOD settings.

</details>


### [70] [What Does the Brain See? Multiview Neural Representations to Demystify the Brain-Visual Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25718)
*Salini Yadav,Taveena Lotey,Pravendra Singh,Partha Pratim Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于EEG零样本视觉解码的统一多视图表示学习框架，通过联合建模时间、频谱与空间电极交互结构，并与预训练视觉语义表示进行对比对齐，在THINGS-EEG上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG视觉解码受低信噪比、非平稳性和空间分辨率有限等问题限制；现有方法多依赖整体EEG嵌入，容易忽略视觉感知相关的互补时间动态、频谱模式和空间电极结构，因此需要更细粒度的多视图建模以提升语义对齐与泛化能力。

Method: 构建统一的多视图EEG编码器：1）使用输入条件化状态空间模型刻画时间动态；2）引入可学习小波频谱分解进行样本自适应频率建模；3）采用注意力调制的图学习建模电极间结构化交互。随后融合多视图EEG嵌入，并通过带EEG特定正则化的对比学习，将其与预训练视觉表示对齐到共享语义空间，实现200类零样本视觉分类。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG基准上达到SOTA：被试内设置Top-1为54.8%、Top-5为85.6%；跨被试设置Top-1为15.3%、Top-5为45.4%。此外首次系统开展跨会话EEG-图像解码评估，取得Top-1为40.8%、Top-5为78.0%。

Conclusion: 显式建模EEG的多视图神经结构能够增强脑电信号与视觉语义表示的对齐效果，并提升EEG零样本视觉解码在被试内、跨被试和跨会话场景下的泛化性能。

Abstract: Zero-shot visual decoding from electroencephalography (EEG) aims to infer visual semantics from non-invasive neural recordings, but remains challenging due to the low signal-to-noise ratio, non-stationarity, and limited spatial resolution of EEG. Existing EEG-vision alignment methods often rely on holistic EEG embeddings, which can obscure the complementary temporal, spectral, and spatial structure underlying visual perception. We introduce a unified multiview EEG representation learning framework for aligning brain responses with visual semantic embeddings. Our method builds an EEG encoder that jointly models three complementary views: input-conditioned state-space temporal dynamics, learnable wavelet-based spectral decomposition for sample-adaptive frequency modeling, and attention-modulated graph learning for structured electrode interactions. The resulting multiview EEG embeddings are fused and aligned with pretrained visual representations in a shared semantic space using contrastive learning with EEG-specific regularization, enabling 200-way zero-shot visual classification. Experiments on THINGS-EEG benchmark show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 54.8% Top-1 and 85.6% Top-5 accuracy in the within-subject setting and 15.3% Top-1 and 45.4% Top-5 accuracy in the cross-subject setting. We further present the first systematic cross-session EEG-image decoding evaluation, achieving 40.8% Top-1 and 78.0% Top-5 accuracy. These results suggest that explicitly modeling multiview neural structure improves both semantic alignment and generalization in EEG-based visual decoding.

</details>


### [71] [Efficient Real-World Dehazing via Physics-Inspired Global-Local Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25732)
*Yifei Qu,Ru Li,Junjie Chen,Jinyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PGL-Net 是一种面向真实场景单图像去雾的轻量化网络，通过物理启发的全局-局部解耦设计，在提升恢复质量的同时显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界单图像去雾存在空间与光谱散射变化，问题高度病态；传统物理反演依赖简化假设且鲁棒性不足，而重型盲去雾网络难以部署到边缘设备。因此需要兼顾恢复质量、低复杂度和低延迟的实用去雾模型。

Method: 提出 Physics-Inspired Global-Local Decoupling Network（PGL-Net），不显式估计物理参数，而是在算子层面引入物理归纳偏置。模型将去雾分解为全局分布校正与局部结构细化：PAF 模块在层级跳连中进行全局条件仿射融合，用于补偿雾霾导致的分布偏移；DAM 模块通过动态特征调制，自适应恢复空间和光谱变化的细节。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准上，PGL-Net 取得了领先的图像恢复质量并显著降低模型复杂度。与近期 SOTA 方法 SGDN 相比，Tiny 版本 PGL-Net-T 的 PSNR 最高提升 2.6 dB，且持续提升下游目标检测精度，同时推理延迟降低超过 10 倍。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过物理启发但避免显式物理参数估计的全局-局部解耦设计，可以在真实图像去雾任务中同时实现高质量恢复与高效部署，适合边缘端低延迟应用。

Abstract: Real-world single image dehazing is highly ill-posed due to spatially and spectrally varying scattering, while practical deployment demands lightweight and low-latency models. Existing approaches either rely on fragile physical inversion under simplified assumptions or adopt heavy blind architectures unsuitable for edge deployment.
