To overcome these limitations, we propose PGL-Net (Physics-Inspired Global-Local Decoupling Network), a lightweight framework that incorporates physical inductive biases via operator-level emulation, avoiding explicit parameter estimation. It decouples dehazing into global distribution rectification and local structural refinement. A Physics-Inspired Affine Fusion (PAF) module performs globally conditioned alignment across hierarchical skip connections to compensate for haze-induced bias, while a compact Degradation-Aware Modulation (DAM) block adaptively restores spatially and spectrally variant details through dynamic feature modulation. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world benchmarks demonstrate that PGL-Net achieves state-of-the-art restoration quality with significantly reduced complexity. Compared with the recent SOTA SGDN, the Tiny variant (PGL-Net-T) improves PSNR by up to 2.6dB and consistently enhances downstream object detection accuracy, while achieving over a 10x reduction in inference latency. Code is publicly available at: https://github.com/sc-30-bit/PGL-Net.

</details>


### [72] [Pulmonary Embolism Risk Stratification from CTPA and Medical Records: Vascular Graphs Are Not All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25956)
*Nathan Painchaud,Tristan Habémont,Morgane des Ligneris,Allan Serva,Pierre Croisille,Laurent Bertoletti,Thomas Lampert,Johannes F. Lutzeyer,Odyssée Merveille*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在缺少血液检测数据的常规场景下，能否仅依靠病历信息与CTPA图像提取的生物标志物完成肺栓塞风险分层；结果显示病历与心脏相关全局特征最有预测价值，血管图结构特征和GNN未能超过强表格基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺栓塞风险分层对临床决策至关重要，但现有指南通常依赖病历、CTPA测量参数和血液检测；现实中血液检测常缺失，因此需要评估仅使用病历和CTPA影像标志物时的风险分层可行性。

Method: 作者使用包含353例、数据较完整的私有肺栓塞风险分层数据集，比较多种多模态融合策略：将肺血管生物标志物加入表格模型，并利用肺血管树的内在图表示训练图神经网络，同时与基于全局特征的强表格基线进行对比。

Result: 实验表明，在全局特征中，病历信息和心脏生物标志物是最重要的预测因子；加入血管生物标志物并未进一步提升分层性能；基于血管树图结构的GNN也未能优于强表格基线。

Conclusion: 研究提示，至少在该数据集和任务设定下，肺血管图结构可能不包含足够的肺栓塞风险分层判别信息；作者进一步讨论了模型与数据层面的可能原因，并指出该发现与直觉相反。

Abstract: Risk stratification for pulmonary embolism (PE) is critical for clinical decision-making. Stratification guidelines are based on patient medical records, parameters measured from computed tomography pulmonary angiography (CTPA), and blood tests. However, blood tests are often missing in routine practice. This work studies whether state-of-the-art models can accurately classify risk stratification from only medical records and biomarkers extracted from CTPA images. We benchmark different approaches to combine medical records and cardiac biomarkers with rich pulmonary vascular information; we add vascular biomarkers to tabular models and apply graph neural networks (GNNs) on the vascular tree's intrinsic graph representation. We use a private dataset (n=353) with uniquely complete data for PE risk stratification. Our results show that, among global features, medical records and cardiac biomarkers are the most significant predictors, while vascular biomarkers do not further improve stratification. Even more surprising, even GNNs on vascular graphs fail to outperform strong tabular baseline on global features. We consider hypotheses, on both models and data, that could explain this suboptimal performance. Our investigation suggests that, counter-intuitively, vascular graphs might hold no discriminative information for PE risk stratification. Code is available from https://github.com/creatis-myriad/GENESIS.

</details>


### [73] [UniTeD: Unified Temporal Diffusion for Joint Perception and Planning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25736)
*Bo Zhao,Xinting Zhao,Naifan Li,Erkang Cheng,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniTeD 将感知与规划统一到共享的时序扩散生成框架中，通过联合去噪和时序建模提升端到端自动驾驶的鲁棒性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式端到端自动驾驶方法通常只在规划模块中使用扩散模型，并依赖独立判别式感知网络的固定输出；这种解耦设计会将感知误差传递给规划器，增加优化难度并降低系统鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 Unified Temporal Diffusion（UniTeD）框架，在共享生成空间中通过迭代去噪联合建模感知与规划，实现双向信息交互与多任务互相 refinement；进一步引入时序上下文以支持流式场景，设计 Temporal Transition Module（TTM）处理历史帧与当前帧之间的噪声级别不匹配，并提出 Anchor Refresh Strategy（ARS）缓解稀疏扩散端到端驾驶框架中的训练-推理分布偏移。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，UniTeD 在不依赖额外复杂技巧的情况下取得了当前最优性能，超过近期判别式端到端方法和扩散式规划方法。

Conclusion: 统一的时序扩散范式能够有效减少感知与规划解耦带来的误差传播，并通过时序建模和分布偏移缓解机制提升端到端自动驾驶系统的稳定性与泛化表现。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown strong potential for multi-modal planning in end-to-end autonomous driving. However, most existing methods confine diffusion to the planning module, conditioning on fixed outputs from separate discriminative perception networks. This decoupled design propagates perception errors to the planner, increasing optimization difficulty and reducing robustness. To overcome these limitations, we propose UniTeD, a Unified Temporal Diffusion framework that jointly models perception and planning through iterative denoising in a shared generative space. By enabling bidirectional information exchange, the framework facilitates mutual refinement between tasks and improves robustness via noise-conditioned multi-task training. We further extend this unified diffusion paradigm to a streaming setting by incorporating temporal context. A Temporal Transition Module (TTM) is introduced to resolve the noise-level mismatch between historical and current frames. In addition, we propose an Anchor Refresh Strategy (ARS) to alleviate the training-inference distribution shift commonly observed in sparse diffusion-based end-to-end driving frameworks. Without bells and whistles, UniTeD achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, surpassing both recent discriminative end-to-end methods and diffusion-based planning approaches.

</details>


### [74] [Dual Distribution Estimation for Zero-shot Noisy Test-Time Adaptation with VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25758)
*Wenjie Zhu,Yabin Zhang,Liang Xu,Xin Jin,Wenjun Zeng,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 DDE，一个零样本、免训练的噪声测试时自适应框架，通过双重高斯分布估计提升分布内分类与分布外检测效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应方法在真实场景中易受 OOD 异常样本影响；已有零样本 NTTA 方法依赖测试时判别式训练，容易产生过度自信误分类且推理效率低。

Method: 提出 Dual Distribution Estimation，包括 PFDE 和 NLDE。PFDE 建模类别级包含/排除高斯分布并构造校准对比得分以提升 ID 分类；NLDE 建模负标签分布，挖掘判别性强的标签以增强 OOD 识别并减少伪相关。

Result: 在大规模 ImageNet 基准上，DDE 的调和平均准确率提升 3.70%，OOD 检测 FPR95 降低 6.20%，同时保持高效、可扩展的在线推理。

Conclusion: DDE 将零样本 NTTA 从实例级训练转向免训练分布建模，在数据稀缺和含噪测试环境中表现出较强鲁棒性与实用价值。

Abstract: While test-time adaptation (TTA) empowers vision-language models to adapt without costly retraining, it remains highly vulnerable to out-of-distribution (OOD) outliers prevalent in real-world applications. This discrepancy motivates Noisy TTA (NTTA), an online task to filter noisy OOD samples on the fly while maximizing in-distribution (ID) classification accuracy. Existing zero-shot NTTA approaches typically rely on test-time discriminative training, leading to overconfident misclassifications and significantly degraded inference efficiency. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework named Dual Distribution Estimation (DDE), shifting the zero-shot NTTA paradigm from instance-level learning to training-free Gaussian distribution modeling. DDE incorporates two novel modules: Positive Feature Distribution Estimation (PFDE) and Negative Label Distribution Estimation (NLDE). PFDE explicitly models class-wise inclusion and exclusion Gaussian distributions to formulate a calibrated contrastive score, robustly enhancing ID accuracy. In parallel, NLDE improves OOD identification by explicitly modeling the negative label distribution to mine highly discriminative labels, effectively mitigating spurious correlations. Extensive experiments show that on the large-scale ImageNet benchmark, DDE achieves an improvement of 3.70\% in harmonic mean accuracy and reduces the FPR95 for OOD detection by 6.20\%, while ensuring highly scalable and efficient online inference. Furthermore, DDE is zero-shot and training-free, demonstrating remarkable robustness in data-scarce scenarios. Codes are available at https://github.com/ZhuWenjie98/DDE.

</details>


### [75] [TriViewBench: Controlled Complexity Scaling for Multi-View Structural Reasoning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26029)
*Yu-Yang Chen,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TriViewBench，一个基于合成 3D 场景的三视图视觉推理基准，用于系统评估多模态大语言模型在对象数量、遮挡和跨视图复杂性增加时的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在标准视觉问答基准上表现较强，但在受控结构复杂度下的扩展能力、遮挡处理能力以及跨视图空间推理机制仍缺乏系统理解。

Method: 作者构建了包含 1,923 个场景和 14K+ 问答对的 TriViewBench，显式参数化对象数量与遮挡程度，并划分为四个复杂度等级和三类任务：局部决策、对象计数、全局恢复；随后在统一提示协议下评测 18 个开源和闭源 MLLM，并分析 CoT 提示与错误模式。

Result: 所有模型呈现一致能力层级：局部决策 > 对象计数 > 全局恢复。性能随复杂度单调下降：局部决策相对下降 12.11%，对象计数下降 59.14%，全局恢复下降 80.02%。对象计数中，单视图主要因遮挡盲区导致少计数，多视图则因跨视图身份混淆导致多计数。CoT 总体收益近零（Δ=-0.16%），且对全局恢复的作用受模型能力门控。

Conclusion: 当前 MLLM 的主要瓶颈不在表层推理策略，而在跨视图空间表征与结构化视觉理解能力。TriViewBench 可作为诊断模型结构推理失败与可扩展性限制的受控评测框架。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate strong performance on standard visual question answering benchmarks, yet their scalability under controlled structural complexity remains poorly understood. We introduce TriViewBench, a controlled three-view visual reasoning benchmark constructed from synthetic 3D scenes with explicitly parameterized object count and occlusion. The benchmark contains 1,923 scenes and over 14K Question-Answer (QA) pairs organized into four complexity levels and three reasoning categories: Local Decision, Object Counting, and Global Recovery. We evaluate 18 open- and closed-source MLLMs under a unified prompting protocol. All 18 models exhibit an identical capability hierarchy without exception (Local Decision > Object Counting > Global Recovery), and performance degrades monotonically with complexity: Local Decision tasks decline modestly (12.11% relative drop), while Object Counting degrades substantially (59.14%) and Global Recovery collapses severely (80.02%). Error analysis on Object Counting reveals two mechanistically independent failure modes: single-view tasks are dominated by undercounting due to occlusion blindness, whereas the multi-view task reverses to overcounting due to cross-view identity confusion. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting yields near-zero overall benefit ($Δ= -0.16\%$) and its effect on Global Recovery is strongly capability-gated, suggesting that the bottleneck lies in cross-view spatial representation rather than reasoning strategy. These findings reveal fundamental scalability limitations in current MLLMs and position TriViewBench as a controlled diagnostic framework for analyzing structural reasoning failures.

</details>


### [76] [ShutterMuse: Capture-Time Photography Guidance with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25763)
*Jiayu Li,Yixiao Fang,Tianyu Hu,Wei Cheng,Ping Huang,Zheheng Fan,Gang Yu,Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CaptureGuide-Bench 与 CaptureGuide-Dataset，并训练 ShutterMuse，用于评估和提升多模态大模型在拍摄时构图与姿态指导中的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实摄影需要在拍摄时同时为摄影者提供构图/取景指导，并为被摄主体提供姿态建议；但现有美学裁剪基准主要关注事后裁剪预测，忽视主体侧建议，导致 MLLM 的拍摄时交互指导能力缺乏系统评估。

Method: 作者构建包含两个互补任务的 CaptureGuide-Bench：摄影者侧的构图决策与细化，以及主体侧的场景条件姿态推荐。同时构建含 13 万样本、文本理由与结构化视觉标注的 CaptureGuide-Dataset，并基于监督微调和强化微调训练统一 MLLM ShutterMuse。

Result: 评测显示，通用 MLLM 能进行构图决策但细化定位不精确；专用美学裁剪模型能有效定位裁剪区域但仅限于细化任务；二者均不能提供可操作的姿态指导。ShutterMuse 在摄影者侧取得最佳总体性能，并以更低推理成本在主体侧姿态推荐上达到有竞争力表现。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，经过合适数据与训练流程增强的 MLLM 可作为拍摄过程中的交互式摄影助手，同时覆盖构图优化与姿态建议，拓展了多模态模型在真实摄影捕获阶段的应用潜力。

Abstract: Real-world photography requires capture-time guidance for both camera framing and subject pose. Yet existing aesthetic cropping benchmarks mainly evaluate post-hoc crop prediction and overlook subject-side recommendations, leaving the capture-time guidance capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce CaptureGuide-Bench, a benchmark with two complementary tasks: photographer-side composition decision and refinement, and subject-side scene-conditioned pose recommendation. Our evaluation reveals limitations: general-purpose MLLMs can make composition decisions but lack precise refinement localization, while specialized aesthetic cropping models localize crops effectively but are limited to refinement; neither provides actionable pose guidance. To support model development, we further construct CaptureGuide-Dataset, comprising 130K samples with textual rationales and structured visual annotations, and develop ShutterMuse, a unified MLLM trained with supervised and reinforcement fine-tuning. Experiments on CaptureGuide-Bench show that ShutterMuse achieves the best overall photographer-side performance among evaluated baselines and competitive subject-side pose recommendation with substantially lower inference cost, demonstrating the potential of MLLMs as interactive assistants for photography during image capture.

</details>


### [77] [A welding penetration prediction model for laser welding process based on self-supervised learning using physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26059)
*Sen Li,Xiaoying Liu,Xiaojian Xu,Chendong Shao,Yaqi Wang,Ling Lan,Xinhua Tang,Haichao Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimPhysNet通过将物理先验嵌入自监督对比学习，并结合少样本原型网络，在仅使用少量标注图像的情况下实现激光焊接熔透状态的高精度分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光焊接全熔透状态对获得无缺陷焊缝至关重要，但传统监督学习方法依赖大量高质量标注数据，限制了其在工业场景中的应用。因此需要一种在标注样本有限条件下仍能准确预测熔透状态的方法。

Method: 提出SimPhysNet：首先采用自监督学习范式，在对比学习框架中嵌入物理先验；通过物理信息神经网络（PINN）引导模型从大量未标注图像中提取与熔池和匙孔相关的物理有意义特征；同时设计三类图像增强任务提升泛化能力；随后利用基于原型网络的少样本学习策略，用少量标注图像构建类别原型并完成分类。

Result: 实验表明，SimPhysNet仅使用200张标注图像（约占全部标注数据的5%）即可达到96.06%的分类准确率，性能接近使用完整标注数据训练的传统监督学习算法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效降低了对大规模标注数据的依赖，在保持高分类精度的同时提升了工业应用可行性，为激光焊接过程的智能化与自动化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The laser welding full-penetration is of critical importance, as it constitutes one of the fundamental factors in achieving defect-free welded joints. Accurate prediction of the penetration state is therefore essential for ensuring weld quality. To this end, this paper introduces SimPhysNet, a novel algorithm that achieves high classification accuracy in laser welding penetration prediction using only a limited number of labelled images. This approach effectively overcomes the limitations of supervised learning classification algorithms, which are hindered in industrial applications by their dependence on extensive, high-quality labelled data. The core of SimPhysNet is a unique self-supervised learning paradigm that embeds physical priors into a contrastive learning framework. By incorporating a physics-informed neural network (PINN), the model is guided to extract physically meaningful features of the molten pool and keyhole from a large set of unlabelled data, while three image augmentation tasks further enhance its generalization capabilities. Subsequently, a few-shot learning strategy, based on prototypical networks, enables robust classification by constructing class representations from a minimal set of labelled images. Experimental results demonstrate that SimPhysNet achieves a classification accuracy of 96.06% using only 200 labelled images (approximately 5% of the total labelled dataset), which is comparable to the performance of conventional supervised learning algorithms that utilize the entire labelled dataset. This work presents a new, efficient, and highly accurate method, providing the way for the intelligent automation of laser welding.

</details>


### [78] [$S^{2}$-FracMix: Label-Preserving Self-Saliency Mixup Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25784)
*Khawar Islam,Arif Mahmood,Xin Jin,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 S²-FracMix，一种面向视觉模型的数据增强框架，通过同图像内显著区域重插入与自相似模式混合，提升泛化、鲁棒性与迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 mixup 类数据增强通常依赖跨样本插值，虽然能改善泛化，但会带来较高计算开销，并可能因不同图像语义混合导致标签或语义不一致。论文旨在设计一种结构一致、标签一致且更高效的增强方法。

Method: 方法包含两部分：Self-Saliency Mixup 从同一图像中提取多尺度显著 patch，并将其重新插入该图像的非显著区域，以构造困难但标签一致的样本；FracMix 则使用自适应比例将自相似/分形模式注入显著区域。二者统一为 S²-FracMix，使模型同时学习图像内的分形与非分形结构。

Result: 作者声称在七类基准任务上进行了广泛实验，包括粗粒度与细粒度分类、鲁棒性、校准、目标检测和迁移学习，并取得优于现有方法的 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: S²-FracMix 通过避免跨样本语义干扰、利用单图像内显著性与自相似结构，实现了更有针对性且结构连贯的数据增强，可有效提升深度视觉模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Data augmentation is known to improve generalization of deep visual models. Recent methods favor mixup strategies that generate interpolated samples to improve model performance. However, these techniques not only incur significant computational overhead, they also lead to semantic disruption of augmentation data due to cross-sample mixing. We first propose Self-Saliency ($S^2$) Mixup, which constructs challenging yet label-consistent samples by extracting multi-scale salient patches and reinserting them into non-salient regions of the same image. This promotes scale-invariant feature learning while avoiding cross-sample interference. To further enhance model robustness, we introduce FracMix, a mixing scheme that injects self-similarity patterns into salient regions using adaptive ratios. Collectively, our unified framework, $S^{2}$-FracMix, enables simultaneous learning from fractal and non-fractal structures within a single image, yielding a targeted and structurally coherent augmentation strategy. We theoretically analyze the advantage of our technique, and empirically establish its superiority over the existing methods by achieving state-of-the-art performance in extensive evaluation with seven benchmarks across classification (coarse and fine-grained), robustness, calibration, object detection, and transfer learning tasks. Project page is available at \href{https://fracmix-data-augmentation.github.io/}{fracmix-data-augmentation.github.io}

</details>


### [79] [A cross-process welding penetration status prediction algorithm based on unsupervised domain adaptation in laser and TIG welding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26078)
*Sen Li,Haichao Cui,Chendong Shao,Yaqi Wang,Xinhua Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种结合渐进式源域扩展（GSDE）的无监督域自适应框架，用于焊接熔透状态分类，在同工艺与跨工艺迁移场景中均显著提升准确率并降低新工艺标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督式深度学习在焊接熔透状态分类中应用广泛，但在域偏移条件下性能明显下降，尤其是模型在物理机制差异较大的焊接工艺之间迁移时，例如从以电弧为主导的TIG焊迁移到基于小孔效应的激光焊。研究旨在提升模型跨焊接工艺的泛化能力，并减少新工艺数据重新标注的成本。

Method: 作者提出无监督域自适应（UDA）框架，并集成渐进式源域扩展（GSDE）策略。该方法利用源域有标签数据与目标域无标签数据学习域不变特征，同时保持类别可分性；并通过逐步扩展源域分布来缓解源域与目标域之间的分布差异。实验在专门构建的TIG与激光焊数据集上进行，覆盖同工艺迁移和跨工艺迁移任务。

Result: 在同工艺设置中，该方法在TIGFH上取得90.65%的平均准确率，在LSPS上取得90.72%的平均准确率，分别比监督基线提升35.83%和38.87%。在跨工艺迁移中，TIG到Laser达到80.48%，Laser到TIG达到81.13%，相较基线分别提升43.39%和43.40%。UMAP可视化显示模型能够学习域不变特征，同时维持清晰的类别边界。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解焊接工艺间域偏移导致的性能退化问题，提高了熔透状态识别模型在不同焊接系统中的迁移能力与适用性，并显著降低新焊接工艺部署时的标注成本。

Abstract: Supervised deep learning has been widely used for weld penetration state classification; however, its performance often degrades significantly under domain shift, such as when transferring models between welding processes with distinct physical mechanisms:for instance, from arc-dominated tungsten inert gas (TIG) welding to keyhole-based laser welding. To overcome this limitation, we propose an unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) framework integrated with a gradual source domain expansion (GSDE) strategy. Evaluated on dedicated TIG and laser welding datasets, our approach achieves high accuracy in both same-process and cross-process transfer tasks. Specifically, it attains average accuracies of 90.65% on TIGFH and 90.72% on LSPS in same-process settings, surpassing a supervised baseline by 35.83% and 38.87%, respectively. More notably, in cross-process scenarios, it reaches 80.48% for TIG to Laser and 81.13% for Laser to TIG, improving upon the baseline by 43.39% and 43.40%. UMAP visualizations verify that the model learns domain-invariant features while maintaining discriminative class boundaries. This method considerably lowers the relabeling cost for new welding processes and enhances the versatility of intelligent monitoring across different welding systems.

</details>


### [80] [Shift Variant Image Degradation and Restoration Using Singular Value Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25818)
*Arun D. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于奇异值分解（SVD）的移变运动模糊图像复原框架，通过奇异值能量保留准则选择小奇异值数量，以在抑制噪声放大和保留图像信息之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际成像系统中常出现点扩散函数（PSF）随空间位置变化的移变退化，无法用单一卷积核描述，导致传统移不变图像复原方法难以有效处理，且逆问题通常病态、易放大噪声。

Method: 论文将位置相关PSF表示为移变成像算子，把退化过程建模为线性系统；针对双向线性运动、高斯运动和简谐运动三类一维移变运动PSF，使用SVD分析并近似求逆退化算子；通过累计奇异值能量百分比准则确定小奇异值的贡献，从而实现稳定复原。

Result: 在三幅退化图像上的实验表明，该方法在不同运动模型下能够恢复部分图像细节并减弱模糊伪影，体现出对移变运动模糊复原的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究说明SVD可用于揭示移变退化算子的病态特征，并可结合能量保留准则构造较稳定的图像复原方法；但摘要中未充分说明与先进基线方法的定量比较、噪声鲁棒性和计算复杂度。

Abstract: Shift-variant image degradation is frequently encountered in practical imaging systems where the point spread function (PSF) varies across the image field due to motion, optical aberrations, atmospheric turbulence, or sensor-related effects. Unlike shift-invariant, shift-variant degradation presents significant challenges for image restoration because the degradation process cannot be represented by a single convolution kernel. This paper proposes a singular value decomposition (SVD)-based framework for restoring images degraded by shift-variant motion blur. The proposed approach determines the contribution of small singular values using a singular-value energy retention criterion. Specifically, the number of small singular values is selected based on a specified percentage of cumulative singular-value energy, providing a systematic approach for controlling noise amplification while preserving useful image information. The degradation model is formulated using a position-dependent PSF represented by a shift-variant imaging operator. Three representative one dimensional shift-variant motion PSFs are considered: bidirectional linear motion, Gaussian motion, and simple harmonic motion. The image degradation process is modeled as a linear system, and SVD is employed to analyze and invert the corresponding degradation operator. The singular-value representation provides insight into the ill-conditioned nature of the restoration problem and enables the development of stable inversion techniques. The proposed SVD-based restoration algorithm is applied to three degraded images. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach in recovering image details and reducing blur artifacts under different motion models.

</details>


### [81] [Graph it first! Enabling Reasoning on Long-form Egocentric Videos through Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25842)
*Agnese Taluzzi,Riccardo Santambrogio,Simone Mentasti,Chiara Plizzari,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用自我中心场景图（EgoSGs）将长视频压缩为结构化文本表示，使多模态大语言模型能在有限 token 预算内进行完整视频问答推理，并在 HD-EPIC VQA 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLMs 受输入 token 限制，处理长视频尤其是自我中心视频时必须大量抽帧，导致时间信息、上下文和细粒度状态变化丢失，限制了长程视频推理能力。

Method: 构建 Egocentric Scene Graphs：以时间为锚点，结构化记录视频中的对象、属性、空间关系和交互，并将其转化为紧凑的文本场景图输入 MLLM，从而替代高冗余的视频帧输入。

Result: 在 HD-EPIC VQA 基准上，该方法在多个模型设置中超过强视频基线，达到当前最优表现。

Conclusion: 结构化且时间对齐的场景图表示能有效缓解 MLLM 的上下文长度限制，同时保留关键语义与时间信息，是连接长时自我中心视频理解与现有 MLLM 推理能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Existing multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) face significant challenges in processing long video sequences due to strict input token limitations. As a result, current video understanding approaches, especially in egocentric settings characterized by complex dynamics, frequent state changes, and moving cameras, are forced to massively subsample frames. This leads to severe loss of temporal and contextual information, constraining their ability to perform fine-grained video reasoning. In this work, we introduce a framework for egocentric video question answering (VQA) that overcomes these input constraints through Egocentric Scene Graphs (EgoSGs), i.e., temporally grounded, structured representations that capture objects, attributes, spatial relations, and interactions over time. By representing videos as compact, text-based scene graphs, our method preserves the essential visual and temporal information of the original video in a symbolic form that drastically reduces input length while maintaining semantic richness. Crucially, this enables MLLMs to reason efficiently over entire video sequences within their token budget. On HD-EPIC VQA, our method achieves state-of-the-art results, outperforming strong video-based baselines on multiple models and suggesting that structured, temporally grounded representations like EgoSGs can bridge long-form egocentric video understanding and the context limitations of today's MLLMs.

</details>


### [82] [Naturalness Predicts but Does Not Cause Transferability in Image Encodings of Real-World Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25844)
*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究一维时间序列编码成图像后，其“自然图像相似性”（FID）与冻结视觉骨干迁移识别性能之间的关系。结果表明，跨编码时 FID 与准确率高度相关，但这种相关主要由局部结构而非频谱自然性驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多方法将一维信号转换为图像，以复用在自然图像上预训练的视觉模型，但很少系统分析这些编码图像的视觉属性为何影响迁移性能。本文关注编码图像接近自然图像分布的程度是否能解释冻结视觉骨干的识别准确率。

Method: 作者构建 WorldStream 数据集，包含来自公共 API 的 299 条异构当前值时间序列，并设计九类来源识别任务。实验比较 7 种编码方式和 6 个冻结视觉骨干，使用 FID 衡量编码图像与自然图像的距离；进一步通过可逆编码器调节频谱指数 β，以及相位扰乱实验，区分频谱自然性与局部结构的作用。

Result: 跨编码与骨干实验中，FID 与准确率呈较强负相关（Spearman ρ=-0.72）。但调节频谱指数 β 时，FID 在 β≈2 附近最低，而准确率基本不变且远低于结构化基线（19.2% vs. 73.0%），相关性较弱（Pearson -0.32）。相位扰乱保持功率谱不变但破坏局部结构时，FID 与准确率同步下降（Pearson -0.89）。全量微调也无法弥合差距（27% vs. 67%）。

Conclusion: FID 能预测迁移性能并非因为频谱更接近自然图像会因果性提升准确率，而是因为 FID/Inception 与视觉骨干共同依赖图像中的局部结构。编码性能缺陷主要是结构性的。提出的可逆编码器还能以 72.9 dB 从 8-bit 图像恢复原始信号，使图像兼具近似无损数据记录功能。

Abstract: A common practice converts a one-dimensional signal into an image so that a vision backbone pretrained on natural photographs can be reused for recognition, yet the encoded image is rarely examined. We ask how the visual naturalness of an encoded image relates to its transfer accuracy under a frozen backbone. We build WorldStream, a corpus of 299 heterogeneous current-value series from key-free public APIs (weather, air quality, earthquakes, gold and oil, equities, crypto, foreign exchange, web activity and space weather), with a nine-way source-recognition task over 3143 temporally split windows. Across seven encodings and six frozen backbones, the Frechet distance of an encoding to natural images (FID) predicts its accuracy: Spearman $ρ=-0.72$. Two controlled interventions show this is not causal in the spectrum. Our invertible encoder has a single adjustable part, a spectral exponent $β$ (power $\propto |f|^{-β}$); varying $β$ moves the image toward or away from the natural-image manifold at fixed content. FID is lowest near the natural value $β\approx 2$, but frozen accuracy stays flat and far below the structured baselines (19.2% vs. 73.0%), and FID and accuracy are only weakly related over the sweep (Pearson $-0.32$). A second intervention, phase scrambling, holds the power spectrum exactly fixed while removing local structure; now FID and accuracy fall together (Pearson $-0.89$). The cross-encoding correlation is thus mediated by local structure, not spectral naturalness: FID predicts accuracy because Inception reads the same structure the backbones do. Full fine-tuning does not close the gap (27% vs. 67%), so the deficit is structural. The encoder is exactly invertible, recovering the signal from the 8-bit image at 72.9 dB, so the image doubles as a lossless record of the data.

</details>


### [83] [USS: Unified Spatial-Semantic Prompts for Embodied Visual Tracking with Latent Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25880)
*Yuchen Xie,Xinyu Zhou,Kuangji Zuo,Yanshuo Lu,Fengrui Huang,Boyu Ma,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 USS：一种用于具身视觉跟踪的统一空间-语义提示框架，支持文本、点、框、掩码等目标提示，并结合潜在动态学习提升时序鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身视觉跟踪多依赖语言描述指定目标，但在杂乱场景中多个物体可能满足同一语义描述，导致目标定位歧义，尤其难以维持实例级目标身份。

Method: 将目标指示从纯文本扩展为统一空间-语义提示；使用模态专属编码器处理文本、点、边界框和掩码提示，通过混合注意力将提示 token 与视觉特征融合，再解码为以自我视角表示的路径点；同时引入潜在世界模型，通过自监督对齐预测未来表征以增强时间一致性。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，显式空间提示相比纯文本提示具有更高成功率，尤其在相似干扰物和长时程跟踪场景中优势明显；仿真基准上，USS 在非 MLLM 方法中达到最优，并以更快推理速度取得接近近期 MLLM 方法的竞争性结果。

Conclusion: 空间-语义统一提示能够为具身视觉跟踪提供更精确、灵活的目标指示方式；结合潜在动态学习后，USS 在目标消歧、实例保持和长时序跟踪方面表现更稳健。

Abstract: Embodied Visual Tracking (EVT) requires an agent to continuously follow a specified target while actively moving through dynamic environments. However, prevailing EVT paradigms predominantly rely on language-based target indication. While language is expressive and convenient, cluttered scenes often contain multiple objects that satisfy the same semantic description, leading to ambiguous target grounding. We therefore propose a paradigm shift, reframing target indication in EVT from text-only specification to unified spatial-semantic prompting. Based on this paradigm, we introduce Unified Spatial-Semantic Prompts for Embodied Visual Tracking with Latent Dynamics Learning, USS, an end-to-end embodied tracking framework that supports text, point, bounding box, and mask prompts within a unified architecture. USS encodes heterogeneous prompts with modality-specific encoders, fuses prompt tokens with visual features through hybrid attention, and decodes compact prompt-conditioned representations into egocentric waypoints. To further improve temporal robustness, USS incorporates a latent world model that predicts future representations through self-supervised alignment. Real-robot experiments demonstrate that explicit spatial target cues yield higher success rates than text-only prompts, particularly in scenarios involving similar distractors and longer-horizon tracking where maintaining instance-level target identity is critical. In the simulation benchmark, USS also achieves state-of-the-art performance among non-MLLM-based methods and competitive results against recent MLLM-based approaches with faster inference speed. Our findings reveal that spatial-semantic prompting provides a more precise and flexible target indication interface for embodied visual tracking. Project site: https://arescheah.github.io/uss-project-page/.

</details>


### [84] [SurgAtlas: A Large-Scale Surgical Video-Language Dataset with 2,391 Hours of Open and Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25905)
*Filippos Bellos,Andre S. Gala-Garza,Miaowei Wang,Alyssa M. Hardin,Ahmad M. Hider,Yayuan Li,Jing Bi,Susan Liang,Chenliang Xu,Donald S. Likosky,Jason J. Corso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SurgAtlas 是一个大规模外科视频-语言数据集，包含 15,291 个公开 YouTube 手术视频、2,391 小时内容，覆盖 18 个外科专科和 5,000 多类术式，并支持开放手术与微创手术的视频理解基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外科视频-语言数据集规模较小、术式覆盖有限，尤其缺乏大规模开放手术数据与标准化开放手术理解基准，难以支撑具备广泛泛化能力的外科多模态基础模型训练与评测。

Method: 作者构建了 SurgAtlas 数据集，整合公开视频资源，设计层级化分类体系和多样化标注模式，包括片段级字幕、步骤/阶段描述、视频级手术描述以及推理型问答；标注通过自动化多层流程、LLM 增强和分阶段 VQA 生成，并加入显式 groundedness 验证；随后使用两阶段“字幕训练—指令微调”流程微调 Qwen3-VL-8B。

Result: SurgAtlas 在规模、开放手术覆盖和标注多样性方面超过既有外科视频-语言数据集；基于该数据集微调的模型在多个外科基准任务上取得有竞争力或最先进表现，包括阶段识别、三元组检测和推理问答。

Conclusion: SurgAtlas 为外科视频理解提供了大规模、公开、多模态且临床语义更丰富的数据基础，可促进外科基础模型的大规模预训练、跨术式泛化能力提升和下一代多模态手术 AI 系统发展。

Abstract: We introduce SurgAtlas, the largest surgical video-language dataset to date, comprising 15,291 videos (2,391 hours) spanning 18 surgical specialties and over 5,000 procedure types, sourced entirely from publicly available YouTube content. SurgAtlas is also the first surgical video-language dataset to include open surgery at scale, with 6,182 open procedure videos alongside over 9,000 minimally invasive recordings, and the first to establish standardized benchmarks for open-surgery video understanding. We additionally provide an expert-validated subset with verified visual question-answer pairs across diverse open and minimally invasive procedures, serving as a clinically grounded benchmark for surgical reasoning. Compared with existing surgical video-language datasets, SurgAtlas provides one of the most diverse annotation schemas, combining segment-level captions, step- and phase-level descriptions, video-level surgical descriptions, and reasoning-oriented question-answer pairs organized within a hierarchical taxonomy. These annotations are constructed through an automated multi-tier pipeline with LLM-based enrichment and a staged VQA generation framework with explicit groundedness verification. The scale and diversity of SurgAtlas enable training surgical foundation models with broad procedural coverage: we finetune Qwen3-VL-8B through a two-stage captioning-then-instruction pipeline and achieve competitive or state-of-the-art results on multiple established surgical benchmarks, including phase recognition, triplet detection, and reasoning question answering. More broadly, SurgAtlas provides a large native public video corpus that can support future large-scale pretraining of multimodal surgical AI systems and contribute to the development of next-generation foundation models for surgery.

</details>


### [85] [OracleAnalyser: Analysing Implicit Semantics of Oracle Bone Scripts through MLLMs with Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25906)
*Zijia Song,Yelin Wang,Zhengyi Ma,Zitong Yu,Tianheng Wang,Jiahuan Zhang,Taorui Wang,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OracleAnalyser，一个面向甲骨文分析的视觉语言推理框架，通过多阶段后训练与新的偏好优化算法 SFPO 提升模型分析能力，并发布数据集与评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有甲骨文智能研究主要集中在识别任务，缺乏对甲骨文字形、释读与语义推理等分析能力的系统研究和评测，因此需要面向分析任务的新框架、数据集和基准。

Method: 以 Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct 为基础模型，进行多阶段后训练；提出 Stable Focal Preference Optimization（SFPO）以适配甲骨文数据特点；构建甲骨文推理数据集、偏好数据集及新的分析能力 benchmark。

Result: 实验显示 OracleAnalyser 在甲骨文分析任务上表现优越，仅使用 3B 参数即可取得显著结果，并超过若干更大规模模型。

Conclusion: 后训练和任务定制化偏好优化能够有效增强小规模视觉语言模型在甲骨文分析中的推理能力，OracleAnalyser 为甲骨文研究从识别走向分析提供了新的数据、方法和评测基础。

Abstract: With the advancement of artificial intelligence, research on oracle bone scripts has entered a new era. However, existing methods and benchmarks remain largely confined to recognition tasks, overlooking the equally crucial aspect of oracle bone analysis. To address this gap, we propose OracleAnalyser, a reasoning framework for oracle bone analysis based on post-training techniques. Specifically, we fine-tune Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct through multiple post-training stages and introduce a new preference optimization algorithm, Stable Focal Preference Optimization (SFPO), tailored to the characteristics of oracle bone datasets. In addition, we release both an oracle bone reasoning dataset and an oracle bone preference dataset, and further construct a new benchmark to evaluate models' analytical capabilities for oracle bone scripts. Extensive experiments validate the superior analytical performance of OracleAnalyser, which achieves remarkable results with only 3B parameters, surpassing models with substantially larger scales.

</details>


### [86] [In-context Region-based Drag: Drag Any Region to Any Shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25907)
*Jiacheng Sui,Tianyu Hao,Bingjie Gao,Li Niu,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ICRDrag，一种基于区域的扩散模型拖拽编辑方法，通过源图、源区域掩码和目标区域掩码生成目标编辑图像，并显著提升编辑精度与视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 drag-style 图像编辑方法多依赖点级拖拽，但点级控制存在天然歧义，难以准确表达复杂区域形变或移动需求。因此，论文转向更明确、更可控的区域级拖拽编辑。

Method: 作者在上下文学习框架下构建 ICRDrag，输入源图像、源区域 mask 和目标区域 mask，输出拖拽后的目标图像。方法引入两种注意力正则化：图像-mask 注意力一致性，用于约束目标区域在图像与掩码模态中关注相似源区域；源-目标注意力对应，用于增强源区域与目标区域之间的双向对应关系。同时构建 Paired Region Dataset（PRD）以支持区域拖拽训练与评测。

Result: 大量实验表明，ICRDrag 在定量指标和用户研究中均显著优于已有方法，表现出更高的区域编辑准确性和更好的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 该工作证明区域级拖拽比点级拖拽更适合减少编辑歧义，并通过上下文学习、注意力正则和大规模配对区域数据集，有效提升扩散模型在可控图像编辑中的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown promise in drag-style editing. Previous works mainly focus on point-based drag, which is inherently ambiguous. This paper focuses on region-based drag and introduces a novel In-Context Region-based Drag (ICRDrag) method. Under the in-context learning framework, ICRDrag consumes a source image, a source region mask, and a target region mask, producing the target dragged image. Built upon the basic in-context learning model, we introduce two novel attention regularization: 1) image-mask attention consistency to ensure that a target region attends to similar source regions for image and mask modalities; 2) source-target attention correspondence to ensure the mutual correspondence between source and target regions. To facilitate region-based drag, we also construct Paired Region Dataset (PRD), a large-scale dataset with paired masks and images. Extensive experiments show that ICRDrag significantly outperforms existing methods in both quantitative metrics and user studies, achieving superior editing accuracy and visual fidelity. The dataset, code, and model are available at https://github.com/bcmi/ICRDrag-Region-Drag-Editing.

</details>


### [87] [FunPiQ: A New Benchmark for Pixel-Level Quality Assessment in Fundus Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25915)
*Pengwei Wang,José Morano,Virginia Mares,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FunPiQ，这是首个提供像素级质量标注的眼底图像质量评估基准，并提出基于解剖结构可见性的可解释 FIQA 方法 EFIQA-CP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 彩色眼底摄影广泛用于大规模筛查，但图像易受退化影响；现有 FIQA 数据集多为图像级标签，标准不一致，且无法量化局部退化，限制了可信和可解释评估。

Method: 作者将像素级 FIQA 建模为基于解剖结构可见性的任务无关评估，构建 FunPiQ 像素级标注基准；提出 EFIQA-CP，利用解剖可见性生成质量伪标签，并通过非负正-未标注学习训练 CNN。

Result: 在分类方法结合事后解释、异常检测方法和可解释设计方法之间进行广泛比较，结果显示可解释设计方法整体更优，其中 EFIQA-CP 表现最佳。

Conclusion: 像素级、基于解剖可见性的 FIQA 更具任务无关性与可解释性；FunPiQ 为局部质量评估提供了基准，EFIQA-CP 为可信眼底图像质量评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Color fundus photography (CFP) is the most common ophthalmic imaging modality for large-scale screening. However, it is highly susceptible to degradations, making robust fundus image quality assessment (FIQA) crucial. The criteria for what constitutes high-quality at the image level vary across clinical tasks, making FIQA dependent on expert knowledge. This motivated the development of automated methods and datasets. While existing datasets aim to standardize image-level quality, their criteria often differ. Furthermore, image-level labels preclude the quantitative evaluation of localized degradations, which is essential for trustworthy FIQA. We argue that pixel-level FIQA based on anatomical visibility represents a more task-agnostic, explainable approach. In this work, we introduce FunPiQ, the first FIQA benchmark to provide pixel-level quality annotations. In addition, we propose EFIQA-CP, an explainable-by-design (EBD) method that uses quality pseudo-labels based on anatomical visibility to train a CNN via Non-Negative Positive-Unlabeled learning. Extensive evaluations of classification methods with post-hoc explanations, anomaly detection methods, and EBD methods demonstrate the superior performance of the last and, particularly, of EFIQA-CP.

</details>


### [88] [A Benchmark for Heterogeneous Stereo Deblurring with Physically- and Epipolar-constrained Cross Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25962)
*Hoju Shin,Jiah Kim,Seung-Wook Kim,Seowon Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出异构双目去模糊框架，包含真实手机双目采集构建的HSD数据集与轻量PECA跨注意力模块，用物理和极线约束提升跨视图特征融合，在多类基线网络上均带来性能与效率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代具备双目能力的智能手机可采集沉浸式XR内容，但不同相机模组的硬件差异会导致左右视图出现严重的非对称模糊。现有方法与基准多假设同质双目系统，难以显式处理这种异构退化问题，因此需要专门的数据集和方法。

Method: 作者构建HSD数据集，通过真实智能手机双目视频/图像的多帧整合获得训练与评测数据；提出PECA模块，将跨视图匹配限制在由极线几何和光学推导的视差上界确定的搜索窗口内，并结合置信度加权注意力与残差融合：匹配可靠区域利用跨视图引导去模糊，遮挡或不可靠区域退化为自去模糊。该模块轻量且与架构无关，可插入CNN、Transformer和NAFNet等基线。

Result: 在HSD数据集上的大量实验显示，加入PECA后，不同类型基线模型的恢复性能均得到稳定提升，同时保持较好的计算效率，说明物理有效的视差约束能提高跨视图融合的可靠性与效率。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统针对智能手机异构双目非对称模糊问题提出数据集与方法。PECA通过物理/极线约束和置信度机制，在可靠匹配与遮挡退化之间取得平衡，是一种高效、通用的异构双目去模糊模块。

Abstract: Modern stereo-capable smartphones enable immersive XR content capture. However, hardware heterogeneity across camera modules often causes severe asymmetric blur artifacts. Existing methods and benchmarks largely assume homogeneous stereo setups and therefore do not explicitly address such asymmetric degradation. To bridge this gap, we present a dedicated framework for heterogeneous stereo deblurring. First, we introduce the heterogeneous stereo deblurring (HSD) dataset, constructed from real smartphone stereo captures via multi-frame integration. Second, we propose physically- and epipolar-constrained cross attention (PECA), a lightweight module that restricts cross-view matching to an epipolar search window bounded by a optics-derived disparity upper bound. By enforcing physically valid disparity constraints, PECA enables efficient and reliable cross-view feature fusion. Moreover, our confidence-weighted attention with residual fusion emphasizes cross-guided deblurring when correspondences are reliable, while naturally falling back to self-deblurring in occluded or unreliable regions. PECA is architecture-agnostic and consistently improves CNN-, Transformer-, and NAFNet-based baselines. Extensive experiments on HSD show that PECA-enhanced models achieve improved restoration performance with favorable efficiency.

</details>


### [89] [Taxonomy-aware deep learning for hierarchical marine species classification in underwater imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25989)
*Dan Zimmerman,Dimitris A. Pados,George Sklivanitis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向海洋物种水下图像自动分类的分类学感知深度学习框架，通过将训练损失与推理规则对齐到生物分类层级，在 FathomNet 2025 数据集上取得接近第一名的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋生物多样性监测需要可扩展的自动识别方法，但现有方法面临跨采集平台的域偏移、近缘物种细粒度相似、以及标注粒度不一致（只能标到属或更高分类等级）等挑战。

Method: 方法结合分类学加权损失、最小风险贝叶斯推理、多尺度特征编码，以及各分类等级独立的分类头，使模型训练目标和预测决策都显式利用生物分类层级结构。

Result: 在包含 7 个分类等级、79 个海洋类别的 FathomNet 2025 数据集上，模型平均分类学距离为 1.581，距离第一名方案的 1.535 不到 3%；主要提升来自与评价指标一致的推理策略，以及简单解耦组件在分布偏移下更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 分类学层级信息不仅应体现在损失函数中，也应体现在推理决策中；相比复杂的学习依赖结构，简洁、解耦且与指标对齐的设计在存在域偏移和不完整标注的海洋物种识别任务中更稳健。

Abstract: Automated classification of marine species from underwater imagery is essential for scalable ocean biodiversity monitoring and conservation policy. Existing approaches struggle with severe domain shift across collection platforms, fine-grained visual similarity between closely related species, and uneven annotation granularity, where many specimens can only be identified to genus or a coarser taxonomic rank. We present a taxonomy-aware deep learning framework that aligns both the training loss and the inference rule with the hierarchical structure of biological classification, combining a taxonomy-weighted loss, minimum-risk Bayesian inference, multi-scale feature encoding, and independent per-rank classification heads. Evaluated on the FathomNet 2025 dataset1 (79 marine classes across seven taxonomic ranks), the system achieves a mean taxonomic distance of 1.581, within 3% of the 1st-place solution (1.535), with the largest gains from metric-aligned inference and simple, decoupled components that generalize better than learned dependencies under distribution shift.

</details>


### [90] [From Sparse and Imperfect 2D Anchors to Consistent 3D Gaussian Street Scenes: Support-Aware Appearance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26007)
*Long Cao,Zhongquan Wang,Jie Li,Yuhan Chen,Kefei Qian,Xiangfei Huang,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向3D Gaussian场景外观烘焙的教师相对残差蒸馏方法，在保持固定几何与标准光栅化部署的同时，实现更一致、更少伪影的目标外观编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像先验可为3D Gaussian街景合成目标条件，但逐视角独立编辑会导致跨视角不一致；直接拟合容易传播视角特异噪声；现有方法难以同时处理稀疏且不完美的教师锚点以及标准光栅器推理部署需求。

Method: 方法以教师锚点与原始渲染之间的外观残差为核心，构建用于频率分解、置信度估计和基元级提升的结构化空间；通过渲染器空间匹配提供直接优化信号，并使用支持感知的Gaussian空间聚合正则化基元分配；采用置信度门控的由粗到细优化，接纳有支撑的细节、抑制无支撑噪声，最终将残差烘焙到固定几何的球谐系数中，推理时丢弃教师与辅助模块。

Result: 在Waymo街景资产、Tanks and Temples场景以及多种目标条件上的评估表明，该方法相比基于编辑的基线，在目标对齐、内容保持、伪影抑制和跨视角一致性之间取得了更好的综合平衡；消融实验验证了主要组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为3D Gaussian场景的外观条件迁移/编辑提供了实用方案：利用教师相对残差和置信度控制实现可靠外观烘焙，并在不增加推理复杂度的情况下提升一致性与部署友好性。

Abstract: Image priors can synthesize target conditions for 3D Gaussian street scenes, but independently edited views do not define a coherent 3D target. Direct fitting can propagate view-specific noise, while existing pipelines do not jointly handle imperfect sparse anchors and standard-rasterizer deployment. To address this gap, teacher-relative appearance residual distillation is introduced for appearance baking. A structured space for frequency decomposition, confidence estimation, and primitive-level lifting is formed by residuals between teacher anchors and original renders. The direct optimization signal is supplied by renderer-space matching, while primitive assignment is regularized by support-aware Gaussian-space aggregation. Supported detail is admitted and unsupported noise is suppressed through confidence-gated coarse-to-fine optimization, after which all residuals are baked into fixed-geometry spherical-harmonic coefficients. The teacher and auxiliary training modules are discarded at inference. Evaluation across Waymo street assets, Tanks and Temples scenes, and multiple target conditions shows a favorable overall balance of target alignment, content preservation, artifact suppression, and cross-view consistency over editing-based baselines. Ablations confirm the effectiveness of the main components. Code will be released at https://github.com/Cagares/Baking-for-3D-Gaussian.

</details>


### [91] [MIMFlow: Integrating Masked Image Modeling with Normalizing Flows for End-to-End Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26016)
*Yang Chen,Xiaowei Xu,Shuai Wang,Xinwen Zhang,Qiushi Guo,Tiezheng Ge,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIMFlow 将掩码图像建模与归一化流统一到端到端生成框架中，通过语义潜变量与像素重建解耦，缓解 NF 在低层细节上的容量浪费，并在 ImageNet 256×256 上取得较好表示与生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化流具备精确密度估计和采样能力，但严格可逆性使其容易将容量消耗在低层像素细节上，削弱对高层语义结构的建模；同时，MIM 虽在表示学习中有效，但与生成模型的结合通常较模块化、割裂。

Method: 提出 MIMFlow：使用 VAE 编码器从掩码图像中推断语义潜变量，将生成任务解耦为低频语义流形建模与高频像素合成；NF 负责建模简化后的语义潜空间，专门的解码器负责重建与合成高频细节，并联合优化语义、重建和流模型目标。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 上，MIMFlow-L 达到 71.3% 线性探测准确率和 2.50 FID；仅使用 128 个 token，比标准模型少 50%，相较同规模 NF 基线获得 32.8% 性能提升。

Conclusion: MIMFlow 通过将 MIM 的语义抽象能力与 NF 的精确生成能力结合，有效缓解 NF 的容量瓶颈，使模型更关注全局结构一致性而非冗余像素噪声，展示了统一表示学习与生成建模的潜力。

Abstract: Normalizing Flows (NFs) are powerful generative models capable of exact density estimation and sampling. However, their strict invertibility often forces the model to exhaust its capacity on low-level pixel details, hindering the capture of high-level semantic structures. While Masked Image Modeling (MIM) has excelled in representation learning, its integration into generative pipelines has remained largely modular and disjointed. In this paper, we propose MIMFlow, a unified end-to-end framework that jointly optimizes latent semantics, pixel reconstruction, and generative flow. By employing a VAE encoder to infer semantic latent from masked images, MIMFlow achieves a principled decoupling of the generative task: the Normalizing Flow focuses on modeling a simplified, low-frequency semantic manifold, while a specialized decoder handles high-frequency synthesis. This design effectively resolves the inherent capacity bottleneck of NFs, allowing the model to prioritize global structural coherence over redundant noise. Empirical results on ImageNet 256$\times$256 show that MIMFlow-L reaches 71.3\% linear probing accuracy and an FID of 2.50. Despite using only 128 tokens (50\% fewer than standard models), it yields a 32.8\% performance gain over similar-scale NF baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/MCG-NJU/MIMFlow.

</details>


### [92] [DomainShuttle: Freeform Open Domain Subject-driven Text-to-video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26058)
*Nan Chen,Yiyang Cai,Rongchang Xie,Junwen Pan,Cheng Chen,Weinan Jia,Zhuowei Chen,Wen Zhou,Zhenbang Sun,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DomainShuttle 面向开放域主体驱动文本到视频生成，在主体保真与跨域可编辑性之间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 S2V 方法多强调域内主体特征保持，导致在跨域场景下对新风格、语义组合和域属性变化的适应性不足。论文希望构建一种可在域内与跨域场景间灵活切换的方法，同时保持主体身份特征并支持文本驱动的灵活生成。

Method: 提出 DomainShuttle。核心包括：Domain-MoT，用于解耦视频特征与参考图像特征，并通过 domain-aware AdaLN 进行域特定建模；Video-Reference DualRoPE，将参考图像 token 与视频 token 放入不同 RoPE 空间，以实现更精确的主体级空间建模；Cross-Pair Consistent Loss，用于学习不受无关属性干扰的主体内在特征。

Result: 大量实验表明，DomainShuttle 相比已有方法在多种开放域应用场景中取得显著性能提升，同时表现出较高的主体保真度和生成灵活性。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种兼顾域内保真与跨域可编辑性的开放域视频个性化框架，为主体驱动文本到视频生成提供了更强的泛化与适配能力。

Abstract: Open domain subject-driven text-to-video (S2V) generation has drawn significant interest in academia and industry. Open domain S2V mainly involves two scenarios: in-domain, which requires retaining the reference subject features as much as possible, and cross-domain, which preserves the intrinsic features of the subject while allowing subject-irrelevant properties to vary flexibly according to the text prompt. Existing methods primarily focus on maximizing subject fidelity in in-domain scenarios, which limits their editability and adaptability in cross-domain scenarios, such as novel styles, semantic combinations, or domain attributes. In this study, we propose that an ideal S2V method should flexibly shuttle between different domains, achieving strong performance in both in-domain and cross-domain scenarios. To this end, we propose DomainShuttle, which could achieve high fidelity and generative flexibility for open domain video personalization. Specifically, we introduce Domain-MoT, which decouples videos and reference features and introduces the domain-aware AdaLN for domain-specific modeling of reference images. We then introduce the Video-Reference DualRoPE scheme, which places reference image tokens and video tokens in separate RoPE spaces to enable precise subject-level spatial modeling, and Cross-Pair Consistent Loss, which aims to extract intrinsic subject features unaffected by irrelevant features. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DomainShuttle achieves significant performance improvements over existing methods, exhibiting high subject fidelity and generative flexibility across diverse open domain application scenarios.

</details>


### [93] [MVTrack4Gen: Multi-View Point Tracking as Geometric Supervision for 4D Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26087)
*JoungBin Lee,Jaewoo Jung,Jongmin Lee,Tongmin Kim,Hyunsung Kim,Takuya Narihira,Kazumi Fukuda,Jahyeok Koo,Jisang Han,Yuki Mitsufuji,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVTrack4Gen 通过在新视角视频扩散模型训练中加入多视角点跟踪监督，增强跨视角与跨时间的对应关系，从而提升几何一致性和运动保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目参考视频的新视角视频合成需要同时保持几何一致性和参考视频中的运动规律。显式 3D 表示方法受限于重建模块，尤其难以准确处理动态物体；仅依赖相机条件的方法视觉质量较高，但容易出现几何与运动不一致。因此需要一种不依赖显式重建、同时能强化运动与几何约束的训练方式。

Method: 论文提出 MVTrack4Gen，一个面向相机条件新视角视频扩散模型的运动感知训练框架。作者发现扩散模型中特定注意力层蕴含强对应关系线索：查询特征会关注跨视角、跨时间的几何对应位置。基于此，将这些注意力特征送入辅助的多视角点跟踪头，并将扩散生成目标与点跟踪目标联合训练，以显式强化运动感知的对应关系。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，该方法提升了现有模型对参考视角运动的跟随能力和跨视角几何一致性，取得了领先的几何一致性表现，并保持有竞争力的相机准确性。

Conclusion: MVTrack4Gen 证明了多视角点跟踪可以作为有效的辅助监督信号，用于改进仅相机条件的新视角视频扩散模型，使其在动态场景中更好地兼顾视觉质量、运动一致性与几何一致性。

Abstract: Synthesizing a novel-view video from a monocular reference video along a target camera trajectory requires both geometric consistency and motion fidelity with respect to the reference video. Existing methods based on explicit 3D representations are limited by the accuracy of off-the-shelf reconstruction modules, which often produce inaccurate geometry for dynamic objects in monocular videos. In contrast, camera-conditioning-only methods can achieve high visual quality but often struggle to preserve geometric and motion consistency. In this work, we introduce MVTrack4Gen (Multi-View point Tracking for Novel-View Generation), a motion-aware training framework that leverages multi-view point tracking as an additional geometric and motion supervision signal for camera-conditioning-only novel-view video diffusion models. Our key finding is that specific attention layers encode strong correspondence cues, where query features attend to key features at geometrically corresponding locations across views and over time, and the misalignment of these correspondences causes motion inconsistency. Based on this observation, we route these features into an auxiliary multi-view tracking head and jointly train the diffusion model with a point-tracking objective. By explicitly strengthening these motion-aware correspondences, MVTrack4Gen improves existing models to better follow the motion in the reference view and maintain cross-view geometric consistency. Across diverse benchmarks, our method achieves state-of-the-art geometric consistency and competitive camera accuracy.

</details>


### [94] [TryOnCrafter: Unleashing Camera Trajectories for Realistic Video Virtual Try-on via a Renderable 4D Try-on Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26092)
*Hao Sun,Hao Yan,Mengting Chen,Quanjian Song,Yu Li,Juan Cao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Bo Zheng,Sheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Camera-controllable Video Virtual Try-on（CaM-VVT）新任务，并给出首个统一的 DiT 框架 TryOnCrafter，用可渲染 4D 试衣代理约束任意相机轨迹下的视频虚拟试衣生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试衣方法依赖源视频相机轨迹，无法支持用户自由控制视角；任意相机运动下还需要同时解决服装纹理补全、人体非刚性运动与背景结构的严格同步问题。

Method: 方法构建 Renderable 4D Try-on Proxy：将高质量 2D 试衣先验蒸馏到基于 3D Gaussian Splatting 的着装人体化身中，再通过 SMPL-X 序列驱动动画，并与重建的背景点云进行尺度/度量对齐。随后使用 Proxy-Anchored Video DiT，将该 4D 代理作为几何锚点，引导生成符合指定相机轨迹、人体形变与背景结构的视频。

Result: 摘要声称该框架可生成受相机轨迹严格约束、具有真实感且形变合理的视频，并支持人体重定位、bullet time 效果和 360 度环绕观看等应用。

Conclusion: TryOnCrafter 将 VVT 从被动跟随源相机扩展到可控相机视角生成，通过显式 4D 几何代理增强结构一致性、纹理密度和运动完整性，为交互式视频虚拟试衣提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: While Video Virtual Try-on (VVT) has achieved remarkable progress in synthesizing realistic garment overlays on dynamic subjects, existing paradigms remains fundamentally constrained by a passive dependency on source camera trajectories, failing to accommodate the requisite interactive freedom for omnidirectional viewpoint exploration. To address this limitation, we define a pioneering research frontier: Camera-controllable Video Virtual Try-on (CaM-VVT). Unlike conventional VVT, CaM-VVT not only necessitates viewpoint-agnostic texture hallucination but also strict structural synchronization between non-rigid human dynamics and background contexts under arbitrary, unconstrained camera movements. To tackle these challenges, we present TryOnCrafter, the first unified DiT-based framework specifically architected for the CaM-VVT task. Departing from implicit pixel-space manipulation, we introduce a Renderable 4D Try-on Proxy that explicitly decouples the human subject from the environment. This is achieved by distilling high-fidelity 2D try-on priors into a clothed 3DGS-based avatar, which is subsequently animated via SMPL-X sequences and metric-aligned into a reconstructed background point cloud. This proxy establishes a robust structural foundation with superior texture density and motion integrity. Our Proxy-Anchored Video DiT leverages this robust structural foundation as a primary geometric anchor, ensuring that the synthesized photorealistic videos are strictly constrained by prescribed trajectories and physically plausible deformations. Benefiting from the inherent editability of the 4D proxy, TryOnCrafter facilitates diverse downstream applications, including human relocalization, ``bullet time'' effects, and $360$-degree orbital viewing.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Graph-Based Phonetic Error Correction of Noisy ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24889)
*Pratik Rakesh Singh,Mohammadi Zaki,Aneesh Mukkamala,Pankaj Wasnik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 G-SPIN：一种结合音系图建模、MLM 上下文评分与指令微调 LLM 重排序的结构化 ASR 纠错框架，用于修正语义关键词上的残余识别错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ASR 虽整体词错率较低，但在命名实体、否定词、情感词等语义关键 token 上仍会产生影响较大的词汇错误；这些错误往往源于发音相似性，具有结构化特征，单纯的 token 级或无约束生成式纠错不足以可靠解决。

Method: G-SPIN 首先使用 GNN 构建被标记 token 的声学可行候选邻域，将搜索空间限制在发音相近替代项中；随后用 MLM 进行局部上下文打分；最后由指令微调 LLM 在紧凑候选集上进行上下文感知重排序。该方法将音系推理与语义选择解耦，并仅在推理阶段运行。

Result: 摘要声称该框架在避免无约束生成的同时提升纠错准确率，并具备轻量、模块化、推理时可用等特点；但未提供具体实验数据、数据集、对比基线或消融结果。

Conclusion: 该工作强调结构化音系约束与上下文语言理解的结合，可作为 ASR 后处理纠错的一种可解释、可控方案；其有效性仍需通过定量实验与关键模块消融进一步验证。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems, despite low overall word error rates, produce residual lexical errors that disproportionately affect semantically critical tokens such as named entities, negations, and sentiment-bearing words. These errors are often structured, arising from phonetic similarity rather than random noise, making naive token-level correction insufficient. We propose a structured ASR correction framework, that we call G-SPIN, that combines phonetic graph modeling with contextual language understanding. A graph neural network (GNN) first constructs acoustically plausible candidate neighborhoods for flagged tokens, explicitly restricting the correction search space to phonetic alternatives. A masked language model (MLM) then provides local contextual scoring, and an instruction-tuned large language model (LLM) performs final context-aware re-ranking over this compact candidate set. By decoupling structured phonetic reasoning from contextual semantic selection, our method avoids unconstrained generation while improving correction accuracy. The framework is lightweight, modular, and operates entirely at inference time.

</details>


### [96] [Small edits, large models: How Wikipedia advocacy shapes LLM values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24890)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Maria Navas,Alexa Gnauck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究少量志愿者通过编辑维基百科内容，是否能够影响大语言模型在动物福利相关议题上的输出行为；结果显示，这类编辑在模型训练数据归因中具有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维基百科广泛出现在主流语言模型训练集中，且通常比普通网页文本具有更高权重。作者希望检验小规模、有组织的主题性编辑活动是否能通过维基百科这一高影响力数据源，改变模型对特定议题的表征与回答倾向。

Method: 研究分析 Pro-Animal Wikipedians 在115个页面上的125次动物福利相关编辑。作者使用梯度数据归因方法 Bergson/MAGIC、TrackStar 检索归因和反事实影响估计，在 Llama 3.1 8B 与 Llama-3.2-1B 上评估这些编辑对动物福利查询与一般公司查询的影响，并通过留子集验证和针对 PAW 内容/控制内容的微调实验进行验证。

Result: 在动物福利查询中，PAW 编辑段落占最高归因文档的68%，显著高于随机水平；MAGIC 实验中，五个随机训练顺序种子下动物福利查询的前10个最有影响文档全部为 PAW 编辑。PAW 对动物福利查询的平均影响显著高于控制内容，效应量是一般查询的6到30倍。微调实验显示，PAW 内容训练模型将动物福利文本困惑度从12.4降至8.4，控制内容训练模型则主要改善控制文本表现。

Conclusion: 小规模、协调性的维基百科编辑活动可以被语言模型训练过程吸收，并对模型在相应主题上的行为产生可测量、主题特异性的影响，说明高权重公共知识库中的编辑可能对 AI 系统输出具有放大效应。

Abstract: Can a small group of volunteers shape how AI systems discuss animal welfare, just by editing Wikipedia? We show that they can. Wikipedia appears in nearly every major language model training dataset and is weighted more heavily than web-crawled text. The Pro-Animal Wikipedians (PAW), a group of advocates who add sourced animal welfare content to relevant articles, have made 125 edits across 115 pages. Using gradient-based data attribution (Bergson; MAGIC), we traced how these edits influence language model behavior. TrackStar retrieval attribution on Llama 3.1 8B found that PAW-edited sections made up 68 percent of the highest-attributed documents for animal welfare queries (p < 0.0001) but only 52 percent for unrelated queries about the same companies (p = 0.53): the model links PAW content specifically to animal welfare topics, not to the entities in general. MAGIC counterfactual influence estimation on Llama-3.2-1B, run across five random training-order seeds, gave the same picture even more sharply: in every seed, the top-10 most influential documents on animal welfare queries were all PAW edits (10 of 10, 5 of 5 seeds), while on general queries the same top-10 sat at chance (4 to 6 of 10). Mean PAW influence exceeded mean control influence on animal welfare queries with p < 0.0001 in every seed, an effect 6 to 30 times larger than on general queries. Leave-subset-out validation gave Spearman rho = 1.00 for all 10 runs. When we fine-tuned separate models on PAW content versus control content, each model performed better specifically on the type of text it was trained on: the PAW-trained model cut perplexity on animal welfare text from 12.4 to 8.4, while the control-trained model cut perplexity on control text from 16.1 to 11.4. A small, coordinated Wikipedia editing campaign therefore measurably shapes how language models handle the topics those edits address.

</details>


### [97] [AgentOdyssey: Open-Ended Long-Horizon Text Game Generation for Test-Time Continual Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24893)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Zehao Wen,Alvin Zhang,Andrew Wang,Jianwen Xie,Daniel Khashabi,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentOdyssey，用于评估测试时持续学习智能体在开放式长程文本游戏中的探索、知识获取、记忆与规划能力，并发现现有智能体显著落后于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习评估通常假设测试阶段不发生学习，难以衡量智能体在真实部署中边交互、边学习、边推理的能力。作者希望评估智能体在长程开放环境中有效探索、获取世界知识与技能、保留情节经验并进行长期规划的关键能力。

Method: 作者构建了 AgentOdyssey 评估框架，可程序化生成包含丰富实体、世界动态和长程任务的开放式文本游戏。同时提出多维评估方法，不仅衡量游戏进展，还诊断世界知识获取、情节记忆、对象与动作探索、动作多样性以及模型成本，并在该环境中评测多类智能体范式。

Result: 实验显示，现有智能体在关键能力上存在明显瓶颈，且其有效长程能力受到多种因素影响。更强的基础模型可提升性能，但最佳智能体仍远低于人类水平。短期记忆机制对多种智能体范式均有帮助，是测试时训练的重要组成部分。

Conclusion: AgentOdyssey 为测试时持续学习智能体提供了更贴近真实部署的长程开放评估基准。结果表明当前智能体距离人类水平仍有较大差距，未来需要在探索、记忆、知识获取和长程规划等方面进一步改进。

Abstract: For agents to learn continuously from interaction with the world at test time, they must be able to explore effectively, acquire new world knowledge and skills, retain relevant episodic experiences, and plan over long horizons. To evaluate these key abilities of test-time continual learning agents, we introduce AgentOdyssey, a novel evaluation framework that procedurally generates open-ended text games with rich entities, world dynamics, and long-horizon tasks. Critically, AgentOdyssey goes beyond the conventional machine learning assumption that learning does not occur at test time by placing agents in a continuous, long-horizon setting that interleaves learning and inference throughout deployment. We further propose a multifaceted evaluation methodology that measures not only game progress but also offers diagnostic tests on world knowledge acquisition, episodic memory, object and action exploration, action diversity, and model cost. We evaluate diverse agent paradigms in the generated games. Our experimental results reveal critical limits in agents' key abilities, as well as factors that influence their meaningful horizon. Although performance scales with stronger base models, even the top agent remains far below human performance, leaving substantial headroom for improvement. Among agent mechanisms, we find that short-term memory benefits multiple agent paradigms and is an important component of agent test-time training.

</details>


### [98] [Error-Aware TF-IDF Retrieval-Augmented Generation for ASR Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24915)
*Mohammad Aref Jafari-Raddani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种高效的纯词法错误感知检索增强框架，用于减少端到端语音识别中罕见实体和领域术语的幻觉问题，并在FLEURS数据集相关子集上显著降低词错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端ASR系统容易在低资源语言、罕见实体和专业术语场景中产生幻觉；现有RAG方法要么采用忽略语音误识别的稀疏检索，要么依赖高延迟的跨模态嵌入，因此需要低延迟且能显式处理音近误识别的方案。

Method: 方法包含对称文本规范化模块和错误感知TF-IDF算法；通过基于历史错误构造稀疏对角惩罚矩阵，使检索器优先召回包含高风险误识别及其纠正信息的文档，从而处理音近错误和循环幻觉。

Result: 错误感知命中率从53.7%提升至90.9%；端到端评估中，最终词错误率从23.06%降低至18.83%，且推理延迟接近于零。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持传统稀疏检索高效率的同时，引入显式错误建模，能有效减少ASR幻觉并提升识别准确率，适合对延迟敏感的语音识别增强场景。

Abstract: End-to-end automatic speech recognition systems frequently hallucinate rare entities and domain-specific terms, especially in low-resource languages. While retrieval-augmented generation frameworks can mitigate these errors using large language models, current architectures face significant challenges. They either rely on standard sparse retrieval that ignores phonetic misrecognitions or utilize heavyweight cross-modal embeddings that introduce high latency. This letter proposes a highly efficient, purely lexical error-aware framework designed to explicitly resolve phonetic and loop hallucinations. Our approach integrates a symmetric text normalization module with a novel error-aware term frequency-inverse document frequency algorithm. By constructing a sparse diagonal penalty matrix based on historical errors, the retriever mathematically prioritizes corrective documents containing specific high-risk misrecognitions. Evaluated on the Persian subset of the FLEURS dataset, our method increased the error-aware hit rate from 53.7% to 90.9%. In end-to-end evaluations, the integrated framework reduced the final word error rate from 23.06% to 18.83%, achieving significant accuracy gains with near-zero inference latency.

</details>


### [99] [Perfect Detection, Failed Control: The Geometry of Knowing vs. Steering in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24952)
*Cosimo Galeone,Anna Ettorre,Minsu Park,Giuseppe Ettorre,Daniele Ligorio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究机械可解释性中“检测方向是否等同于控制方向”的隐含假设，发现对幻觉/虚假实体而言二者显著不一致：模型能线性检测，却不能沿同一方向有效干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性常假设：若某行为可在激活空间中被某个方向检测到，则沿该方向进行干预应能控制该行为。本文希望用几何角度检验这一假设，即比较“最佳检测方向”和“最佳致因/控制方向”的夹角。

Method: 作者在 Gemma 2-2B-it 及其他多个模型上，分别估计行为检测方向与行为控制方向，并用余弦相似度衡量二者对齐程度；研究对象包括输出格式控制和幻觉/虚假实体识别。同时测试跨模型、跨规模、指令微调前后的一致性，并尝试将检测方向向拒答方向旋转以评估干预效果。

Result: 输出格式任务中，检测与控制基本落在同一轴上；但幻觉任务中，模型从第 5 层起可完美线性区分虚假实体（AUC=1.000），其检测方向与产生拒答的方向余弦仅约 0.12（约 83 度）。基于激活的检测器也不对齐（cos=-0.06）。该差距在 1B-9B、三类模型家族、指令微调前后均稳定存在。将方向向拒答方向旋转 15 度可部分提升拒答率，同时保持较低误报。

Conclusion: 检测与控制并不必然等价；在幻觉场景中，“知道”虚假实体和“引导模型拒答”是可分离的机制。余弦相似度可作为检测—干预解耦的权重可计算信号，但不能可靠预测可操控性，因为检测往往是高维类别而非单一方向。

Abstract: A central aspiration of mechanistic interpretability is controllability: if we know where a behavior is represented in a model's activations, we should be able to modify it. This rests on a hidden premise -- that the direction which detects a behavior and the direction which controls it are the same, or close. We test this geometrically: what is the angle between the direction that best detects a behavior and the one that best causes it? If detection implies control the cosine is near 1; otherwise it quantifies a detection-intervention gap. On Gemma 2-2B-it, output format (clean JSON vs markdown fencing) collapses both roles onto one axis. Hallucination does not: the model detects fake entities with perfect linear separability (AUC = 1.000 from layer 5), yet that direction sits at cos = 0.12 (about 83 degrees) from the direction producing a refusal -- a small, reproducible alignment, far from the cos = 1 that "detection is control" would require. A detector built from activations, with no chosen tokens, likewise fails to align (cos = -0.06). The gap generalizes: across four models from three families and two scales (1B-9B), cos stays in [0.12, 0.20], identical before and after instruction tuning (0.1197 vs 0.1200), placing its origin in pretraining. A 15-degree rotation toward the refusal direction partially bridges it -- 73% and 60% refusal on two held-out fake-entity categories at 1.8% false positives. We then ask whether this cosine predicts steerability, and it does not: detection is a high-dimensional class, not a single direction, and what separates the steerable case is functional, not readable from a static angle. The cosine is a weight-computable signature of the dissociation between knowing and steering, not a predictor of it.

</details>


### [100] [Dustin: Draft-Augmented Sparse Verification for Efficient Long-Context Generation with Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24957)
*WenHung Lee,Jian-Jia Chen,Xiaolin Lin,Pei-Shuo Wang,Chi-Chih Chang,Chun-Che Yang,Ning-Chi Huang,Grace Li Zhang,Kai-Chiang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Dustin 是一种面向长上下文投机解码的稀疏验证框架，通过更精准地选择关键 KV token 缓解验证阶段 KV cache 加载瓶颈，在保持精度几乎不变的同时显著提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多批次长上下文 LLM 的投机解码虽然能提升吞吐，但验证阶段需要加载大量 KV cache，导致延迟主要受自注意力验证瓶颈限制。现有 KV 压缩方法不适合该场景：静态淘汰会因 token 重要性随生成过程变化而损失精度，动态选择又会在验证路径中引入过高计算开销。

Method: 论文提出 Dustin，将 draft model 的前瞻信号与 target model 的历史注意力结合，用于在多步验证窗口内高保真识别关键 token；同时采用稀疏估计策略，仅在少量注意力头上进行重要性评分，以降低重计算和选择开销。

Result: 在 PG-19 和 LongBench 上，以 Qwen2.5-72B 为目标模型进行评测，Dustin 在 32k 序列长度下实现了 27.85 倍 self-attention 加速和 9.17 倍端到端解码加速，并且精度下降可以忽略。

Conclusion: Dustin 证明了结合草稿模型前瞻信息与目标模型历史注意力的稀疏验证，可以有效突破长上下文投机解码中的 KV cache 验证瓶颈，是一种兼顾速度、精度与低开销的推理优化方案。

Abstract: While speculative decoding improves inference throughput for multi-batch long-context Large Language Models (LLMs), its efficiency is often limited by a verification bottleneck where Key-Value (KV) cache loading dominates latency. Existing compression methods fail in this regime: static eviction incurs accuracy loss due to saliency shift, while dynamic selection introduces prohibitive computational overhead during the verification path. We propose Dustin, a sparse verification framework designed for long-context speculative decoding. Dustin integrates lookahead signals from the draft model with historical attention from the target model to identify critical tokens with high fidelity across multi-step verification windows. To reduce recomputation latency, this approach further employs a sparse estimation scheme that restricts importance scoring to a minimal subset of attention heads. Evaluations on PG-19 and LongBench with Qwen2.5-72B demonstrate that Dustin achieves a 27.85x speedup in self-attention and a 9.17x end-to-end decoding speedup at a 32k sequence length, all with negligible accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [101] [LLM Performance on a Real, Double-Marked GCSE Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24973)
*Malachy Fox,Kavi Samra,Paul Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含32,534份真实学生双评分答卷的数据集，并评估现成大语言模型在自动阅卷中与人工考官评分的一致性；结果显示，顶尖模型与考官共识的一致性甚至超过两名考官之间的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考试阅卷需要高一致性、公平性和效率，但人工阅卷存在评分差异且成本较高。研究希望检验通用大语言模型是否能在真实、复杂、多学科、含手写内容的考试场景中达到或超过人工考官的一致性水平。

Method: 作者收集32,534份经两名考官独立评分的真实学生模拟考试答卷，覆盖328道题和五个学科，并包含手写作答。随后使用现成大语言模型进行评分，将模型评分与考官共识及两名考官之间的一致性进行比较。

Result: 模型在不同学科上普遍与考官共识高度一致；表现最好的模型与考官的一致性高于两名考官之间的一致性。模型不仅能处理英语作文等主观任务，也能处理复杂、潦草的手写数学试卷。模型规模对一致性的影响并不显著，较小模型也可能具备较高性价比。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在真实考试自动阅卷中具有较强可行性，能够达到接近甚至超过人工考官间一致性的表现，并可能为低成本、可扩展的自动评分系统提供支持。

Abstract: We introduce a dataset of 32,534 double-marked real student responses to GCSE mock exams (GCSEs are the UK's national exams, taken at age ~16), spanning 328 questions across five subjects and including handwritten work. We test whether off-the-shelf large language models agree with examiners as closely as the two examiners agree with each other. We find that models overwhelmingly agree well with the examiner consensus across subjects, with the top performing models agreeing more closely with examiners than examiners agree with each other. Models achieve high scores for subjective tasks like English essay marking, as well as handling complex and messy handwritten Maths paper scripts. Agreement is uniform near the examiner line, and not massively discriminated by model size, providing cost-effective automated marking solutions.

</details>


### [102] [LLM-Based Scientific Peer Review: Methods, Benchmarks, and Reliability Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25057)
*Thi Huyen Nguyen,Zahra Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大语言模型用于科学同行评审的系统性综述，重点分析自动评审中的评语生成与评分预测，并讨论可靠性、鲁棒性、安全性和可信性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文投稿量快速增长，使传统同行评审面临可扩展性瓶颈；尽管LLM已能生成流畅评语并近似预测审稿分数，但其作为决策支持系统的可靠性、鲁棒性与安全性仍缺乏充分理解。

Method: 论文从系统层面对LLM辅助同行评审进行综述，围绕评语生成和评分预测两类核心功能，构建方法分类体系，包括提示式方法、监督学习方法、检索增强方法和对齐优化方法，并综合分析现有基准上的经验结果、数据集限制、评估缺陷和领域偏差。

Result: 综述指出，现有LLM评审系统虽在文本生成和评分近似方面表现出潜力，但评估实践受到数据集规模与质量、评价指标不足、学科领域集中等因素限制；同时存在提示注入、数据投毒、检索脆弱性和奖励黑客等鲁棒性与安全风险。

Conclusion: 论文认为自动化同行评审应被视为高风险、多目标决策问题，未来需要重点解决主观分歧建模、跨领域泛化、透明性、鲁棒性和可信性等问题，以构建可靠的AI辅助科学评价系统。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific submissions has pushed traditional peer review toward its scalability limits, motivating the exploration of large language models (LLMs) as intelligent automated evaluation assistants. Although recent studies show that LLMs can generate fluent critiques and approximate reviewer scores, their reliability, robustness, and security as decision-support systems remain insufficiently understood. This survey offers a systems-level analysis of LLM-based scientific peer review, focusing on two core evaluative functions: critique generation and score prediction. We present a structured taxonomy of modeling approaches (including prompt-based, supervised, retrieval-augmented, and alignment-optimized approaches), and synthesize empirical findings across existing benchmarks. We analyze dataset constraints, evaluation shortcomings, and domain concentration biases that limit current assessment practices. Beyond performance metrics, we identify emerging robustness risks, including prompt injection, data poisoning, retrieval vulnerabilities, and reward hacking, which expose automated review pipelines to strategic manipulation. From a data mining perspective, we outline key open challenges in modeling subjective disagreement and cross-domain generalization. By reframing automated peer review as a high-stakes, multi-objective decision problem, this survey provides a roadmap for developing robust, transparent, and trustworthy AI-assisted scientific evaluation systems.

</details>


### [103] [Dream at SemEval-2026 Task 13: SALSA for Single-Pass Machine-Generated Code Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25102)
*Ruslan Berdichevsky,Shai Nahum-Gefen,Elad Ben-Zaken*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向代码片段作者归属检测的单次自回归结构化分类方法，将类别映射为专用输出 token，并通过参数高效微调与保守训练提升跨语言、跨领域 OOD 泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型显著提升代码生成能力，引发代码作者归属、评测诚信与软件可信性方面的问题。任务要求在未见编程语言和应用领域上进行二分类检测，因此核心挑战是分布外泛化。

Method: 采用 SALSA 风格的 Single-pass Autoregressive LLM Structured Classification：将每个类别绑定到专用输出 token，训练模型在结构化响应中输出单 token 标签；同时使用跨语言均衡采样、参数高效微调、低学习率和单轮训练，以降低对训练域的过拟合。

Result: 最佳系统在官方排行榜上达到 OOD F1 = 0.789，明显优于 CodeBERT 基线的 F1 = 0.305。

Conclusion: 该方法表明，将代码检测建模为结构化单 token 自回归分类，并结合保守微调策略，可以显著增强大语言模型在代码作者归属检测中的分布外鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models have transformed code generation, raising concerns around authorship, assessment integrity, and software trust. SemEval-2026 Task 13 Subtask A operationalizes detection as binary classification over code snippets, with a particular emphasis on out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization across unseen programming languages and application domains. We propose a SALSA-style formulation, Single-pass Autoregressive LLM Structured Classification, that maps each class to a dedicated output token and trains the model to emit a single-token label in a structured response. Rather than engineering hand-crafted features or decision rules, this formulation delegates the authorship decision to the model. To improve OOD robustness, we combine balanced sampling across languages with parameter-efficient fine-tuning and conservative training (low learning rate, single epoch) to avoid overfitting to the training domain. Our best system achieves OOD $F_1 = 0.789$ on the official leaderboard, substantially outperforming the CodeBERT baseline ($F_1 = 0.305$).

</details>


### [104] [The cognitive, affective, and behavioral expression of self-stigma among people who use drugs in online substance use communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25143)
*Layla Bouzoubaa,Hyung Wook Choi,Milan Varghese,Valerie Earnshaw,Rezvaneh Rezapour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建并验证了一个用于识别药物使用者 Reddit 发帖中自我污名的十指标编码本，并用大语言模型扩展分析，发现自我污名虽只出现在少数帖子中，但涉及多数用户，且行为指标常与内化指标共现并可能更早出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在系统刻画在线文本中药物使用者的自我污名表达，覆盖认知、情感与行为维度，并检验这些指标的流行程度、共现关系与时间变化模式，以弥补传统阶段模型难以直接解释数字化自我披露的不足。

Method: 作者通过共识式溯因编码开发十项指标编码本，涵盖认知、情感和行为三大领域；两名编码员达到较高一致性。随后用经专家编码验证的大语言模型进行大规模分类，分析 2006—2025 年间 1,660 名英语用户发布的 72,115 条 Reddit 主题帖。

Result: 共有 3,838 条帖子（5.3%）和 1,228 名用户（74.0%）出现自我污名。十项指标均能区分自我污名帖子，其中自我标签化和绝望/无望最常见。核心指标与行为指标在用户层面显著相关，且多数含行为指标的帖子同时包含核心指标。与递进式模型相反，行为指标出现时间早于部分核心指标；多数指标随时间稳定，仅悲观/自我挫败显著增加。

Conclusion: 在线药物使用者的自我污名是一种整合性现象，行为表达通常不脱离内化体验，且可能先于更显性的核心情绪或认知表达出现。随时间加深的改变悲观感可作为早期数字干预的重要靶点，也提示传统渐进阶段模型不能简单套用于在线文本披露。

Abstract: Objectives: To develop a codebook for self-stigma across cognitive, affective, and behavioral domains, and to estimate the prevalence, co-occurrence, and temporal patterns of these indicators in Reddit posts by people who use drugs. Methods: We developed a ten-indicator codebook through consensus-based abductive coding spanning cognitive (self-labeling, pessimism/self-defeatism, deservingness/worthlessness), affective (shame, guilt/self-blame, despair/hopelessness), and behavioral (concealment, anticipated rejection, desire to quit, ambivalence) domains; two coders reached substantial agreement (Cohen's k = 0.72). We then scaled classification with a large language model validated against expert coding (k = 0.73, F1 = 0.80), analyzing 72,115 thread-initiating posts from 1,660 English-language users (2006-2025). Results: 3,838 posts (5.3%) from 1,228 users (74.0%) contained self-stigma; all ten indicators discriminated self-stigma posts (RR 3.6 to 86.2), led by self-labeling (56.0%) and despair/hopelessness (48.5%). Self-stigma was integrated: core and behavioral indicators were strongly associated at the user level (OR = 4.65, 95% CI 3.12-6.94, p < 0.001), and 87.0% of posts with behavioral indicators also contained a core indicator. Contrary to progressive models, behavioral indicators emerged earlier than core ones (desire to quit at median position 0.08 vs. shame at 0.38). Nine of ten indicators were stable across posting trajectories; only pessimism increased (OR = 1.62, 95% CI 1.25-2.10). Conclusion: Among people who use drugs online, self-stigma is an integrated phenomenon in which behavioral indicators rarely appear without internalized ones and often precede them. Most expressions remain stable over time, but pessimism about change deepens, marking a target for early digital intervention and showing that progressive stage models do not map directly onto textual disclosure.

</details>


### [105] [Hitting a Moving Target: Test-Time Adaptation for AI Text Detection under Continual Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25152)
*Kevin Ren,Manish Raghavan,Nikhil Garg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于 AI 文本检测的测试时自适应方法，利用推理阶段未标注样本之间的同质性来应对部署后的分布偏移，相比现有监督检测器在对抗改写、新模型和人类写作时间漂移下更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 文本检测器通常依赖训练阶段获得的人类文本与 AI 生成文本标注数据，但部署后会持续遭遇无标注的分布偏移，包括对抗性“人类化”改写、新 LLM 发布以及人类写作风格随时间变化；同时，已有方法未充分利用 LLM 使用带来的推理时样本同质性信号。

Method: 作者提出基于半监督学习的测试时自适应（TTA）框架，在推理阶段利用未标注样本之间的同质性进行模型适配，从而在没有新标注数据的情况下缓解分布偏移对检测性能的影响。

Result: 实验表明，当前先进的监督式检测器在遇到 AI 生成文本和人类写作的自然或对抗性分布偏移时会系统性失效；相比之下，基于半监督学习的 TTA 方法表现出较强鲁棒性。例如，商业模型 Pangram 对对抗性 AI 文本的检出率仅为 24.1%，而作者的测试时方法达到 90.5%。

Conclusion: 测试时自适应是面向真实部署场景的 AI 文本检测的有前景框架，能够在缺乏新标注数据的情况下提升对分布偏移的鲁棒性；作者还公开了训练、评估和绘图代码。

Abstract: Deployed approaches for AI text detection often rely on training-time access to labeled datasets of both human-written and AI-generated text. This approach is vulnerable to three types of distribution shifts that occur continually post-deployment, and for which labeled data is often unavailable: adversarial humanization, new LLMs being released, and temporal drift in human writing. Simultaneously, existing approaches do not leverage a key signal of LLM usage: inference-time homogeneity. We propose a test-time adaptation (TTA) approach, using semi-supervised learning, that adapts to distribution shifts by leveraging homogeneity among unlabeled samples observed at inference time. Empirically, we find that state-of-the-art supervised detectors systematically fail when they encounter distribution shifts in AI-generated and human writing, both adversarial and natural, while test-time adaptation with semi-supervised learning is largely robust; e.g., the commercial model Pangram detects just 24.1% of our adversarial AI-generated text, compared to 90.5% for our test-time approach. We establish that test-time adaptation is a promising framework for AI text detection in the wild. We publicly release our code (which includes code for model training, evaluation, and plots) at https://github.com/kkr36/llm_detection.

</details>


### [106] [What Intermediate Layers Know: Detecting Jailbreaks from Entropy Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25182)
*Sofiia Nikolenko,Michele Papucci,Mina Rezaei,Shireen Kudukkil Manchingal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM越狱攻击意图如何体现在模型内部表征中，发现逐token预测熵在中间层的动态变化比静态统计量更能区分越狱提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御多集中在输入提示或输出层面，但尚不清楚有害意图在已对齐大模型内部如何编码；理解这一机制有助于改进安全检测与模型解释。

Method: 作者使用冻结LLM与logit lens分析各层逐token预测熵轨迹，比较提示级熵的静态聚合统计与跨token位置的动态趋势特征，并在Llama、Qwen、Gemma及多个对抗基准上验证。
