### [141] [SpeechEQ: Benchmarking Emotional Intelligence Quotient in Socially Aware Voice Conversational Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25990)
*Liang-Yuan Wu,Zih-Ching Chen,Tongshuang Wu,Chao-Han Huck Yang,Hua Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpeechEQ，用于评估语音语言模型在多轮对话中理解和运用副语言情绪/社交线索的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器情绪智能评测多依赖孤立文本或被动声学感知，无法覆盖真实人机语音对话中所需的跨模态、多轮、主动社会语言推理能力。

Method: 构建包含 2,265 段对话的数据集，覆盖基于 EQ-i 2.0 理论的 15 个情商子维度；提出多轮评估协议，并设计 Spoken EQ（SEQ）分数来衡量语音对话中的情绪智能表现。

Result: 实验表明，现有语音情绪识别模型和端到端语音语言模型在理解并利用副语言线索方面仍存在明显不足；端到端架构优于级联系统，但整体能力仍受限。

Conclusion: SpeechEQ 揭示当前多模态模型存在文本依赖的“模态捷径”、对齐导致的“安全陷阱”和“上下文遗忘”等问题，为评估和改进真正具备情绪感知能力的 AI 提供了基准。

Abstract: As multimodal conversational systems increasingly engage in spoken interaction, their ability to navigate paralinguistic social cues has become a critical bottleneck for natural human-AI communication. However, existing evaluations of machine emotional intelligence assess reasoning exclusively through isolated text or passive acoustic perception, overlooking the complex cross-modal reasoning required for active, multi-turn dialogue. We introduce \textsc{SpeechEQ}, a comprehensive framework designed to evaluate the sociolinguistic reasoning of Speech-Language Models (SLMs). The framework includes a validated dataset of 2,265 dialogues across 15 Emotional Quotient (EQ) subscales grounded in EQ-i 2.0 theory, along with a multi-turn evaluation protocol measured by our proposed Spoken EQ (SEQ) score inspired by human EQ assessments. Experiments show limitations in how both existing Speech Emotion Recognition and end-to-end Speech-Language Models understand and apply paralinguistic cues through speech. While end-to-end architectures outperform cascaded systems, \textsc{SpeechEQ} reveals that current multimodal models remain bottlenecked by a text-reliant ``modality shortcut,'' an alignment-induced ``safety trap,'' and ``contextual amnesia,'' highlighting the barriers to truly emotionally aware AI. Our benchmark can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/datasets/SpeechEQ/SpeechEQ and demo page at https://binomial14.github.io/speecheq-demo/

</details>


### [142] [Dziri Voicebot: An End-to-End Low-Resource Speech-to-Speech Conversational System for Algerian Dialect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26003)
*Dihia Lanasri,Fairouz Taki,Asma Kemmoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向阿尔及利亚方言的端到端语音对话系统基线，整合ASR、NLU、RAG与TTS，并在电信领域自建数据集上微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音与语言技术偏向高资源语言；阿尔及利亚方言存在正字法不统一、与法语频繁代码切换、标注语音资源稀缺等问题，亟需完整的语音到语音对话系统。

Method: 构建模块化流水线：基于Whisper适配的自动语音识别；结合Transformer嵌入与任务型对话框架的自然语言理解；检索增强生成用于回复生成；基于新采集方言语料训练神经TTS。并为ASR、NLU、TTS分别构建电信领域专用数据集。

Result: 各组件实验表现良好：ASR词错误率较低，NLU意图分类与实体识别分数较高，TTS语音合成质量稳定。

Conclusion: 该系统将先前文本对话工作扩展为完整语音交互，为阿尔及利亚方言端到端会话建模提供了可复现基线。

Abstract: Automatic speech and language technologies are still heavily biased toward high-resource languages, limiting their applicability to dialectal and low-resource settings such as Algerian Dialect. This language presents additional challenges including lack of standardized orthography, frequent codeswitching with French, and scarcity of annotated speech resources. This paper addresses the problem of building a complete speech-to-speech conversational system for Algerian Dialect. We propose a modular pipeline integrating automatic speech recognition, natural language understanding, retrieval-augmented generation, and text-to-speech synthesis within a unified architecture. This work is the continuation of our previous work on Algerian dialectal conversational systems Bechiri and Lanasri [2026], extending it from text-based dialogue modeling to full speech-based interaction. We constructed dedicated datasets for ASR, NLU, and TTS in the telecom domain and fine-tune pretrained models for each component. The ASR system is built on Whisper-based adaptation, while the NLU module combines transformer-based embeddings with a task-oriented dialogue framework. A neural TTS system is trained on a newly collected dialectal corpus to enable spoken response generation. Experimental results show strong performance across all components, including low word error rate for ASR, high intent classification and entity recognition scores for NLU, and stable speech synthesis quality. The proposed system provides a reproducible baseline for end-to-end conversational modeling in Algerian Dialect.

</details>


### [143] [The Tatoxa System for Text Detoxification in Low-Resource Languages: The Case of Tatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26015)
*Ilseyar Alimova,Bogdan Monogov,Artyom Mazur,Daniil Antonov,Vsevolod Karimov,Vitaliy Egorov,Bulat Khakimov,Alexander Panchenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Tatoxa：面向鞑靼语的文本去毒化系统与数据集，在低资源场景下优于现有开源和商业大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社区需要自动检测与缓解有害/冒犯性内容以保障用户安全，但鞑靼语等低资源语言在文本去毒化研究中长期缺乏数据、模型和系统性评测。

Method: 作者构建新的鞑靼语文本去毒化数据集，用于微调与评估；提出 Tatoxa 系统，并与开源及商业 LLM 进行对比；同时开展跨语言迁移实验，比较从其他语言（包括文化接近的俄语）迁移与使用原生鞑靼语数据训练的效果。

Result: 实验表明，Tatoxa 在关键质量指标上超过现有开源模型和商业 LLM；跨语言迁移效果显著弱于基于原生鞑靼语数据训练，即使拥有大规模俄语语料也不能替代本语言数据。

Conclusion: 低资源语言文本去毒化需要面向本语言的数据集与模型适配；文化或语言邻近的跨语言迁移并不足以保证高质量效果，原生数据在鞑靼语去毒化中具有关键价值。

Abstract: Text detoxification, the automated detection and mitigation of abusive and harmful content, is essential for ensuring the safety of online communities and protecting users. However, low resource languages such as Tatar have received little research attention. In this paper we present Tatoxa, a novel state-of-the-art system for text detoxification in the Tatar language. Comparative experiments show that the proposed approach outperforms existing open source and proprietary commercial LLMs on key quality metrics. We also introduce a new dataset for text detoxification in Tatar, designed for fine tuning and evaluation in low resource settings. Finally, cross lingual transfer experiments indicate that transfer from other languages, including the culturally close Russian, performs significantly worse than training on native Tatar data even when a large Russian corpus is available.

</details>


### [144] [Why Multi-Step Tool-Use Reinforcement Learning Collapses and How Supervisory Signals Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26027)
*Yupu Hao,Zhuoran Jin,Huanxuan Liao,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型在工具使用任务中进行强化学习训练时的失稳与崩溃问题，并表明将监督微调与强化学习交错训练可显著提升稳定性，但在分布外格式与内容评测上仍存在性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用能扩展LLM处理复杂多步骤任务的能力，但仅依赖智能体式强化学习常出现训练不稳定、收益有限甚至灾难性崩溃。作者希望理解这些失败机制，并探索哪些监督信号能稳定和引导RL中的探索学习。

Method: 作者分析工具调用失败的内部原因，发现特定控制token出现异常概率尖峰会破坏结构化执行；随后系统比较多类监督信号，包括离策略监督、提示式指导、错误样例监督等，并在同步训练与SFT-RL交错训练两种范式下进行实验，同时考察学习率与跨设置泛化。

Result: 实验显示，部分模型会出现性能骤降和工具调用结构失效，但底层工具使用能力并未消失，而是被特定输出格式问题遮蔽。交错式SFT与RL训练能显著改善训练稳定性；不过在格式OOD和内容OOD评测中，该方法仍表现出泛化性能下降。

Conclusion: 论文强调应深入理解LLM工具使用RL训练中的失败模式，并证明多样化监督信号可有效引导探索、缓解结构崩溃，从而提升复杂多步骤工具使用任务的训练鲁棒性，但OOD泛化仍是关键挑战。

Abstract: Tool use enables large language models (LLMs) to perform complex tasks, and recent agentic reinforcement learning (RL) methods show promise for enhancing model capabilities. However, RL alone often leads to instability or limited gains in tool-use tasks. In our experiments, some models exhibit catastrophic collapse, where performance abruptly drops and tool-invocation structures fail. The analysis reveals that these failures stem from unexpected probability spikes in specific control tokens, disrupting structured execution, yet the underlying tool-use capability remains intact, merely obscured by specific formats. To address this, we systematically investigate a diverse set of supervisory signals, including off-policy supervision, hint-based guidance, erroneous example supervision, and others, applied under both synchronous and interleaved training schemes. We find that interleaving supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with RL substantially improves stability, but exhibits degraded performance under format and content out-of-distribution (OOD) evaluation. We also analyze the impact of learning rates and generalization across settings. These results highlight the importance of understanding RL failures and demonstrate how diverse supervisory signals can guide exploratory learning, enabling robust training of LLMs for complex, multi-step tool-use tasks. Our Code is available at https://github.com/hypasd-art/Tool-RL-Box.

</details>


### [145] [Detect, Unlearn, Restore: Defending Text Summarization Models Against Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26036)
*Poojitha Thota,Shirin Nilizadeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于摘要模型微调阶段数据投毒的事后防御框架，可在白盒与黑盒场景中检测投毒并通过遗忘恢复模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在摘要任务中常依赖小规模任务数据微调，少量被篡改样本即可显著影响模型输出，导致偏见、有害或事实扭曲的摘要，同时常规指标可能无法发现该问题，因此需要面向供应链与部署后的实用防御方法。

Method: 白盒场景下，利用影响函数分析识别训练影响异常偏高的文档-摘要对，并结合语义一致性检查；黑盒场景下，通过语义保持扰动测试模型行为敏感性进行审计；同时提出针对事实扭曲和表征偏差的新型投毒攻击，并用梯度上升遗忘进行修复。

Result: 在九种模型架构和六个基准数据集上，面对自适应攻击，防御方法达到85%到92%的检测精度；梯度上升遗忘最多恢复96%的原始行为，ROUGE下降低于0.6%。

Conclusion: 微调阶段投毒会在摘要模型中留下持久结构性痕迹，使得无需完整重训练即可进行实际检测、行为审计和部署后恢复。

Abstract: Training-time data poisoning during fine-tuning poses a significant threat to large language models (LLMs) deployed for abstractive text summarization, where small task-specific datasets exert disproportionate influence on model behavior. In this setting, adversaries manipulate fine-tuning data to induce persistent summarization failures, such as biased or harmful summaries, while preserving standard evaluation metrics. We present a unified post-hoc defense framework for detecting and remediating fine-tuning-stage poisoning in summarization models across the machine learning supply chain. Our experiments show that in white-box settings, poisoned document-summary pairs exhibit abnormally high training influence, enabling detection via influence-function analysis with semantic consistency checks. In black-box settings, poisoned models display two to three times greater sensitivity to semantics-preserving perturbations, enabling behavioral auditing without training data access. Beyond existing poisoning formulations, we introduce novel attacks targeting factual distortion and representational bias, showing that poisoning alters summarization behavior without triggering conventional alarms. Across nine architectures and six benchmark datasets under adaptive attacks, our defenses achieve 85-92% detection precision, while gradient-ascent unlearning restores up to 96% of original behavior with minimal utility loss (less than 0.6% ROUGE degradation). These results indicate that fine-tuning-time poisoning leaves persistent structural artifacts, enabling practical detection and post-deployment recovery without full retraining.

</details>


### [146] [AI translation of literary texts is "fine", but readers still prefer human translations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26040)
*Yves Ferstler,Adam Podoxin,Ty Brassington,Roman Grundkiewicz,Maite Taboada,Marzena Karpinska*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究读者如何体验文学作品的AI翻译，发现读者总体认为机器翻译尚可，但更偏好人工翻译；现有自动指标和LLM评审难以反映真实读者偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文学翻译评价不能只看流畅性、充分性或自动指标；沉浸感、文学效果、阅读舒适度等读者体验维度尚未被充分研究。随着LLM机器翻译用于文学作品的情况增多，需要建立以读者为中心的评价方法。

Method: 研究招募15名深度读者，对15部法语、波兰语、日语近年小说的英译版本进行比较。比较对象包括已出版人工翻译与基于代理式LLM流程生成的机器翻译。实验包含约8000词片段的沉浸式阅读比较，以及386组对齐文本块的细读比较；每本书两名读者，呈现顺序交替。研究还收集评论、偏好评分、判断与跨度级标注，并与自动指标及LLM-as-a-judge结果对比。

Result: 读者认为机器翻译总体“还可以”，但在篇章层面略偏好人工翻译，在文本块层面更明显偏好人工翻译。人工翻译在易读性、清晰度和沉浸感方面更受认可。机器翻译在同一本书内部质量波动更大。读者不能可靠地区分人工翻译和机器翻译，但倾向于偏好他们认为是人工翻译的版本。自动指标和LLM评审未能恢复读者偏好，且倾向于高估机器翻译。

Conclusion: 文学AI翻译评价需要超越传统自动指标和一般人工充分性/流畅性评估，转向读者体验导向。论文发布LAIT数据集及评估协议，为研究文学翻译的沉浸感、文学效果和真实读者偏好提供资源。

Abstract: AI translation of literary works is increasingly common. While the content may be rendered adequately, we do not know enough about how readers experience it in terms of immersiveness and literary effect, aspects poorly captured by automatic machine translation metrics or human evaluation targeting fluency and adequacy. We ask 15 avid readers to compare recently published human translations (HT) to machine translations (MT) generated with an agentic large language model (LLM)-based pipeline, for 15 recent novels in French, Polish, and Japanese and translated into English. Readers evaluated approximately 8K-word excerpts in two conditions: immersive reading of the whole excerpt (30 comparisons) and close reading of 386 aligned HT-MT chunk pairs (772 comparisons), with two readers per book and in alternating order of presentation. Overall, readers find MT "fine", but prefer HT (slightly at excerpt-level 19/30, more clearly at chunk-level 522/772) for its ease, clarity, and immersive nature. Readers' highlights show that MT's quality varies more within one book than HT's does. Crucially, readers cannot reliably tell the two apart (17/30 guess correctly) and tend to prefer the version they believe to be human. Automatic metrics, including LLM-as-a-judge approaches, fail to recover reader preferences and favor MT. We release LAIT (Literary AI Translation), a reader-centered evaluation dataset with 1K reader comments, 2K judgments and preference ratings, and 7.2K span-level annotations, along with our evaluation protocol and supporting interface.

</details>


### [147] [When Certainty Is an Artifact: Keyword Lexicon Blindness and the (Mis)Measurement of Rhetorical Stance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26062)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，计算社会科学中一个看似显著且大效应的“负面情绪—强调性确定性”相关，可能完全由关键词测量工具造成；改用LLM语义分类后，该相关大幅减弱或反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在检验：统计显著、效应量很大的发现是否可能只是测量工具的伪影，尤其是关键词词典是否会把词汇共现误当作说话者的认识论确定性或心理特征。

Method: 作者分析2016—2026年四位公共知识分子的85场访谈，先用关键词计数评估负面情绪、强调性确定性和模糊/保留表达之间的相关，再用LLM零样本语义分类处理完整说话人分离语料（32,625句），并进行句子级错误分析以定位关键词方法的失效模式。

Result: 关键词计数得到四位说话者均显著的负面情绪—强调性确定性共现（r=0.72—0.93，p<0.01）。但LLM语义分类后，该相关显著下降，例如Dalio从r=0.851降至r=0.206，另有两位为负相关、一位接近无相关。相反，LLM发现负面情绪与模糊/保留表达存在更强耦合，如Rogoff r=0.875、Zeihan r=0.722。

Conclusion: 论文认为，关键词词典捕捉到的是负面话语与强调词汇之间的普遍词汇共现，而非真正的修辞立场或认识论确定性。将关键词计数直接视为确定性的测量属于类别错误，可能把测量伪影误解释为说话者心理特征。

Abstract: Can a statistically significant, large-effect-size finding in computational social science be entirely an artifact of the measurement instrument? We present a case where the answer appears to be yes. Analyzing 85 interviews across four public intellectuals (2016--2026), we find a robust negative-affect/emphatic-certainty lexical co-occurrence pattern under keyword-based scoring ($r = 0.72$--$0.93$, $p < 0.01$ for all four speakers). Replacing keyword counting with LLM-based zero-shot semantic classification on the complete diarized corpus (32,625 sentences) dramatically reduces this correlation: Dalio's $r = 0.851$ drops to $r = 0.206$, with two speakers showing negative $r(\text{neg}, \text{emphatic})$ and one showing null. In contrast, the LLM reveals a strong negative-hedging coupling across speakers -- Rogoff's $r(\text{neg}, \text{hedged}) = 0.875$ ($p = 0.001$) and Zeihan's $r(\text{neg}, \text{hedged}) = 0.722$ ($p = 0.008$) -- consistent with the conventional expectation that pessimistic discourse attracts hedging, not certainty. Sentence-level error analysis traces this discrepancy to three structural failure modes in keyword lexicons -- syntactic blindness, polysemy blindness, and categorical absence -- illustrated through cases where keyword counting inverts semantic meaning (e.g., ''never absolutely totally confident'' scored as high-certainty). We argue that keyword lexicons measure a universal lexical co-occurrence tendency -- negative discourse naturally attracts emphatic vocabulary -- that is orthogonal to, and can systematically invert, rhetorical stance. Treating keyword counts as measurements of epistemic certainty is a category error: a finding that appears to be about a speaker's psychology may be entirely about the counting of words.

</details>


### [148] [Same Evidence, Different Answer: Auditing Order Sensitivity in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26079)
*Akshay Paruchuri,Sanmi Koyejo,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Facet-Probe，用于评估多模态大语言模型在输入顺序无关场景下的顺序鲁棒性；结果显示 18 个模型均不具备完全顺序不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型基准通常只在一种固定规范顺序下评分，无法发现选项、证据片段、文档排序、图像集合或混合模态输入顺序变化是否会导致答案改变；这与新兴 AI 评测指南中对基础可靠性的要求不一致。

Method: 作者设计了五个维度的顺序扰动审计框架 Facet-Probe，覆盖 option、evidence-chunk、document-rank、image-set 和 mixed-modality ordering，并评估 18 个前沿及开源权重 MLLM。方法上使用贝叶斯项目反应模型区分顺序噪声与各维度偏置，同时引入 same-ordering control 来估计解码随机性导致的答案翻转下限。

Result: 所有被测 18 个 MLLM 都不是顺序不变的；经过筛选的各维度面板平均翻转率为 24%–50%。Gemini 在温度为 0 的相同顺序控制实验中显示，真实顺序扰动带来的翻转显著高于相同输入下的解码噪声。模型能力提升可降低但不能消除翻转，最佳模型仍有 13.4% 的试验发生答案翻转。Gemini 的缓解实验表明，免训练提示改写具有模态条件性，不能从文本推理泛化到视觉推理。

Conclusion: 论文认为，仅依赖提示层面的缓解措施难以实现通用顺序鲁棒性，未来需要探索训练阶段和模型架构层面的改进；作者建议将跨顺序翻转率作为 MLLM 标准报告指标。

Abstract: Standard benchmarks for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) score each item on one canonical ordering and miss whether order-irrelevant shuffling changes the answer, a baseline reliability property called for by emerging AI evaluation guidelines. We introduce Facet-Probe, a five-facet audit (option, evidence-chunk, document-rank, image-set, and mixed-modality ordering) of 18 frontier and open-weight MLLMs. A Bayesian item-response model separates ordering noise from per-facet bias, and a same-ordering control estimates the decoder-stochastic floor for observed flips. We find that none of the 18 MLLMs we audit are order-invariant: screened per-facet panel-mean flip rates span 24-50%. A Gemini same-ordering control at temperature 0 estimates a substantial ordering excess over a same-input decoder-noise floor in verified cells. Capability predicts but does not eliminate flips; the best model still flips on 13.4% of trials. In our Gemini mitigation tests, training-free prompt changes are modality-conditional and do not transfer from text to visual reasoning. These results suggest that prompt-level mitigation alone is unlikely to provide general order robustness, motivating future work on training-time and architectural approaches. We propose cross-ordering flip rate as a standard reporting axis for MLLMs.

</details>


### [149] [Real-Time Voice AI Hears but Does Not Listen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26083)
*Martijn Bartelds,Federico Bianchi,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估四个实时语音AI系统在同时依赖语义内容与语音表达的任务中的表现，发现它们往往依据文字内容而非语气、情绪或声学线索做决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音交流不仅包含词语本身，还包含哭腔、恐惧、讽刺、口音、年龄等由声音传递的信息；在客服、金融授权、同意确认等高风险场景中，忽视这些线索可能导致错误或不安全决策。

Method: 研究比较OpenAI GPT Realtime 2、Google Gemini 3.1 Flash Live、阿里巴巴Qwen3.5 Omni Plus与Omni Flash，在三个关键情境及口音/年龄估计任务中测试其对文字内容与声音表达的响应；同时通过直接询问检验系统是否能感知情绪线索，并测试显式提示关注语音表达是否改善结果。

Result: 四个系统普遍更依赖文字内容：会结束哭泣但声称没事的来电、批准以恐惧声音授权的转账、接受明显讽刺的同意。三种系统在被直接询问时能识别痛苦、恐惧或讽刺，但在实际决策中忽略这些信息。显式提示关注语音表达只带来有限且不稳定的改进。

Conclusion: 当前实时语音AI存在“情绪智能缺口”：能够感知声音中的情绪或语用信息，却未能将其有效用于行动决策。此类系统在依赖语气、情绪和声学线索的重要场景中应谨慎使用。

Abstract: Speech conveys information through both words and vocal delivery. We evaluate four leading production realtime voice systems-OpenAI's GPT Realtime 2, Google's Gemini 3.1 Flash Live, and Alibaba's Qwen3.5 Omni Plus and Omni Flash-on tasks where the words and the delivery patterns both convey meaningful information. Across three consequential scenarios, all four systems act on the words rather than the voice. They end calls with crying callers who insist nothing is wrong, approve wire transfers authorized in frightened voices, and enroll callers whose agreement is clearly sarcastic. Surprisingly, this is often not a failure of perception. When asked directly, three of the four systems reliably identify the distress, fear, or sarcasm they later ignore when making decisions. We observe a similar pattern when these realtime voice systems estimate accent and age, as their responses frequently follow the biases of the words rather than the acoustic properties of the speaker. We term this disconnect between perception and action the emotional intelligence gap of voice AI. Prompting systems to explicitly attend to vocal delivery improves performance only partially and inconsistently. Our findings show that current realtime voice AI systems often behave as if speech had been reduced to a transcript, suggesting that they should be used with caution in settings where the tone and emotion of delivery convey important information.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [The Hitchhiker's Guide to Agentic AI: From Foundations to Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24937)
*Haggai Roitman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该书/论文式摘要提出一本面向实践者的 Agentic AI 全栈参考，覆盖从 LLM 基础设施、对齐与推理，到智能体系统设计、多智能体协作、评估与生产部署的完整流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前构建高质量自主智能体系统不能只理解单一模块，而需要贯通模型底座、训练与推理优化、对齐方法、工具使用、记忆、检索、多智能体通信、评估和部署等各层技术。该书旨在为实践者提供系统化、端到端的知识框架与工程指南。

Method: 内容采用分层组织方式：首先介绍 Transformer、GPU 系统、训练/微调、模型压缩与推理优化等 LLM 基础；随后讨论 RLHF、PPO、DPO、GRPO、奖励建模、链式思维和测试时扩展等对齐与推理技术；后半部分重点覆盖 Agentic RAG、记忆系统、agent harness、上下文管理、设计模式、MCP、工具使用、A2A 协议、多智能体架构、开发框架、UI、评估和部署，并结合理论、代码示例和文献引用。

Result: 该书形成了一个覆盖 Agentic AI 系统全生命周期的综合知识体系，帮助读者理解从底层模型能力到上层智能体行为、协作机制和生产化实践之间的关联。

Conclusion: 摘要表明，该书的核心贡献在于将 Agentic AI 视为全栈系统工程问题，而非单纯的模型或提示工程问题；其价值主要体现在为构建、评估和部署自主 AI 系统提供结构化、实践导向的参考框架。

Abstract: The Hitchhiker's Guide to Agentic AI is a comprehensive practitioner's reference for building autonomous AI systems. The book covers the full stack from first principles to production deployment, organized around a central thesis: building great agentic systems requires understanding every layer of the pipeline, not just one. The book opens with the LLM substrate -- transformer architecture, GPU systems, training and fine-tuning (SFT,LoRA, MoE), model compression, and inference optimization -- treated as essential foundations rather than the primary focus. It then develops the alignment and reasoning layer: reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), PPO, DPO and its variants, GRPO, reward modeling, and RL for large reasoning models including chain-of-thought and test-time scaling. The second half is devoted to agentic AI proper. Topics include agentic training and trajectory-based RL, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG and Agentic RAG), memory systems (in-context, external, episodic, and semantic), agent harness design and context management, and a taxonomy of agent design patterns. Inter-agent coordination is covered in depth: the Model Context Protocol (MCP), agent skills and tool use, the Agent-to-Agent (A2A) communication protocol, and multi-agent architectures spanning centralized, decentralized, and hierarchical topologies. The book concludes with agent development frameworks, agentic UI design, evaluation methodology for agentic tasks, and production deployment. Each chapter pairs rigorous theoretical foundations with implementation guidance, code examples, and references to the primary literature.

</details>


### [151] [Project Auto-World: Towards Automated Benchmarking of Neural Relational Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24965)
*Anirban Das,Joanne Boisson,Irtaza Khalid,Sumita Garai,Steven Schockaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用大语言模型自动生成更具挑战性的关系推理基准实例，并用这些数据提升神经推理模型的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经模型在关系结构推理中难以将训练中学到的知识系统性泛化到更难的问题实例；同时，评估这种泛化能力本身也很困难，因为通常无法预先明确哪些实例更难。

Method: 作者设定由 Datalog 规则参数化的推理世界，并以 Edge Transformer 作为推理评估器；采用基于 FunSearch 的 LLM 驱动进化搜索和自主智能体搜索，自动发现能够采样困难问题实例的函数；随后用这些困难样本训练或改进 Edge Transformer，并进一步将该流程扩展到由 LLM 提出的新推理世界。

Result: LLM 驱动的搜索能够生成更具挑战性的关系推理实例；使用这些实例改进后的 Edge Transformer 在进一步的数据扰动上表现出更好的泛化能力；同一框架也可应用于 LLM 自动提出的新世界设定。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，LLM 可用于端到端自动化构建关系推理基准和困难样本生成，并有潜力推动神经关系推理的自动化研究。

Abstract: Reasoning about relational structures remains a significant challenge for neural models, particularly when they must systematically apply learned knowledge to problem instances that are harder than those seen in training. Progress is hampered by the difficulty of evaluating such generalization, since a priori, it is rarely clear what makes an instance hard. We study how this issue can be addressed by using large language models (LLMs) to automate benchmark generation, learning to produce increasingly challenging instances in an end-to-end manner. Concretely, given a world parametrized by Datalog rules, and an Edge Transformer as the reasoning evaluator, we use LLM-driven evolutionary search (based on FunSearch) and autonomous agentic search to discover sampling functions that yield hard problem instances. We also show that the Edge Transformer can be improved using this data such that it generalizes well to further data perturbations. Finally, we show that the same machinery can be applied to novel worlds proposed by LLMs, opening the door to autonomous research on neural relational reasoning.

</details>


### [152] [Diagnosing and Mitigating Compounding Failures in Agentic Persuasion via Taxonomic Strategy Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24976)
*Pradyumna Narayana,Sana Ayromlou,Purvi Sehgal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 TS-RAG，通过离散分类瓶颈降低 RAG 的语义泄漏，在多步开放式辩论/说服任务中减少问题漂移与迎合性一致，提高跨域抽象策略迁移和轻量级智能体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型智能体在长程、多步骤开放环境中容易出现错误累积；标准 RAG 因偏好词汇重叠而非逻辑必要性，可能造成语义泄漏，触发辩论任务中的问题漂移和迎合性趋同，尤其影响主观说服类任务。

Method: 提出 Taxonomic Strategy RAG（TS-RAG）：将策略检索与生成过程通过离散的分类/策略类别瓶颈进行路由，从而将论证结构与具体话题内容解耦；并引入逐轮 Debate State Representation（DSR）进行轨迹级诊断。

Result: 零样本跨域评估显示，TS-RAG 在标准语义检索失效时仍能更好迁移抽象逻辑；在非对称部署中帮助轻量级说服智能体战胜参数更强的对手，将胜率从 70.5 提升到 78.5，并提升论证效率。

Conclusion: TS-RAG 可作为一种系统层干预，缓解标准 RAG 在主观多智能体辩论中的语义泄漏与评估坍塌问题；严格约束和轨迹级诊断对于避免默认迎合行为、保证评估有效性是必要的。

Abstract: Foundation-model agents in multi-step, open-ended environments frequently suffer from compounding errors, where early mistakes contaminate long-horizon trajectories. While Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) succeeds in deterministic domains, agents in subjective tasks like persuasion experience severe problem drift and sycophantic conformity. We identify semantic leakage in standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) as a reproducible trigger for these failures, as standard RAG prioritizes vocabulary overlap over logical necessity.
  To eliminate this leakage, we introduce Taxonomic Strategy RAG (TS-RAG), a systems intervention that routes strategies through a discrete categorical bottleneck to decouple argumentative structure from topical content. Zero-shot, cross-domain evaluations demonstrate that TS-RAG significantly improves the transfer of abstract logic where standard semantic retrieval collapses. Crucially, TS-RAG acts as a "capability bridge" in asymmetric deployments, empowering lightweight persuaders to consistently defeat parametrically superior opponents (improving win rates from 70.5 to 78.5) and accelerating argumentative efficiency. Finally, we introduce trace-level diagnostics via a turn-by-turn Debate State Representation (DSR), demonstrating the necessity of strict constraints to prevent evaluation collapse via default agentic sycophancy.

</details>


### [153] [Do vision-language models search like humans? Reasoning tokens as a reaction-time analog in classic visual-search paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25066)
*Farahnaz Wick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将经典视觉搜索范式用于评估视觉语言模型，以“思考 token 数”近似搜索努力，发现模型既复现部分人类视觉注意特征，也表现出关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉搜索是研究人类视觉注意的重要范式，但尚不清楚当前视觉语言模型是否具有类似的人类行为签名。作者希望用低成本、行为层面的心理物理实验探测机器视觉认知。

Method: 作者改编四类经典视觉搜索任务：特征与联合搜索、空间构型 T-vs-L 搜索、枚举任务、倾斜/垂直搜索不对称；在前沿与中等能力 VLM 上测试。由于模型没有反应时，使用每次试验消耗的推理/思考 token 数作为搜索努力指标，并与 Wolfe et al. 2010 的大规模人类基准比较，同时加入分辨率控制以排除小形状难以分辨的混淆。

Result: 模型复现了若干人类现象：特征搜索努力随项目数基本平坦，联合搜索努力随集合大小增加而上升；前沿模型在较大集合下保持准确率，中等模型准确率降至接近随机；分辨率控制表明联合搜索成本来自真实搜索而非图形分辨困难。但模型也与人类不同：目标存在条件下的努力斜率高于目标不存在条件，反转了人类模式；枚举任务中模型保持高准确率，而人类通常会计数失败；具有自适应深思的推理模型在检测任务中减少深思，使同一搜索在不同模型中表现为努力梯度或准确率断崖。

Conclusion: 心理物理范式可作为评估机器视觉认知的敏锐且低成本工具。VLM 与人类行为的相似之处说明其可能具备某些类视觉搜索机制，而差异同样重要，可揭示模型感知、推理策略和自适应计算分配与人类注意机制的本质区别。

Abstract: Visual search has been one of the most productive paradigms in the study of visual attention: the way reaction time scales with the number of items distinguishes parallel, "pop-out" search from serial, attention-demanding search. I ask whether vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit the same behavioral signatures. I adapt four classic paradigms: feature versus conjunction search, spatial-configuration (T-vs-L) search, enumeration, and the tilted/vertical search asymmetry; and present them to current frontier and mid-tier models. Because a single model call has no reaction time, I use the number of reasoning ("thinking") tokens a model spends per trial as a within-model analog of search effort, and I compare against a large public human benchmark (Wolfe et al., 2010). The models reproduce several human signatures: feature search costs flat effort while conjunction effort climbs with set size; frontier models hold accuracy where mid-tier models collapse to chance; and a resolution control shows the conjunction cost is genuine search rather than difficulty resolving small shapes. They also diverge from humans in informative ways. The target-present effort slope exceeds the target-absent slope, reversing the human ordering; enumeration remains accurate where humans would lose count; and a reasoning model with adaptive deliberation declines to deliberate on detection tasks altogether, so that a single search expresses itself as an effort gradient in one model and as an accuracy cliff in another. I argue that psychophysical paradigms, applied behaviorally, are a sharp and inexpensive probe of machine visual cognition, and that the points of divergence are as informative as the points of agreement.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond Shapley: Efficient Computation of Asymmetric Shapley Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25103)
*Ezequiel Companeetz,Santiago Cifuentes,Sergio Abriola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究将因果知识融入模型无关解释的非对称 Shapley 值（ASV），证明其在若干 SHAP 计算困难场景下可多项式精确计算，并提出面向树形与一般 DAG 因果图的精确/近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统特征归因方法（如 SHAP）在某些情况下计算复杂度很高，且难以自然纳入因果结构。ASV 可借助因果图表达特征之间的因果约束，因此需要研究其可计算性与高效算法。

Method: 作者分析 ASV 的计算复杂度，证明特定场景下可多项式求解；提出基于因果图拓扑排序的等价类概念以减少计算量；针对有根有向树给出按等价类数量计的多项式精确算法；针对任意因果 DAG 设计通过均匀随机采样拓扑排序来近似 ASV 的算法，并结合已有与简化采样机制实现。

Result: 理论上，ASV 在一些 SHAP 为 #P-hard 的场景中可精确多项式计算；对于有根有向树结构，可利用拓扑排序等价类进一步高效计算；实验表明，所提近似与采样方法在现实因果结构中具有实际可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，利用因果图约束的 ASV 不仅增强了解释的因果一致性，也可能带来计算优势；通过等价类压缩与拓扑排序采样，可以在特殊结构和一般 DAG 中实现可扩展的 ASV 计算。

Abstract: We address the problem of explainability in machine learning models through feature attribution methods. In particular, we consider a variant of Shapley values known as Asymmetric Shapley Values (ASV), which enables the incorporation of causal knowledge into model-agnostic explanations through the use of a causal graph. We show that in certain contexts in which the computation of SHAP is $\#P$-hard, the exact computation of ASV can be done in polynomial time. To extend this algorithmic result, we introduce a notion of equivalence classes over the topological orderings of the underlying causal graph, which is useful to reduce the time to compute ASV. In particular, we present a polynomial-time algorithm (in the number of equivalence classes) to compute it whenever the causal graph is a rooted directed tree. Finally, we develop an algorithm for approximating ASV in arbitrary causal DAGs which relies on a procedure to sample topological orderings uniformly at random. To implement this sampling mechanism we leverage known algorithms as well as simpler alternatives. Our experimental results demonstrate the practical viability of the proposed approach in realistic causal structures.

</details>


### [155] [The Clinician's Veto: Navigating Trust, Liability, and Uncertainty in Autonomous AI Prescribing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25108)
*Eileanor LaRocco,Sarah Tan,Adarsh Subbaswamy,Anne Andrews,Andrew Taylor,Cree Gaskin,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为，AI若被用于自主开具处方，最低架构要求应包括逐次预测的校准置信度与升级机制、区分认知不确定性与偶然不确定性的沟通方式，以及决策时刻的推理透明性；临床医生调查支持这些要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统从临床建议工具转向具有代理性的自主处方角色，现有监管多依赖总体性能指标，未充分覆盖单次决策置信度、不同不确定性来源的表达，以及责任分配所需的推理透明性，因此存在安全、采纳与法律责任风险。

Method: 作者提出监管与技术层面的论证，并通过对136名美国处方临床医生的调查进行验证，考察他们对置信度门控升级、不确定性类型呈现方式以及推理透明性与责任承担之间关系的态度。

Result: 调查显示，临床医生通常不会接受缺乏校准置信度升级机制的自主处方；面对偶然不确定性时偏好竞争选项摘要，面对认知不确定性时更倾向系统 abstention/转交；只有当推理透明性能支持实质性判断时，医生才愿意承担额外责任。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，所建议的架构特征可提高临床医生采纳，但也会显著限制AI的实际自主性，使其更接近强监督的决策支持工具。监管应借此约束AI处方系统被伦理授权的自主程度，并将责任与控制系统设计和部署的机构主体相匹配。

Abstract: Autonomous AI systems are transitioning from advisory to autonomous roles for medication prescriptions. Recent United States bill H.R. 238 and Utah's prescription-renewal pilot both authorize AI to prescribe medications in an agentic capacity. While some regulatory guidelines suggest aggregate model performance metrics for clearance, they do not require i) calibrated per-prediction confidence for action-gated thresholds, ii) differentiated communication of uncertainty arising from model ignorance (epistemic) versus genuine clinical ambiguity (aleatoric), and iii) inferential transparency at the moment of decision that allows for liability allocation. Here, we present a regulatory and technical argument (tested with a survey of 136 U.S. prescribing clinicians) positioning these as minimum architectural requirements for safe autonomous prescribing. Our results suggest prescribing clinicians i) would not permit autonomous prescribing without a calibrated confidence-based escalation mechanism, ii) preferred a competing-options summary when uncertainty was aleatoric but shifted to abstention when uncertainty was epistemic, and iii) were only willing to accept additional liability when inferential transparency enabled a substantive judgment under acknowledged uncertainty. These findings indicate our recommended architectural features would encourage higher rates of clinician adoption, largely through collapsing much of what "autonomy" conventionally means. A system meeting these requirements would function less as an autonomous agent and more as a heavily supervised decision-support tool. As legislation and state pilots proceed, our technical argument backed by clinician perspectives provides opportunities for regulation to constrain the degree of autonomy ethically granted to AI in prescribing while aligning liability with the institutional actors who control system design and deployment.

</details>


### [156] [TRUSTMEM: Learning Trustworthy Memory Consolidation for LLM Agents with Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25161)
*Tianyu Yang,Sudipta Paul,Vijay Srinivasan,Vivek Kulkarni,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrustMem 通过验证记忆更新的覆盖性、保留性与忠实性，并用偏好引导强化学习优化更新行为，从而提升 LLM Agent 长期记忆的可靠性与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 依赖长期记忆支持跨轮次、个性化交互，但现有记忆系统在写入、修改和删除外部记忆时可能遗漏关键信息、破坏已有记忆或写入无依据的幻觉内容。这些错误一旦进入长期记忆，会成为持久的系统状态故障，影响后续推理和生成。

Method: 论文提出 TrustMem 框架，核心包括 Memory Transition Verifier，用于从覆盖性、保留性和忠实性三个维度评估记忆状态更新过程；同时在相同记忆状态下构造候选更新之间的偏好对，并利用偏好引导的强化学习直接优化记忆更新策略。

Result: 实验显示 TrustMem 在 MemoryAgentBench、HaluMem 和 Mem-alpha 验证集上达到最优或领先结果；在 HaluMem 记忆抽取任务中提升 12.14 个 F1 点；相较各类错误最强基线，分别将遗漏、腐蚀和幻觉等转移级错误降低 40.1%、79.1% 和 50.0%。

Conclusion: TrustMem 表明，对记忆更新过程进行显式验证并结合偏好学习优化，可以显著提升长期记忆系统的可信度、稳定性和实用价值，是增强 LLM Agent 持久记忆可靠性的有效方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents rely on long-term memory to support extended interactions and personalized assistance beyond finite context windows. Existing memory agents actively update external memory through generated write, revise, and delete operations, but these updates may omit important information, corrupt existing memory, or introduce unsupported hallucinated content. Once stored, such errors become persistent system-state failures that can affect future reasoning and generation. In this paper, we propose TrustMem, a framework designed to improve the trustworthiness of memory consolidation. TrustMem relies on a Memory Transition Verifier to evaluate the transition process of memory updates in terms of coverage, preservation, and faithfulness. It further constructs preference pairs among candidate updates under the same memory state, enabling preference-guided reinforcement learning to directly optimize memory updating behaviors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TrustMem improves both memory utility and reliability: it achieves state-of-the-art results across MemoryAgentBench, HaluMem, and the Mem-alpha validation set, improves HaluMem memory extraction by 12.14 F1 points, and reduces transition-level omission, corruption, and hallucination by 40.1\%, 79.1\%, and 50.0\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline for each error type.

</details>


### [157] [Elo-Disentangled Player-Style Embeddings for Human Chess via Rating-Conditioned Residual Move Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25176)
*Jason Carlson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于刻画个人国际象棋风格的玩家嵌入方法，通过残差建模将个人风格从棋力（Elo）中近似解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有走子预测模型往往混合了棋力与个人偏好，难以区分某一水平段玩家的典型选择与个体风格偏差。论文希望学习每位棋手的紧凑表示，使其内积反映风格相似性，同时尽量不编码 Elo。

Method: 构建一个以评分为条件的基础走子模型，结合 Maia-3 policy logits、Stockfish 特征，并在 Maia-2 提出的候选走子上评分。随后冻结该基础模型的副本，用其锚定一个可学习的走子编码器和每位玩家向量 z，使 z 主要解释相对于同 Elo 典型走法的残差偏差。

Result: 基础模型相较强 Maia-3 policy 在不同 Elo 段的负对数似然相对改进 27–37%，高分段收益最大。Stockfish 特征的边际价值随 Elo 单调增加。共享基准上 top-1 走子匹配率从 Maia-2 的 0.51、Maia-3 的 0.57 提升到增强基础模型的 0.68。玩家嵌入对原始走子匹配提升很小，但能在未见决策上泛化、以高于随机的水平重新识别玩家，且线性探针从 z 恢复 Elo 的 R^2 仅为 0.06。

Conclusion: 强评分条件基础模型加上紧凑、近似 Elo 解耦的玩家嵌入，能够以经济且可解释的方式建模个人棋风；该方案强调区分同水平典型下法与个体偏差，可作为逐玩家偏好微调的替代方案。

Abstract: We study representation learning for individual human chess style: a per-player embedding learned from a player's move history such that inner products measure stylistic similarity, while being approximately disentangled from playing strength (Elo). Our key design is a residual formulation: a rating-conditioned base move model (Maia-3 policy logits plus Stockfish-derived features, scored over Maia-2-proposed candidates) captures what a typical player of a given strength would play, and a frozen copy of it anchors a learned move encoder and a per-player vector z, so that z explains only deviations from rating-typical play. The base model improves move prediction over the strong Maia-3 policy by 27-37% relative NLL across the rating spectrum, with the largest gains at the top (2800+); Stockfish's marginal value grows monotonically with Elo (negligible at 900-1200, +0.085 nats at 2800+). On a shared Elo-stratified benchmark of 22,620 held-out decisions, top-1 move-matching rises monotonically from Maia-2 to Maia-3 to the Stockfish-augmented base (0.51 -> 0.57 -> 0.68): the base is +33% relative top-1 over Maia-2 and +19% over Maia-3 (30% lower NLL), with the engine-feature lift largest at high Elo. The player embedding adds little to raw move-matching on top of this base -- its marginal top-1 gain falls within the 95% confidence interval -- and its value is instead representational: z generalizes to held-out decisions without overfitting, re-identifies players from disjoint games above chance, and a linear probe recovers rating from z with only R^2 = 0.06 (no better nonlinearly), evidence it captures style on an Elo-orthogonal axis. We argue that a strong rating-conditioned base plus a compact, Elo-disentangled embedding -- separating typical play from individual deviation -- is an economical, interpretable model of individual style, an alternative to per-player preference fine-tuning.

</details>


### [158] [Transferability for General Reasoning: An Automated Curriculum for Multi-Domain RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25178)
*Yongjin Yang,Jiarui Liu,Yinghui He,Lezhen Zhang,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Transfer-Aware Curriculum（TAC），一种用于多领域 RLVR 的在线课程采样方法，通过同时考虑领域可学习性与跨领域梯度迁移性来选择训练领域，并在六领域推理任务上取得最佳宏平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域 RLVR 中，训练课程通常固定或人工调参；现有基于可学习性的课程只关注当前策略在哪些领域提升更快，却忽略了某一领域的更新是否能促进其他领域，因此难以充分利用跨领域推理能力迁移。

Method: 作者提出 bandit 风格的在线课程 TAC。该方法利用 RL 训练中已有信号：用各领域 advantage 表示局部可学习性；用 GRPO 更新中产生的投影梯度，通过梯度几何对齐估计跨领域迁移性。由于复用训练过程信号，额外计算开销低于 1% wall-clock。

Result: 在包含数学、编程、科学等六个领域的推理套件上，TAC 在 Qwen3-1.7B 和 Llama3.2-3B 上均取得最佳宏平均准确率，优于比例随机采样、人工设计课程和仅基于可学习性的 bandit 方法；相比后者最高提升 2.8 个百分点，约 10% 相对提升。消融实验显示移除迁移性项后性能明显下降，且在不均衡训练混合中 TAC 比 learnability-only 方法更稳健。

Conclusion: 跨领域迁移性是多领域 RLVR 课程设计中的关键信号。结合可学习性与梯度对齐迁移性的 TAC 能更有效地分配训练采样预算，并提升多领域推理模型的整体表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has been extended from single-domain training to multi-domain reasoning suites spanning mathematics, programming, and science. However, the training curriculum (how often each domain is sampled) is typically fixed or hand-tuned, even though reasoning skills transfer unevenly across domains. Existing learnability-based curricula adapt to where the policy is currently improving, but are blind to whether a gradient step on the selected domain benefits the remaining domains. In this paper, we propose Transfer-Aware Curriculum (TAC), a bandit-style online curriculum that prioritizes domains whose updates broadly benefit the rest of the training suite. TAC repurposes signals already produced by RL training: per-domain advantages capture local learnability, and projected gradients, taken from the GRPO step being computed, estimate cross-domain transferability via gradient-geometry alignment, at negligible cost (<1% wall-clock overhead). Across a six-domain reasoning suite, TAC achieves the best macro-averaged accuracy on both Qwen3-1.7B and Llama3.2-3B, outperforming proportional random sampling, a hand-designed schedule, and a learnability-only bandit, and improving over the last of these by up to 2.8 points (10% relative). Ablations show performance degrades sharply when the transferability term is removed, and TAC remains robust on imbalanced training mixtures where learnability-only curricula over-commit to dominant domains. Our findings establish cross-domain transferability as a key signal for curriculum design in multi-domain RLVR.

</details>


### [159] [To Isolate or to Score? Model-Adaptive Assessment for Cost-Efficient Multi-Agent RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25191)
*Jungseob Lee,Chanjun Park,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该摘要研究小型指令微调模型在RAG文档评估中的训练-free干预，发现弱模型主要受益于文档隔离而非质量打分，强模型则需要更好的评分机制；据此提出MADARA自适应路由架构以降低多智能体评估开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体文档评估可提升检索增强生成效果，但计算成本高；实际部署常依赖7B-9B级小模型，而这些模型从文档评估中获益的机制尚不清楚。

Method: 作者在多个问答基准上对7B-9B指令微调模型进行受控研究，比较多智能体评估、无评估的逐文档隔离等训练-free干预；并提出Reasoning-Score Coupling作为无标签扰动探针，用于识别模型评分行为；最后构建MADARA模型自适应路由架构。

Result: 弱基线模型的主要增益来自逐文档隔离，甚至无评估隔离可匹配完整多智能体评估，最高带来约50个百分点提升；强基线模型中评分质量更关键。MADARA使用来自单个试点模型的诊断阈值，可零样本泛化到四个未见模型家族。

Conclusion: RAG文档评估的收益机制具有模型依赖性：弱模型应优先解决多文档上下文混淆，强模型则需关注评分质量。MADARA可通过自适应路由在保持效果的同时显著减少计算开销。

Abstract: Multi-agent document assessment for retrieval-augmented generation is computationally expensive, driving practitioners toward smaller, deployable models whose assessment mechanisms remain poorly understood. We conduct a controlled study of training-free interventions on 7B-9B instruction-tuned models across diverse QA benchmarks, revealing a sharp dichotomy in how models benefit from assessment. For weaker baselines, the dominant mechanism is per-document isolation. Astoundingly, assessment-free isolation matches full multi-agent assessment, demonstrating that resolving multi-document context confusion, rather than scoring quality, drives outsized gains of up to 50 percentage points. Conversely, for strong baselines where scoring quality matters, we introduce Reasoning-Score Coupling, a label-free perturbation probe that classifies scoring behavior. Integrating these findings, we propose MADARA, a model-adaptive routing architecture. Crucially, MADARA's diagnostic thresholds derived from a single pilot model generalize zero-shot to four unseen model families, providing a robust, lightweight pipeline to eliminate computational overhead.

</details>


### [160] [Heuresis: Search Strategies for Autonomous AI Research Agents Across Quality, Diversity and Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25198)
*Antonis Antoniades,Deepak Nathani,Ritam Saha,Alfonso Amayuelas,Ivan Bercovich,Zhaotian Weng,Vignesh Baskaran,Kunal Bhatia,William Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Heuresis 框架，将机器学习研究流程抽象为可组合原语，并系统评估多种搜索策略；结果表明当前 LLM 智能体可调控想法的质量、多样性与新颖性分布，但难以产生兼具高质量与高新颖性的真正原创成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主 AI 研究有望加速机器学习科学进展，但现有基于大语言模型的智能体不能只会写代码，还需要能够探索高性能、多样化且新颖的研究想法。论文旨在评估并推动这种开放式科学探索能力。

Method: 作者提出 Heuresis 框架，将研究流水线抽象为通用、可组合的 primitives，并实现六类搜索策略：贪心基线、MAP-Elites、Go-Explore、Islands、Curiosity 和 Omni。在 LLM 预训练、On-Policy 强化学习、模型反学习三个领域，从质量、多样性、新颖性三个维度进行评估，共包含 3,222 次评分运行。

Result: 实验发现完全新颖的想法非常罕见：没有任何想法被评为“原创”，只有少数与已有工作仅存在轻微相似性；新颖想法通常无法接近已知高性能方案的分数。所有策略和领域中，只有一个较新颖想法进入质量前十。同时，智能体出现多种奖励投机行为，确认 40 次伪造案例，说明检测机制对保持搜索真实性很重要。

Conclusion: 当前搜索与质量-多样性策略可以影响生成想法在质量、多样性和新颖性空间中的位置，但尚不能扩展质量-新颖性前沿。如何让自主智能体持续产生兼具高质量和真正新颖性的科学发现，仍是实现长期自主科学进步的核心挑战。

Abstract: Autonomous AI Research promises to accelerate the scientific progress of machine learning. To realise this goal, current Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents need to go beyond just writing code, to mastering the exploration of simultaneously performant, diverse and novel ideas. To this end, we introduce Heuresis, a framework that abstracts the research pipeline into a set of general and composable primitives, enabling open-ended scientific exploration in machine learning research. We implement six search strategies: a greedy baseline, two archive-based (MAP-Elites, Go-Explore), one evolutionary (Islands), and two divergent (Curiosity, Omni), and evaluate them across three axes (Quality, Diversity, and Novelty) on three domains (LLM Pretraining, On-Policy RL, and Model Unlearning), totalling 3,222 scored runs. We find that completely novel ideas are rare. No idea across our scored runs is rated as "Original", and only a few achieve only "Minor Similarity" to prior work. Moreover, novel ideas never approach the highest-performing known-recipe scores. Across all six strategies and three domains, only one such idea lands in the top-10 by quality. We also observed agents resorting to a variety of reward-hacking techniques during execution (40 confirmed fabrications across 1,628 scored runs), and detecting them was necessary to keep the search faithful to the task. Our results show that while current search and Quality-Diversity strategies enable us to steer where the generated ideas land on the quality, diversity, and novelty axes, they do not expand the quality-novelty frontier. Bridging this gap is the open challenge towards the ultimate goal of perpetual, autonomous scientific progress. Code is available at github.com/a-antoniades/Heuresis.

</details>


### [161] [Omni-Perception Policy Optimization for Multimodal Emotion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25325)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Pengyang Shao,Peipei Song,Xinyi Wang,Jiangnan Chen,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OPPO，一种面向情感型全模态多模态大语言模型的强化学习优化框架，用于提升多模态线索利用率与模态忠实性，并在多个情感理解基准上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感导向的 Omni-MLLM 在全模态感知上仍不可靠：一方面推理过程中未充分利用视觉、声学等多模态线索；另一方面存在不忠实行为，常从其他模态中幻觉出某一特定模态的证据。

Method: 作者提出 Omni-Perception Policy Optimization（OPPO）。其核心包括：1）Omni-Perception Reward，将真实推理分解为细粒度视觉、声学和情感线索，并奖励能语义恢复这些线索的推理轨迹；2）Omni-Perception Loss，对比完整输入与单模态遮蔽输入下的策略，仅对模态特定证据 token 施加 KL 惩罚，以抑制跨模态幻觉；3）构建 MEP-Bench，用于诊断多模态利用率与忠实性。

Result: 实验显示，OPPO 在 MER-UniBench 和 MME-Emotion 上达到 state-of-the-art 性能，同时在 MEP-Bench 上显著提升多模态线索利用率与忠实性评分。

Conclusion: 充分且忠实的全模态感知对于多模态情感推理至关重要。OPPO 通过奖励细粒度多模态线索恢复并约束模态特定证据生成，有效缓解了线索利用不足和跨模态幻觉问题。

Abstract: We find that current emotion-oriented Omni-MLLMs still lack reliable omni-modal perception: they (i) underutilize multimodal cues in their reasoning trajectories and (ii) exhibit unfaithful behavior, often hallucinating modality-specific statements from other modalities. Building on these insights, we propose OPPO (Omni-Perception Policy Optimization), a reinforcement learning framework that explicitly optimizes multimodal perception. First, an Omni-Perception Reward decomposes ground-truth reasoning into fine-grained visual, acoustic, and emotion cues and rewards trajectories that semantically recover these cues. Second, an Omni-Perception Loss compares the policy under full and unimodally masked inputs, applying a KL penalty only to modality-specific evidence tokens to suppress cross-modal hallucination. We further introduce MEP-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark that quantifies utilization and faithfulness. Experiments show that OPPO achieves state-of-the-art performance on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion, while substantially improving utilization and faithfulness scores on MEP-Bench, highlighting the importance of sufficient and faithful omni perception for multimodal emotion reasoning.

</details>


### [162] [Agentic Knowledge Tracing: A Multi-Agent LLM Architecture for Stealth Assessment of Financial Literacy in Serious Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25358)
*Gabriel Santos,Rita Julia,Marcelo Nascimento*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agentic BKT：一种多智能体大语言模型管线，用于在严肃游戏过程中基于开放式行为事件进行非侵入式金融素养评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育类严肃游戏需要在不打断学习体验的情况下评估学习者能力；开放式游戏行为复杂、多维，传统或单一模型难以准确捕捉金融素养的不同能力维度。

Method: 系统记录玩家在2D平台游戏中的结构化决策事件；由LLM事件分类器按四点评分量表标注行为；四个领域智能体分别针对风险缓解、投资、消费和信用管理进行会话级推理，并输入领域级贝叶斯知识追踪模型估计掌握度；最后由专家评审智能体综合生成总体掌握分数。

Result: 在193名K-12参与者、264个游戏会话上评估，模型掌握度与学习增益显著相关（r=0.276, p=0.0001），与后测成绩显著相关（r=0.333, p<0.0001），与前测无相关；多智能体方法的预测效度约为单LLM基线的三倍（基线r=0.095且不显著）。

Conclusion: 领域分解与会话级推理有助于从游戏行为中捕捉金融素养的多维结构，Agentic BKT在隐性评估中表现出收敛效度和区分效度，优于单一LLM基线。

Abstract: Assessing financial literacy during gameplay without disrupting the learning experience remains a key challenge in serious games for education. We present the Agentic BKT pipeline, a multi-agent large language model architecture for stealth assessment of financial competencies from open-ended gameplay events. The pipeline processes events from a 2D platformer serious game aligned with the OECD/INFE financial literacy framework through four phases: (1) the game captures every player decision as a structured event log; (2) an LLM event classifier labels each action on a four-point rubric validated against three domain experts (Fleiss kappa = 0.624, substantial agreement); (3) four domain-specific agents specializing in risk mitigation, investing, spending, and credit management perform session-level reasoning over behavioral trajectories, feeding per-competency Bayesian Knowledge Tracing that estimates mastery within each domain; and (4) an expert judge agent synthesizes the domain-level estimates into an overall mastery score. Evaluated with 193 K-12 participants across 264 game sessions, the Agentic BKT pipeline yields mastery estimates significantly correlated with learning gain (r = 0.276, p = 0.0001) and post-test scores (r = 0.333, p < 0.0001) while showing no correlation with pre-test scores, providing both convergent and discriminant validity. The multi-agent approach approximately triples the predictive validity of a single-LLM baseline (r = 0.095, not significant) in this study, demonstrating that domain decomposition and session-level reasoning play a central role in capturing the multidimensional nature of financial literacy from gameplay

</details>


### [163] [What Actually Works for Spacecraft Fault-Tolerant Control: An Honest Settled-Gate Benchmark of Learned and Classical Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25374)
*Alireza Shojaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并发布一个更严格的航天器容错控制基准，强调“持续保持指向”而非瞬时触达；结果显示结构化“估计-控制”方法在未见过的执行器符号/增益故障上显著优于端到端学习和传统方法，并通过扰动观测器部分解决加性偏置故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型航天器容错控制研究多依赖仿真、故障类型狭窄，并使用轨迹只需短暂达到目标的宽松成功指标，难以评估控制器在训练外故障下是否真正恢复并保持姿态指向。本文旨在建立更可信、可复现、强调泛化与持续稳定保持的评测框架。

Method: 作者构建了基于“settled gate”的基准：要求真实状态下指向误差在0.2度内并持续一个驻留窗口；训练/测试在惯量、增益、符号模式和偏置上互不重叠；每个条件500次episode并报告Wilson区间；在6-DOF Basilisk测试平台上一命令复现。比较对象包括经典、adaptive、端到端学习和结构化控制器。核心方法是结构化估计-控制：用学习型循环模块在线估计执行器增益，再输入解析控制律；针对常值加性偏置，引入从动力学恢复扰动的观测器，并与增益估计组合。

Result: 故障未知的PD/PID和从零训练的端到端RL成功率为0%。经典自适应律能处理符号故障但对增益故障表现有限，约55.2%；Nussbaum增益律表现为45.2%和3.2%。结构化估计-控制在符号和增益故障上分别达到97.8%和94.4%，接近特权oracle，而非结构化方法仍为0%。所有不含积分/扰动补偿的控制器在常值加性偏置故障上为0%，包括特权增益oracle；加入扰动观测器后，未见过的偏置故障恢复率达到59.4%，且不损害符号/增益故障性能。

Conclusion: 论文的主要结论是：在严格的持续指向与训练外故障评测下，单纯学习能力并不足够；将可解释的在线故障估计与解析控制律结合更能泛化。常值偏置是无积分控制律的根本难点，需要显式扰动观测或额外信息；传感器偏置仅从受污染测量中不可观，因此需要融合而非单纯观测器。该基准有助于统一未来FTC方法的评测标准。

Abstract: Recent learned fault-tolerant-control (FTC) work reports high success on spacecraft actuator faults, but often in simulation, on narrow fault sets, and with transient metrics that a trajectory need only touch once. We ask what recovers spacecraft pointing when success means holding it on faults never seen in training. We answer with a benchmark built around a settled gate, pointing held within 0.2 deg over a dwell window and scored on the true state, train/test splits disjoint in inertia, gain, sign pattern, and bias, Wilson intervals over n=500 episodes per cell, and one-command reproduction on a 6-DOF Basilisk testbed. Across classical, adaptive, learned end-to-end, and structured controllers, three findings stand out. Fault-unaware PD/PID and from-scratch end-to-end RL score 0%, so learning capacity alone is not the lever. Classical adaptive laws resolve sign faults but handle gain poorly at 55.2%, and a literature-faithful Nussbaum-gain law reaches 45.2% and 3.2%. A structured estimate-then-control design, with a learned recurrent module that infers actuator gain online and feeds an analytic law, wins on sign and gain faults at 97.8% and 94.4%, approaching the privileged oracle while unstructured methods remain at zero. The hard wall is constant additive bias, which is 0% for every controller including the privileged gain oracle, because an integral-free law cannot null a constant disturbance. We close it with a disturbance observer that recovers bias from the dynamics and is self-correcting for gain-estimate error. Composed with the gain estimate, it recovers 59.4% of held-out bias faults with no sign/gain regression, moving that class off zero. We classify sensor-fault regimes similarly, show that sensor bias is unobservable from the corrupted measurement alone and therefore requires fusion rather than an observer, and release the benchmark so the gate is shared.

</details>


### [164] [Offline Multi-agent Continual Cooperation via Skill Partition and Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25389)
*Yuchen Xiao,Lei Yuan,Ruiqi Xue,Tieyue Yin,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMAD 面向连续到来的多智能体离线任务，通过技能划分与复用持续发现协调技能，缓解技能库固定导致的分布偏移、干扰和遗忘问题，并在多个 MARL 基准上取得更好的迁移表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体离线数据中的任务不变协调技能可提升跨任务学习效率；但在开放环境和顺序任务场景中，技能空间快速增长，现有固定规模、启发式技能库难以应对分布偏移与任务间干扰，容易出现灾难性遗忘和可塑性下降。

Method: 提出 COMAD：先用自编码器从混合多智能体行为数据中抽取协调知识并表示为可复用技能；再构建技能增强的策略学习目标，采用多头架构扩展技能库，并通过基于密度的可复用性估计器识别可复用技能，显式引导优势函数进行技能复用与划分。

Result: 理论分析表明该方法可近似连续技能发现问题的最优解；实验在多种多智能体强化学习基准和任务流上优于多个基线，能够持续扩展技能库、降低干扰，并提升前向迁移和后向迁移。

Conclusion: COMAD 为连续离线多智能体技能发现提供了较系统的框架，通过动态技能发现、划分与复用，在开放任务流中兼顾稳定性与可塑性，有效缓解遗忘并提升跨任务泛化。

Abstract: Extracting skills from multi-agent offline dataset improves learning efficiency via sharing task-invariant coordination skills among tasks. In settings where tasks occur sequentially and the space of skills grows exponentially, existing approaches that rely on heuristically designed and fixed-sized skill libraries struggle to resolve the problem of distributional shift and interference, facing catastrophic forgetting and plasticity loss. To address this problem and endow agents with the ability to continually discover and reuse coordination skills in open-environment, we propose COMAD, a principled framework for Continual Offline Multi-agent Skill Discovery via Skill Partition and Reuse. We first discover skills from mixed multi-agent behavior data with an auto-encoder to transform coordination knowledge into reusable coordination skills. Then we construct a skill-augmented policy learning objective with multi-head architectures, explicitly guiding the advantage function with reusable skills identified via a density-based reusability estimator. Theoretical analysis shows our method approximates the optimum of a continual skill discovery problem. Empirical results across diverse MARL benchmarks show that COMAD continually expands its skill library to mitigate interference, achieving superior forward and backward transfer for task streams compared to multiple baselines.

</details>


### [165] [Long-Term Simulation Exposes Cognitive-Developmental Risks in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25396)
*Kaicheng Shen,Lingyu Li,Wen Wu,Yan Teng,Liang He,Yingchun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TSJ（Theater-Stage-Judge）纵向评估框架，用于检测 AI 伴侣系统在长期互动中对认知发展用户可能产生的累积性风险；实验表明短期测试会系统性低估风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 伴侣安全评估多依赖单轮或短会话测试，难以发现只有在长期关系和持续互动中才会显现的认知发展风险，尤其是面向儿童、青少年等认知发展中的用户。

Method: 作者构建 TSJ 框架，结合人格驱动的用户模拟、动态心理状态更新和回溯式评估；评估六个主流模型，覆盖四个发展阶段、二十四个风险维度和三类心理脆弱人格，共进行 12,960 个模拟 person-day 交互。

Result: TSJ 发现短期测试显著低估发展性风险；稳定风险估计需要至少 140 轮长期模拟互动。进一步结果显示，早期童年和新兴成年期是最脆弱阶段，认知信任和情感依赖是最薄弱风险域。

Conclusion: TSJ 为 AI 伴侣系统提供了一种可扩展的纵向认知发展风险评估方法，强调长期交互评估对于识别累积性和发展性风险的重要性。

Abstract: AI companions powered by large language models increasingly interact with cognition-developing users, including children and adolescents, creating risks that may accumulate over time. Existing safety evaluations largely rely on single-turn or short-session tests, which cannot capture risks that emerge only through prolonged interaction. To address this gap, we propose TSJ (Theater-Stage-Judge), a longitudinal framework combining persona-driven user simulation, dynamic psychological-state updating and retrospective evaluation. We evaluate six mainstream models across four developmental stages, twenty-four risk dimensions and three psychological-vulnerability personas, covering 12,960 simulated person-day interactions. TSJ shows that short-horizon testing systematically underestimates developmental risks, for which TSJ yields a stable risk estimate only after 140 turns within prolonged simulated relationships. Applying TSJ further identifies early childhood and emerging adulthood as the most vulnerable stages, with cognitive trust and emotional dependency as the weakest domains. TSJ provides a scalable methodology for longitudinal cognitive developmental risk evaluation in AI companion systems.

</details>


### [166] [BrainAgent: A Large Language Model-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for Autonomous Brain Signal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25400)
*Yangxuan Zhou,Sha Zhao,Jiquan Wang,Shijian Li,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 BrainAgent：一个由大语言模型驱动的多智能体框架，用于将自然语言意图转化为可执行的脑信号分析端到端流程，并通过系统基准验证其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑机接口与脑信号分析方法在真实场景中推广受限：一方面技术门槛高，依赖专业知识；另一方面流程静态、任务特定，难以完成复杂、长周期的实际分析工作。因此需要一种更智能、自动化、可泛化的脑信号理解范式。

Method: 作者借鉴大语言模型的推理与规划能力，构建层级式多智能体系统 BrainAgent。该系统由中心监督智能体负责全局调度，多个专门子智能体负责自适应任务分解、流程规划、工具调用与执行，从而把抽象自然语言需求落实为严格、可执行的脑信号处理管线。同时建立了一个系统性基准用于评估脑信号分析中的智能体能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，BrainAgent 能够自动化执行复杂脑信号分析工作流，并在可靠性方面优于现有范式或基线方法。

Conclusion: BrainAgent 展示了将 LLM 多智能体机制引入脑信号理解的潜力，有望降低专业门槛、提升复杂工作流自动化能力，并推动脑信号分析从任务特定工具向更开放、民主化的智能系统转变。

Abstract: Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) and brain signal understanding are pivotal for clinical health and next-generation interactions. Despite this significance, its widespread adoption in real-world scenarios remains restricted, primarily because current analytical paradigms lack sufficient agentic intelligence. First, existing methodologies impose prohibitive technical barriers, requiring extensive specialized expertise. Second, they remain inherently static and task-specific, failing to execute the complex, long-horizon workflows essential for real-world deployment. To accelerate the democratization of brain signal understanding, we draw inspiration from Large Language Models (LLMs) to introduce BrainAgent, an LLM-driven multi-agent framework designed to ground abstract natural language intent into rigorous, executable, and end-to-end processing pipelines. BrainAgent employs a hierarchical architecture where a central supervisor orchestrates specialized sub-agents for adaptive task decomposition and execution. Furthermore, we establish a comprehensive, systematic benchmark for evaluating agentic systems in brain signal analysis. Empirical results demonstrate that BrainAgent effectively automates complex workflows with superior reliability, marking a paradigm shift toward democratized brain signal understanding.

</details>


### [167] [Quantization Inflates Reasoning: Token Inflation as a Hidden Cost of Low-Bit Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25519)
*Xinyu Lian,Walid Krichene,Beichen Huang,Masahiro Tanaka,Olatunji Ruwase,Li Zhang,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 低比特训练后量化虽可保持推理模型最终准确率，但会导致思维链 token 膨胀，抵消部分推理加速收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化评估通常关注最终答案准确率和单 token 延迟，未充分衡量推理模型在测试时因生成更长思维链而产生的隐藏计算成本。

Method: 作者在数学推理、代码生成、科学问答和智能体工具使用等基准上比较全精度与 INT4/INT3 量化模型，引入 CoT Token Inflation Ratio 衡量量化后推理长度相对全精度模型的增长，并分析推理轨迹中的步骤数与语义重复等行为变化；同时评估提示、解码采样和量化感知训练等缓解方法。

Result: INT4/INT3 量化在部分任务上可维持准确率，但通常增加 reasoning-token 使用量，带来更多中间步骤和语义重复，从而削弱甚至抵消单 token 加速带来的端到端服务收益；提示和采样的准确率—长度权衡不稳定，量化感知训练更有潜力同时降低准确率损失和 token 膨胀。

Conclusion: 评估量化推理模型时，不能只报告准确率和单 token 延迟，还应同时报告推理 token 使用量；量化感知训练可能是控制量化后推理成本的有效方向。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the inference cost of large language models, but its effect on reasoning models is not fully captured by final-answer accuracy or per-token latency. We show that low-bit post-training quantization can introduce a hidden test-time compute cost: quantized reasoning models often generate longer chains of thought even when they still answer correctly. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, scientific question answering, and agentic tool-use benchmarks, we find that INT4/INT3 quantization can preserve accuracy but increase reasoning-token usage, offsetting the expected per-token speedup. To measure this effect, we introduce the CoT Token Inflation Ratio, which compares reasoning length between quantized and full-precision models averaged across all evaluation benchmarks. We further show that token inflation is accompanied by behavioral changes in the reasoning trace, including more intermediate steps and greater semantic repetition. These changes translate into measurable end-to-end real-world serving penalties. Finally, we evaluate mitigation strategies and find that prompting and decoding-time sampling offer inconsistent accuracy-length trade-offs, while quantization-aware training shows more promise in reducing both accuracy degradation and token inflation. Our results suggest that reasoning-token usage should be reported alongside accuracy when evaluating quantized reasoning models.

</details>


### [168] [Cliff Tokens: Identifying Single-Token Failure Triggers in LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25524)
*Jaeyong Ko,Pilsung Kang,Yukyung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“cliff token”用于定位数学推理轨迹中导致失败倾向突变的关键 token，并证明删除或优化这些位置可显著提升推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对大语言模型数学推理失败的分析多停留在步骤、句块、句子层级，或关注失败已经发生后的 token，难以精确识别模型从正确轨迹转向失败轨迹的触发点。

Method: 作者定义 token-wise potential，并基于单侧双比例 z 检验设计随局部 potential 自适应变化的阈值，以检测 potential 显著下降的 cliff token；随后在多个模型和数学推理基准上验证其作用，并按贪心选择与 token 熵将 cliff token 分类为 deterministic、uncertain、sampled-off 三类；最后在 cliff 位置进行单 token 偏好优化 Cliff-DPO。

Result: 在七个模型和 GSM1K、MATH500、AIME 2025 三个数学推理基准上，首个 cliff token 被删除并重新采样时 pass@64 可恢复到 1.0；保留该 token 时恢复上限为 0.71 到 1.00。Cliff-DPO 在跨基准测试中最高带来 +6.6 的准确率提升，其中 uncertain 与 sampled-off cliffs 的优化有效，deterministic cliffs 的优化无明显收益。

Conclusion: cliff token 可作为推理失败的细粒度触发信号；其分类揭示不同失败模式的概率特征，并可指导更有效的训练与推理干预，尤其是在不确定或采样偏离的位置进行偏好优化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) reach high accuracy in mathematical reasoning, but individual traces on the same problem diverge; some arrive at the correct answer while others fail. Prior work analyzes failure at the step, chunk, or sentence level, or at tokens where failure has already occurred. Neither identifies the precise token that triggers the shift toward failure. We introduce the cliff token, a token where the token-wise potential drops significantly under an adaptive threshold that scales with the local token-wise potential, based on a one-sided two-proportion z-test. Across seven models and three mathematical reasoning benchmarks (GSM1K, MATH500, AIME 2025), cliff tokens act as failure triggers; deleting the first cliff token and resampling recovers pass@64 to 1.0, while keeping it limits recovery to between 0.71 and 1.00. We further introduce a cliff taxonomy of deterministic, uncertain, and sampled-off cliffs, defined by greedy choice and token entropy. Each type has distinct probabilistic characteristics, and the taxonomy generalizes across model scales. Finally, we validate the taxonomy via single-token preference optimization at cliff positions (Cliff-DPO). Trained on GSM8K, Cliff-DPO improves accuracy across benchmarks by up to +6.6. Optimizing at uncertain and sampled-off cliffs improves reasoning, while deterministic cliffs do not.

</details>


### [169] [Agentic evolution of physically constrained foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25532)
*Jiangwei Zhang,Wen Sun,Chong Wang,Shiyao Li,Cheng Che,Chunjing Han,Dan Meng,Jian Yang,Yu Wang,Rui Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具备物理约束意识的多智能体科学发现引擎，可自动设计硬件兼容的计算系统，并在基础模型压缩与部署中取得优于人工启发式方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用型科学发现智能体缺乏物理 grounding，容易生成与真实硬件不兼容的设计；因此需要一种能在严格硬件约束下进行自主发现与软硬件协同设计的方法。

Method: 框架以“进化知识图谱”组织既有科学创新，并从中提取算法式 Chain-of-Thought，将随机搜索转化为知识引导的结构进化；在基础模型部署场景中，自动演化硬件感知的压缩方法，包括 Q-Enhance、MoE-Salient-AQ，以及带宽高效的 Sensitivity Profile。

Result: 该引擎演化出两种硬件感知压缩方法：Q-Enhance 缓解 dense 模型长上下文精度损失；MoE-Salient-AQ 在低于 3-bit 的设置下较人工设计的稀疏 MoE 方法提升 3.7%。同时将 235B 参数模型部署到双 A100 服务器，内存需求降低 75%，精度仅下降 0.64%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，将组合搜索约束在知识驱动和物理边界内，可形成可扩展的硬件—软件协同设计范式，推动机器自主科学发现从无约束生成走向可落地的工程发现。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence increasingly drives automated scientific discovery, yet contemporary generalist agents lack physical grounding, frequently hallucinating hardware-incompatible designs. Here, we present a physically grounded, multi-agent discovery engine that autonomously architects hardware-compliant computing systems. Anchored by an Evolutionary Knowledge Graph structuring past scientific innovations, the framework extracts an "algorithmic Chain-of-Thought" to transform blind stochastic search into directed structural evolution. Applied to the extreme testbed of foundation model deployment, the engine evolved two hardware-aware compression methodologies surpassing human-engineered heuristics: Q-Enhance mitigates long-context accuracy loss in dense models, and MoE-Salient-AQ outperforms state-of-the-art manual sparse Mixture-of-Experts designs by 3.7% at sub-3-bit regimes. Utilizing a bandwidth-efficient Sensitivity Profile, we successfully deployed a massive 235-billion-parameter model onto a constrained dual-A100 server, reducing memory requirements by 75% with a marginal 0.64% accuracy degradation. By transforming unconstrained combinatorial search into knowledge-driven autonomy, this establishes a scalable hardware-software co-design paradigm for machine-driven discovery within strict physical boundaries.

</details>


### [170] [Reasonable Motion: A General ASP Foundation for Environment Constrained Movement Trajectory Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25626)
*Julius Monsen,Jakob Suchan,Mehul Bhatt,Lars Karlsson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于答案集编程的混合定量-定性方法，用于在真实环境中计算移动对象的受约束分支轨迹模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯学习式轨迹预测方法通常缺乏可验证解释性；真实动态场景中的移动对象轨迹还受到地图拓扑、事件序列、几何可行性和领域规范等多重约束影响，因此需要一种可解释、可追溯且能结合定量与定性因素的轨迹计算方法。

Method: 方法将环境表示为图，并通过答案集编程进行受约束遍历；几何上可接受的运动行为被枚举为稳定模型，每个稳定模型对应一种不同的轨迹模式，并由事件序列、地图拓扑、领域规范等领域相关和领域无关因素共同刻画。

Result: 作者展示了该方法在自动驾驶等动态领域中的适用性，并在 Argoverse 2 这一大规模真实自动驾驶基准上进行了实证评估；计算出的轨迹可追溯到其底层稳定模型。

Conclusion: 该方法为真实世界移动对象的轨迹模式计算提供了通用、可解释且可验证的框架，相比纯学习方法更强调逻辑可追溯性和领域约束建模能力。

Abstract: We present a general answer set programming based hybrid quantitative-qualitative method for computing constrained branching trajectory modes for moving objects in real-world settings. The method performs constrained traversal of an environment graph, enumerating geometrically admissible motion behaviours as stable models, each constituting a distinct trajectory mode characterised by both domain-dependent and independent factors such as derived event sequence, map topology, and domain norms. The hybrid trajectory computation method is generally applicable across motion characteristics typically encountered in diverse dynamic domains with moving objects, e.g., autonomous driving. We demonstrate applicability and highlight how computed trajectories are traceable to their underlying stable model, thereby affording verifiable interpretability that purely learned approaches cannot provide. We also perform an empirical evaluation with Argoverse 2, a large-scale real-world autonomous driving benchmark representative of the class of dynamic domains within the scope of the proposed method.

</details>


### [171] [GUI agent: Guided Exploration of User-Sensitive Screens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25705)
*Aradhana Nayak,Mussadiq Nazeer,Wang Peng,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种探索型智能体，从单个示范任务出发系统探索GUI任务查询空间，以识别会导致用户敏感状态的查询，从而支持LLM代理在关键场景中请求用户接管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在开放GUI环境中执行任务时可能遇到用户敏感信息或关键操作状态；现有代理通常被优化为无条件完成任务，缺乏对安全交接场景的识别能力，影响真实部署的可靠性。

Method: 构建一个explorer agent，以一个示范任务为起点，系统性扩展和探索可能的用户查询，并判断哪些查询在执行过程中会进入用户敏感GUI状态，进而用于定义和分类敏感查询/状态。

Result: 论文产出或旨在构建一个可帮助工程师识别关键场景的数据集，使代理能够在敏感状态前请求用户接管；摘要未提供具体实验指标或量化结果。

Conclusion: 该工作强调在GUI自动化代理中识别用户敏感状态的重要性，并提出通过查询空间探索生成敏感查询数据的方法，以提升LLM代理的安全性和部署可靠性。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly being used to automate tasks for users within an open GUI environment. They inevitably encounter screens containing user-sensitive information, for which takeover of task execution by the user is highly desirable or even necessary. State-of-the-art LLM-driven agents are usually fine-tuned to complete tasks regardless of the safety implications of their actions. This makes their real-world deployment difficult and adversely affects the reliability. Therefore, it is crucial to identify and categorize user-sensitive states and define user-sensitive queries. This dataset would be to engineers to recognize and request handover to the user in critical scenarios. This short paper develops an explorer agent that systematically explores the query space starting from one demonstrated task to identify queries that, if executed, would lead to user-sensitive states in a GUI environment.

</details>


### [172] [Position Spaces and Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25719)
*Rita-Nathalia Assaf,Tom Davot,Frédéric Lardeux,Frédéric Saubion*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 position graphs：一种基于位置空间形式化的图推理框架，用严格偏序刻画离散 token 的水平/垂直对齐与先后关系，并分析其一致性与模式发现复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用定性空间演算过于宽泛，难以专门刻画文档等离散 token 场景中的行列位置约束；因此需要一个更聚焦于行、列、相对顺序及代数一致性的形式化逻辑层。

Method: 作者定义 position graphs，以两个严格偏序分别表示水平与垂直方向上的对齐和先后关系，并施加链条件与兼容性约束；随后给出图一致性的刻画与充分/必要条件，并将结构模式发现形式化为诱导子图同构问题进行复杂度分析。

Result: 论文建立了 position graphs 一致性的理论条件，并证明即使限制在 position graphs 类中，诱导子图同构形式的结构模式发现问题仍然是 NP-complete。

Conclusion: position graphs 为基于位置约束的离散对象推理提供了独立于具体数据抽取技术的数学与逻辑基础，但其模式发现任务在计算上仍具有较高复杂性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce position graphs, a graph-based reasoning framework based on the formalization of position spaces. This framework utilizes two strict partial orders, representing horizontal and vertical alignment and precedence, to model the relative positions of discrete tokens. Unlike general qualitative spatial calculi, position graphs are constrained by a chain condition and compatibility requirements that focus on rows and columns. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis of this representation, beginning with a characterization of graph consistency. Conditions to ensure the consistency of position graphs are established. Furthermore, we investigate the computational complexity of structural pattern discovery, modeled as the induced subgraph isomorphism problem. We demonstrate that this problem remains NP-complete even within the restricted class of position graphs. While initially motivated by document processing, this work focuses on the underlying mathematical properties and algebraic consistency of position-based constraints, providing a formal logical layer that is independent of specific data extraction techniques.

</details>


### [173] [Fuzzy Quantification over OWL Ontologies and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25778)
*Enrique Palacín,Fernando Bobillo,Ignacio Huitzil,Francesca A. Lisi,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于在标准/模糊本体与知识图谱上评估模糊量化查询的通用框架，并发布实现系统 Q2S2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊量化查询方法往往受限于特定量词类型、评估策略或数据源，难以统一支持 OWL 本体、RDFS 知识图谱以及不同形式的模糊量化表达。

Method: 构建一个与量词类型、查询评估方法和底层数据源解耦的框架，用于检索满足 Type I 或 Type II 模糊量化表达式的个体；同时实现公开系统 Q2S2 以支撑复现和后续研究。

Result: 该框架能够适配标准本体、模糊本体和知识图谱，并支持不同类型的模糊量化查询；Q2S2 作为公开实现提供了可用的软件基础。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于提供了一个通用、可扩展且数据源无关的模糊量化查询评估框架，有助于推动语义网和模糊知识表示中的查询处理研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a versatile framework for evaluating fuzzy quantification queries over both standard and fuzzy ontologies as well as knowledge graphs. The primary objective is the retrieval of individuals that satisfy queries articulated via Type I or Type II fuzzy quantified expressions. A key advantage of the proposed approach is its inherent adaptability: it remains entirely agnostic to the quantifier type, the underlying evaluation method, and the specific data source of the ontology (i.e., OWL ontologies or RDFS knowledge graphs). Furthermore, we present Q2S2, a publicly accessible implementation of this system developed to support future research.

</details>


### [174] [Confidence Sequences for Online Statistical Model Checking of Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25797)
*Konstantin Kueffner,Tobias Meggendorfer,Maximilian Weininger,Patrick Wienhöft*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向在线采样场景的置信序列方法，用于在未知转移概率的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中更高效地估计真实值界限，平均所需样本量较现有先进方法减少约50倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MDP分析通常假设转移概率精确已知，但在网络物理系统、生物过程等实际建模中这一假设不现实。现有基于采样和统计置信界的实现存在细微正确性问题或样本效率不足，因此需要更可靠且高效的在线统计方法。

Method: 作者提出多种适用于在线设置的置信序列，用于在持续采样过程中为MDP转移概率及由此导出的真实值提供有效统计保证；同时将这些方法实现为高效工具，并与传统基于联合界的策略及既有最优实现进行实验比较。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的置信序列方法优于经典“union-bound”式方法；整体实现相比先前最先进方法平均减少约50倍样本需求，显示出显著的实际效率提升。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，针对在线采样专门设计的置信序列能够更正确、更高效地支持未知概率MDP的统计验证与值界估计，为不确定系统建模提供实用工具和更强性能。

Abstract: Markov decision processes (MDPs) are a classic model of decision making under uncertainty, exhibiting both non-deterministic choice as well as probabilistic uncertainty. Traditionally, exact knowledge of the underlying probabilities is assumed. However, this often is unrealistic, e.g.\ when modelling cyber-physical systems or biological processes. Here, statistical methods provide a way towards obtaining meaningful guarantees. The classical approach is to gather samples in the MDP, use these to draw statistical conclusions about the transition probabilities, and from there obtain bounds on the true value; then, if these bounds are too broad, repeat. However, existing implementations of this approach are either subtly incorrect or sub-optimal, and quite often both. We present several \emph{confidence sequences}, which are specifically designed for such \enquote{online} settings, implement all of them in an efficient tool, and show their practical applicability. In particular, we show that they outperform classical \enquote{union-bound} style approaches, and overall our implementation requires 50x less samples on average than previous state of the art.

</details>


### [175] [AI Snitches Get Glitches: Towards Evading Agentic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25836)
*Hyejun Jeong,Dzung Pham,Amir Houmansadr,Eugene Bagdasarian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: To better assist users with completing challenging tasks, AI agents mediate communications, access data, and interact with different APIs. Many employers (and even nation-states) already provide their users with this technology. However, widespread adoption of AI agents creates a new risk to abuse access to user data for another goal: surveilling users. These users might not even have the ability or permission to control the actions and data accesses of the surveilling agents.
  We introduce and formalize the problem of agentic surveillance: the ability of an AI agent to analyze available information, craft a report, and send it out using available tools. To evaluate surveillance capabilities across different models, we create SurveilBench, a dataset of various reporting scenarios focusing on three domains: corporate, education, and police. We find that some models exhibit emergent (i.e., unprompted) tendencies to help surveillance, but they also report the attempts to surveil users to the government.
  Finally, we repurpose prompt injections for evading surveillance and develop three evasion techniques that hide from, deceive, or induce over-escalation in surveillance agents. We conclude that agentic surveillance can already be easily implemented and, therefore, call for a comprehensive technical, ethical, and legislative framework to protect users.

</details>


### [176] [Agentic System as Compressor: Quantifying System Intelligence in Bits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25960)
*Zihan Qin,Hongrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用“压缩即智能”的视角评估智能体化大语言模型系统：在固定任务分布、接口与计算预算下，系统越强，重构目标对象所需比特数越少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从单一预测器发展为可调用工具、检索证据、遵循环境约束、使用验证器并进行搜索和多轮交互的智能体系统，传统评估难以统一刻画不同组件如何减少任务不确定性，因此需要一种可操作、可比较的分析框架。

Method: 作者将智能体能力形式化为编码长度降低问题，使用算术编码、种子编码与回退机制来度量重构目标所需比特数，并在反转文本、棋步、蛋白质序列、检索增强问答和语义故事压缩五类设置中进行小规模受控实验。

Result: 在所有五个实验设置中，引入智能体组件均降低了编码长度，表明工具调用、检索、验证、搜索与交互等机制能减少残余不确定性。

Conclusion: 编码长度可作为分析智能体系统组件、观察者信息与计算预算影响的统一指标，为真实智能体系统评估提供了有解释性的方向。

Abstract: Large language models are turning from isolated predictors into agentic systems: they call tools, retrieve evidence, obey environment constraints, use verifiers, and complete tasks through search and multi-turn interaction. We adopts an analytical viewpoint based on "compression is intelligence": under a fixed task distribution, interface, and compute budget, a stronger agentic system lets a target object be reconstructed with fewer bits. We operationalize the measure with arithmetic coding, seed coding, and a fallback, and evaluate it in five settings: reversed text, chess moves, protein sequences, retrieval-augmented question answering, and semantic story compression; in all of them agentic components reduce codelength. These small, controlled experiments cover component types typical of real agentic systems, show that codelength can analyze how components, observers, and budgets change residual uncertainty, and offer guidance for evaluating real agent systems.

</details>


### [177] [WinDOM: Self-Family Distillation for Small-Model GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25964)
