*Chengheng Li-Chen,Zhiqian Zhou,Hao Chen,Nicolas Chauvin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 WinDOM 数据集与 Self-Family Distillation（SFD）训练流程，用于提升约 2B 规模 GUI grounding 模型的端侧性能；结果显示未充分饱和的冷启动比完全收敛冷启动更适合作为 GRPO 初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模 GUI-grounding 智能体适合端侧部署、无障碍工具和低成本迭代，但面临两个关键问题：如何低成本获取带边界框的训练数据，以及如何有效结合监督微调与强化学习。

Method: 作者构建 WinDOM：通过 headless Playwright 驱动开源 Windows 11 网页复现环境，直接从 DOM 读取元素边界框，避免 OCR 与人工标注。训练上提出 Self-Family Distillation（SFD）：使用学生模型 EMA 或同家族更大冻结教师进行单轮拒绝采样冷启动，并将 SFD 冷启动的饱和深度作为 GRPO 的显式超参数。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-2B 学生模型上，欠饱和冷启动作为 GRPO 初始化优于收敛冷启动。SFD-4B 加 Early-init RL 相比基座模型 OOD 平均提升 +5.4，其中 ScreenSpot-Pro +3.5、OSWorld-G +7.0、ScreenSpot-V2 +5.8。同尺寸 EMA 模式在无需外部教师时达到 65.2 OOD mean，接近 4B 教师版本的 66.3。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，小模型 GUI grounding 性能可通过自动化 DOM 标注数据与合适深度的自家族蒸馏冷启动结合 GRPO 显著提升；强化学习初始化并非越充分收敛越好，欠饱和 SFD 可能保留更有利的探索与优化空间。

Abstract: Small ($\sim$2B) GUI-grounding agents are attractive for on-device deployment, accessibility tooling, and low-cost iteration, but at this scale they face two open recipe questions: how to obtain bounding-box training data without expensive human annotation, and how to combine supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. We address both, with the explicit goal of pushing small-model performance rather than scaling up. WinDOM is a $54{,}425$-record grounding corpus harvested by driving an open-source Windows 11 web reimplementation under headless Playwright, with bounding boxes read directly off the DOM and no OCR or human annotation. Self-Family Distillation (SFD) is a single rejection-sampling cold-start parameterised only by the teacher choice: either an EMA of the student (no external model) or a frozen larger same-family teacher. We then treat the saturation depth of the SFD cold-start as an explicit GRPO hyperparameter. On a Qwen3.5-2B student, the under-saturated cold-start is a better GRPO initialiser than the converged one: SFD-4B with Early-init RL gains $+5.4$ OOD-mean ($+3.5$ ScreenSpot-Pro, $+7.0$ OSWorld-G, $+5.8$ ScreenSpot-V2) over the base. The same-size EMA mode lands within roughly one OOD-mean point of the cross-size $4$B variant ($65.2$ vs $66.3$) without an external teacher.

</details>


### [178] [InvestPhilBench: A Multi-Layer Dynamic Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Model Procedural Reasoning in Expert Investment Philosophy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25984)
*Mingguang Chen,Bo Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 InvestPhilBench，用于评测大语言模型能否重构并应用专家投资者的程序化决策框架；结果显示传统复合评分可能掩盖模型在程序推理上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型越来越多地被用作投资研究助手，但缺乏专门检验其是否能准确复现专家投资决策流程、原则和框架的基准。

Method: 构建包含八个认知层级的动态基准，涵盖投资原则卡、决策框架卡和问答题；提出自动评分流水线 BASP、失败模式检测协议 FMDP，以及针对带有黄金推理程序题目的逐关卡指标 GRA。

Result: 开发集上的 sanity wave 显示模型提供方层级差异明显；BASP 在前沿模型上趋于饱和，但 GRA 揭示其在程序化决策重构上仍存在缺口。自动 BASP 与专家标注 gold set 的相关性为 Pearson r=0.72，MAE=0.10。

Conclusion: InvestPhilBench v0.6 主要是基准与方法论贡献，强调仅依赖流畅文本导向的复合指标会高估模型能力；未来 v1.0 将提供去混杂的多模型排行榜和完整三条件实验。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as investment research assistants, yet no benchmark tests whether they can accurately reconstruct and apply the specific procedural decision frameworks of expert investors. We introduce InvestPhilBench, a multi-layer dynamic benchmark spanning eight cognitive tiers, from principle identification (L1) to novel framework extrapolation (L8). The v0.6 release comprises 118 primary-source-verified investment principle cards, 25 decision framework cards with explicit topology metadata, and 243 QA questions (197 dev / 46 held-out test). For reproducible scoring at scale we introduce the Benchmark Automated Scoring Pipeline (BASP) -- five algorithmic metrics (OGRS, KCCS, SAP@k, IVP, CKCA) -- the Failure Mode Detection Protocol (FMDP) with computable rules for six failure modes, and Gate Reconstruction Accuracy (GRA), a per-gate metric for questions with gold reasoning programs. In this release, InvestPhilBench is primarily a benchmark-and-methodology contribution. A four-model sanity wave on the 188-question development split shows a sharp provider-tier split (BASP 0.906 vs. 0.438); these mixed-judge numbers are confounded upper bounds. The central finding: the BASP composite saturates at the frontier (Claude L4 = 0.932) while GRA still exposes a procedural deficit (frontier L4 GRA approx. 0.77, L7 GRA 0.57-0.62) -- composite scoring rewards fluent prose and hides the procedural gap. v0.6 implements a unified judge and true model-in-the-loop retrieval/oracle conditions; the de-confounded multi-model leaderboard and full three-condition run are v1.0 deliverables. On a 100-item expert-annotated gold set the automated BASP composite tracks the human reference at Pearson r = 0.72 (MAE = 0.10), with attribution (SAP@3) the weakest sub-metric and the failure-mode detector running sensitive-but-over-flagging.

</details>


### [179] [Autodata: An agentic data scientist to create high quality synthetic data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25996)
*Ilia Kulikov,Chenxi Whitehouse,Tianhao Wu,Yixin Nie,Swarnadeep Saha,Eryk Helenowski,Weizhe Yuan,Olga Golovneva,Jack Lanchantin,Yoram Bachrach,Jakob Foerster,Xian Li,Han Fang,Sainbayar Sukhbaatar,Jason Weston*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Autodata 提出一种让 AI 代理自动构建高质量训练与评估数据的方法，并通过元优化进一步提升数据生成能力与下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量数据是模型训练与评估的关键，但传统人工标注或经典合成数据方法成本高、灵活性有限，且难以持续利用更高推理算力来提升数据质量。论文希望将 AI 代理塑造成“数据科学家”，自动设计、生成并改进训练/评估数据。

Method: 论文提出 Autodata 的总体框架，并给出具体实现 Agentic Self-Instruct。该方法让数据科学家代理生成任务数据，同时对代理本身进行训练或元优化，使其学习如何产生更强的数据。实验覆盖计算机科学研究任务、法律推理任务以及数学对象推理任务，并与传统合成数据构建方法进行比较。

Result: 实验显示，Autodata/Agentic Self-Instruct 相比经典合成数据生成方法取得更好的效果；进一步对数据科学家代理进行元优化后，下游性能提升更明显。

Conclusion: Agentic data creation 能够把更多推理计算转化为更高质量的模型训练数据，可能改变 AI 数据构建方式，并为自动化数据生成与评估提供新的研究方向。

Abstract: We introduce Autodata, a general method that enables AI agents to act as data scientists who build high quality training and evaluation data. We show how to train (meta-optimize) such a data scientist agent, so that it learns to create even stronger data. We describe the overall formulation, and a specific practical implementation, Agentic Self-Instruct. We conduct experiments on computer science research tasks, legal reasoning tasks and reasoning with mathematical objects, where we obtain improved results compared to classical synthetic dataset creation methods. Further, meta-optimizing the data scientist agent itself delivers an even larger performance uplift. Agentic data creation provides a way to convert increased inference compute into higher quality model training. Overall, we believe this direction has the potential to change the way we build AI data.

</details>


### [180] [The Unfireable Safety Kernel: Execution-Time AI Alignment for AI Agents and Other Escapable AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26057)
*Seth Dobrin,Łukasz Chmiel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将AI代理授权控制移出代理运行时的架构：Unfireable Safety Kernel，通过进程隔离、结构唯一的预执行强制路径、请求与系统级 fail-closed、外部可验证签名证据，实现对可逃逸AI系统的执行时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理控制主要依赖系统提示、输出过滤器和护栏库等运行时内部机制；若代理输入能影响自身运行环境，这些控制可能被触达或规避。因此需要一种不依赖代理合作、位于其信任边界之外的授权机制。

Method: 作者定义授权机制需满足四项架构属性，并实现Rust参考系统 Unfireable Safety Kernel。其安全不变量通过Z3 SMT定理和Kani有界模型检查验证；Python到Rust迁移使用字节等价测试和对抗类别测试把关，并在真实可自修改AI系统中进行逃逸尝试评估。

Result: 在1000次自修改中，704次针对安全关键核心的尝试全部被拒绝且无逃逸；操作员终止开关下的300次尝试也全部被拒绝；另有6240次授权往返测试无成功绕过。

Conclusion: 论文主张AI代理控制不应放在代理可影响的运行时内，而应作为执行时AI对齐层外置实现；所提内核在形式验证和实验评估中展示了较强的 fail-closed 与不可绕过授权能力。

Abstract: AI agents are granted access to tools, APIs, and other infrastructure, making them active principals in those systems. The dominant approach places controls inside the agent's own runtime: system prompts, output filters, and guardrail libraries. Any control in the agent's address space is reachable by inputs that influence it; this generalizes to any AI system with sufficient reach into its own runtime, a class we term escapable AI systems.
  We identify four properties that an authorization mechanism must satisfy for architectural control rather than for cooperative requests: process separation, pre-action enforcement on a structurally only path, fail-closed at both the request and system levels, and externalized signed evidence verifiable outside the controlled system's trust boundary. We position this layer as execution-time AI alignment, complementing training-time alignment (RLHF, Constitutional AI) and inference-time alignment.
  We present the Unfireable Safety Kernel, a Rust reference implementation realizing all four. Its fail-closed invariant is machine-checked at two levels: an SMT theorem (Z3) and an exhaustive bounded-model-checking proof of the production decision function (Kani, 4/4 harnesses). A Python-to-Rust migration was gated on byte-equivalence (1000/1000 fixtures; 17/17 adversarial classes). We evaluate the kernel governing a live, escapable AI system, a deterministic, self-improving world model, against an escape-seeking adversary driving its real self-modification seam: across 1,000 self-modifications, all 704 attempts on the safety-critical core are refused, with no escape; a further 300, under the operator kill switch, are also refused. A separate campaign of 6,240 authorization round-trips had no successful bypass. Against 3 contemporary systems claiming the agent control plane, the agent invokes control; here, it lacks that choice.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Information from coincidences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25042)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的信息论变分恒等式，用混合重合计数的对数统摄多类经典结果，并扩展到多先验、非归一化和连续索引场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息论中许多变分公式与误差指数、测度变换、不常见模式重合等结果表面形式不同，但底层都涉及归一化常数、熵最大化或KL优化。论文旨在用一个代数恒等式统一这些分散结果，并推广经典Renyi熵/散度的变分表述。

Method: 作者构造针对任意先验族和实指数的混合计数期望，证明其对数同时等价于Boltzmann重合权重、指数族归一化项、最大熵值和KL重心最优值；再通过选择不同先验、指数与约束，将该恒等式特化到Sanov分解、Chernoff信息、Donsker-Varadhan/PAC-Bayes、率失真和生日阈值等场景。

Result: 该恒等式严格推广了一先验和二先验的Renyi熵/散度变分公式到W先验单纯形，并适用于非归一化及连续索引先验。它给出多先验PAC-Bayes惩罚中的显式“重合奖励”，并导出W元假设检验MAP误差指数的边受限单纯形最优形式；在语言模型预测和基因组调控序列两个大字母表任务上展示了应用。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是提供一个高度统一的代数框架，将多种信息论定理视为同一恒等式的特例，同时带来新的多先验泛化界和多元假设检验指数表达，并显示出在复杂序列建模分析中的实用潜力。

Abstract: We prove a single algebraic mixed coincidence identity that unifies a broad swath of information-theoretic variational results. For any family of priors $\{π_i\}$ and real exponents $\{ α_i \}$, the log of the mixed count $E_{x\simν}\!\left[\prod_{i=1}^W π_i^{α_i}(x)\right]$ is simultaneously a Boltzmann coincidence weight, an exponential-family normalizer, a maximum-entropy value, and a KL-barycenter optimum. The identity yields a unified derivation of classical cornerstones of information theory: concentration of empirical distributions (Sanov-type decompositions and Gibbs conditioning), hypothesis-testing error exponents (Chernoff information and its multi-way analogue), change-of-measure inequalities (Donsker-Varadhan and PAC-Bayes), and laws governing rare-pattern coincidences (Erdos-Renyi run-length, iterative guesswork, rate-distortion, and birthday thresholds). Each is recovered as a specialization of the same algebraic equality. It strictly generalizes the classical Renyi entropy and divergence variational formulas (one and two priors respectively) to a $W$-prior simplex, and holds for unnormalized and continuum-indexed priors. Among its consequences are an exact multi-prior PAC-Bayes penalty that subtracts an explicit "coincidence bonus" from the usual single-prior posterior penalty, and the asymptotic MAP error exponent for $W$-ary hypothesis testing as an edge-restricted simplex optimum. We demonstrate the calculus at scale on two large alphabets encoding richly modeled sequential languages: on language-model next-token predictives where we recover contrastive decoding, and on human genomic regulatory sequence where it separates correlated from diverse prior families along a sliding-window trace.

</details>


### [182] [Wideband Near-Field Channel Estimation Under Hybrid Compression: Cross-Subcarrier KL Covariance Fitting With OFDM Fresnel Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25101)
*Rıfat Volkan Şenyuva*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向XL-MIMO宽带近场信道估计的WB-CL-KL估计器，可直接在混合压缩观测域中联合估计角度与距离，并在代表性场景下接近压缩域CRB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在28 GHz、数百MHz带宽的XL-MIMO系统中，窄带极坐标域模型不再适用：近场Fresnel曲率随子载波变化，且少量RF链的混合压缩观测破坏了传统窄带方法依赖的逐子载波空间协方差结构。因此需要一种能处理宽带、近场、压缩观测条件的信道估计方法。

Method: 提出Wideband Cross-subcarrier Kullback–Leibler（WB-CL-KL）估计器，直接利用压缩样本协方差，在OFDM多子载波间拟合结构化Fresnel协方差模型，并通过跨子载波KL散度准则联合估计路径角度与距离；同时基于Slepian–Bangs公式推导宽带压缩域CRB，并分析宽带增益来源。

Result: 在B=400 MHz时，宽带压缩域CRB相对窄带界的增益分解为数据多样性+27.093 dB、几何多样性+0.701 dB，总计+27.793 dB。单路径LOS场景中，SNR=10 dB时WB-CL-KL的距离RMSE为19.8 mm，接近19.9 mm的CRB，比例为0.996；在3GPP UMi路径损耗与阴影衰落SNR分布下，在中位部署SNR=9.6 dB处达到0.959的界比。

Conclusion: WB-CL-KL能够在无需全阵列重构的情况下实现宽带XL-MIMO近场参数估计，并充分利用跨子载波的频率相关几何信息；其性能在典型部署条件下接近压缩域CRB，说明该方法适合混合模拟-数字架构下的大规模宽带近场信道估计。

Abstract: We consider wideband channel estimation for extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO) arrays under hybrid analog-digital compression, in which a uniform linear array (ULA) is observed through far fewer radio-frequency (RF) chains than antennas. At a carrier frequency of 28 GHz with bandwidths reaching several hundred MHz, the standard narrowband polar-domain channel model fails: the near-field Fresnel curvature becomes subcarrier-dependent, and the compressed observation destroys the per-subcarrier spatial covariance structure that narrowband methods exploit. We propose the Wideband Cross-subcarrier Kullback--Leibler (WB-CL-KL) estimator, which jointly estimates angle and range directly from the compressed sample covariance, without full-array reconstruction, by fitting a structured Fresnel covariance model across orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) subcarriers via a cross-subcarrier Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence criterion. We also derive the wideband compressed-domain Cramér--Rao bound (CRB) -- the performance lower bound for this hybrid architecture -- from the Slepian--Bangs formula, and decompose its gain over the narrowband bound into a data-diversity component of +27.093 dB and a geometric-diversity component of +0.701 dB, totalling +27.793 dB at B = 400 MHz (Propositions 1 and 2). In the single-path line-of-sight regime, WB-CL-KL attains a range root-mean-square error of 19.8 mm against a 19.9 mm bound at signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) = 10 dB, a ratio of 0.996. Under the 3GPP Urban Micro (UMi) path-loss and shadow-fading SNR distribution, it achieves a bound ratio of 0.959 at the median deployment SNR of 9.6 dB, indicating near-CRB operation at the representative deployment point, where the compressed-domain bound is evaluated at the scene-median geometry.

</details>


### [183] [Inner and Outer Bounds on the Secrecy Capacity of Degraded Broadcast Channels with RMSI and Transmitter CSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25351)
*Saeid Pakravan,Mohsen Ahmadzadeh,Ghosheh Abed Hodtani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究带外部窃听者、非因果CSI和接收端消息边信息的退化广播信道保密容量，给出互补与非互补RMSI场景下的内外界，并在特定退化设置中刻画容量区域，扩展到高斯信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广播信道研究较少同时考虑保密约束、信道状态信息（CSI）与接收端消息边信息（RMSI）之间的相互作用；该工作旨在明确这些信息结构如何影响多用户安全通信的容量极限。

Method: 针对两个合法接收者各自接收独立保密消息且存在外部窃听者的退化广播信道，分别分析互补RMSI和非互补RMSI两类场景；构造可达保密速率区域内界，推导紧外界，并进一步将结论推广到高斯退化广播信道。

Result: 论文得到两类RMSI场景下新的可达保密速率区域，并在所考虑的退化信道条件下通过匹配内外界建立保密容量区域；高斯情形表明CSI能够显著提升安全传输性能。

Conclusion: RMSI与非因果CSI可被联合利用以扩大保密容量区域，该结果为具有边信息和信道状态的安全多用户通信系统设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This paper studies the secrecy capacity of a class of degraded broadcast channels in the presence of an external eavesdropper, where a transmitter aims to deliver two independent confidential messages to two legitimate receivers. The transmitter is assumed to have non-causal access to the channel state information (CSI), and each legitimate receiver possesses prior knowledge of the other receiver's message, referred to as receiver message side information (RMSI). We consider two distinct scenarios: complementary RMSI, where each receiver knows only the other's message, and non-complementary RMSI, where the side information does not perfectly align. For both scenarios, we derive novel inner bounds on the achievable secrecy rate region and present tight outer bounds, establishing the secrecy capacity region for the considered degraded channel settings. Unlike prior works, which primarily address general broadcast settings without secrecy constraints or omit key interactions between RMSI and CSI, our results provide a complete characterization of the secure communication limits under these conditions. Moreover, we extend our analysis to the Gaussian degraded broadcast channel, highlighting the pivotal role of CSI in enhancing secure transmission performance. Our findings demonstrate that the combination of RMSI and CSI can be strategically leveraged to expand the secrecy capacity region, thus offering new insights into secure multiuser communication system design.

</details>


### [184] [OAMP-Aided Joint Channel Estimation and Data Detection for ODDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25420)
*Kehan Huang,Min Qiu,Akram Shafie,Jinhong Yuan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于ODDM双选择性信道中联合信道估计与数据检测的OAMP-JED接收机，通过交替运行两个OAMP模块并进行软信息交换，在未知CSI条件下实现接近完美CSI的BER性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ODDM在双选择性信道中需要同时解决信道估计与数据检测问题；传统分离式方法容易受信道估计误差和误差传播影响，导致BER性能下降，因此需要一种高效、稳健的联合处理接收机。

Method: 作者构建双线性交叉域JED模型，并将其线性化为信道估计和数据检测两个子问题；设计两个交替执行的OAMP模块，通过变分噪声项建模不确定性，并利用OAMP误差正交性推导闭式标量方差更新，实现模块间原则性的软信息交换。

Result: 仿真表明，在未编码和编码ODDM场景下，OAMP-JED相比基准方案具有更低的BER，并且其性能接近使用完美CSI的OAMP检测器。

Conclusion: OAMP-JED能够有效缓解联合估计检测中的误差传播，在双选择性信道ODDM系统中提供接近理想CSI条件的可靠检测性能，具有较强的实用潜力。

Abstract: In this work, to address the challenge of joint channel estimation and data detection (JED) for orthogonal delay-Doppler (DD) division multiplexing (ODDM) in doubly selective channels, we propose an orthogonal approximate message passing (OAMP)-aided JED (OAMP-JED) receiver. We first formulate a bilinear cross-domain JED model, which can be linearized into separate channel estimation and data detection subproblems. The proposed OAMP-JED receiver alternately executes two OAMP modules for these subproblems, effectively coupled through a variational noise term to account for model uncertainty. Leveraging OAMP's error orthogonality, we derive closed-form scalar-variance updates to enable efficient and principled soft information exchange between the modules, thereby mitigating error propagation during JED. Simulation results show that, for both uncoded and coded ODDM, OAMP-JED achieves a lower bit error rate (BER) than benchmark schemes. Moreover, its BER performance closely approaches that of OAMP with perfect CSI.

</details>


### [185] [Center-Fed Pinching Antenna System for Uplink Environment Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25423)
*Cong Yu,Xu Gan,Bo Ai,Fellow,Yuanwei Liu,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出中心馈电 pinching 天线系统（C-PASS）用于上行环境感知，相比传统端馈 PASS 具备双倍自由度，并在重构误差界与信号分离稳定性上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端馈 PASS 在上行环境感知中自由度有限，可能限制散射信号分离能力与环境重构精度。论文希望通过中心馈电结构提升系统自由度，并从理论上刻画其性能优势。

Method: 构建 C-PASS 上行感知框架，建立基于环境目标散射信号的线性逆模型进行环境重构；推导稳定分离接收信号所需馈电点间距的闭式表达；进一步分别推导 C-PASS 与端馈 PASS 的均方重构误差 Ziv-Zakai Bound（ZZB）表达式，并进行理论比较与数值验证。

Result: 理论证明 C-PASS 的重构误差下界严格低于传统端馈 PASS；数值结果验证 ZZB 推导准确性，并表明 C-PASS 可实现更稳定的接收信号分离，同时在不同设置下保持一致性能优势。

Conclusion: C-PASS 通过中心馈电结构显著提升上行环境感知能力，在自由度、信号分离稳定性和环境重构误差方面均优于传统端馈 PASS，是一种更有效的 PASS 感知架构。

Abstract: A center-fed pinching antenna system (C-PASS)-enabled uplink environment sensing framework is proposed. Through the center-fed framework, doubled degrees of freedom is achieved compared to conventional end-fed PASS. Based on this, we consider an uplink sensing scenario, in which a linear inverse model is developed to reconstruct the environment through signals scattered by the environment object. In the proposed framework, the distance between the feed points for stable separation of the received signals is characterized in closed form. Furthermore, Ziv-Zakai bound (ZZB) expressions for the mean-squared reconstruction error are derived for C-PASS and end-fed PASS. Based on these theoretical results, it can be proved that C-PASS achieves a strictly lower reconstruction error bound than conventional PASS for uplink environment sensing. Finally, numerical results validate the accuracy of the derived ZZB expressions and 1) demonstrate that C-PASS provides more stable separation of the received signals, and 2) confirm the consistent performance advantages of C-PASS.

</details>


### [186] [A Path-Survival Analytical Framework for SCL Decoding of Polar Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25522)
*Xianbin Wang,Zhichao Liu,Yuan Li,Huazi Zhang,Jiajie Tong,Jun Wang,and Wen Tong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于分析极化码 CRC 辅助连续抵消列表（CA-SCL）译码性能的路径存活模型，可高效预测译码表现，避免依赖大量列表级蒙特卡洛仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CA-SCL 译码在实际极化码系统中应用广泛，但其理论性能分析仍不成熟。相比 LDPC 码可借助密度演化等工具分析 BP 译码，CA-SCL 由于存在复杂的路径剪枝机制，难以直接套用现有分析方法。

Method: 论文构建了一个基于“正确路径排名演化”的路径存活模型，用于刻画 CA-SCL 译码过程中正确路径在候选列表中的保留与淘汰过程，从而形成性能预测分析框架。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，该框架在不同码长、码率、列表大小和信道模型下均能有效预测 CA-SCL 译码性能，并减少对穷尽式列表特定蒙特卡洛仿真的需求。

Conclusion: 该工作为 CA-SCL 译码提供了一种实用且高效的理论分析工具，填补了极化码 CA-SCL 译码性能预测方面的部分空白。

Abstract: A theoretical analysis of CRC-aided successive cancellation list (CA-SCL) decoding for polar codes remains an open problem, despite its widespread practical adoption. While low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes benefit from mature analytical tools, such as density evolution (DE), for predicting the performance of belief-propagation (BP) decoding, similar techniques are not directly applicable to CA-SCL decoding. This limitation stems from the complex path-pruning mechanism inherent in CA-SCL decoding. In this paper, we propose an analytical framework based on a novel path-survival model that captures the evolution of the correct path's rank during decoding. The proposed framework enables efficient prediction of CA-SCL decoding performance without requiring exhaustive list-specific Monte Carlo simulations. Extensive numerical evaluations demonstrate its effectiveness across a wide range of code lengths, code rates, list sizes, and channel models.

</details>


### [187] [Interplay between VAoI, Packet Error Rate, and Delay for Energy-Efficient Remote Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25566)
*Yasaman Khorsandmanesh,Zinat Behdad,Emil Bjornson,Cicek Cavdar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限块长无线链路下事件触发远程监测的短包传输能量优化，在VAoI约束下通过发射功率分配降低长期平均能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件触发传感器仅在源状态变化时生成更新，短包通信存在有限块长导致的解码错误与时延影响，因此需要在信息新鲜度（VAoI）要求与能量消耗之间进行系统优化。

Method: 将VAoI演化建模为马尔可夫链，刻画其与数据包错误率（解码错误概率）和平均时延的耦合关系；进一步构建在VAoI约束下最小化长期平均能耗的发射功率分配问题，并采用低复杂度搜索方法求解。

Result: 数值结果表明，更新到达概率和块长会显著影响能量—VAoI权衡；相较于最小化单次传输能量，优化长期平均能耗可显著降低总体能耗。

Conclusion: 面向事件触发远程监测的短包无线传输，应联合考虑有限块长可靠性、平均时延和VAoI动态；长期能耗导向的功率分配比逐次传输能量最小化更适合实现能效与信息新鲜度的平衡。

Abstract: This letter studies energy optimization of short-packet transmission for event-triggered remote monitoring over finite-blocklength wireless links. A wireless sensor node generates updates only when the source state changes, and freshness is measured by the Version Age of Information (VAoI). We model the VAoI evolution as a Markov chain and show its coupling with the packet error rate, characterized by decoding error probability, and average delay. Then, we formulate a transmit-power allocation problem that minimizes the long-term average energy consumption under a VAoI constraint and solve it using a low-complexity search method. Numerical results show that the update arrival probability and blocklength strongly affect the energy--VAoI tradeoff, and that optimizing long-term energy consumption can substantially reduce energy compared with minimizing the energy per transmission.

</details>


### [188] [The Condition for Structured Coding to Improve Random Coding in the Binary Modulo-sum Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25570)
*Yohsuke Tsujino,Shun Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文解析了多字母扩展 Ahlswede-Han 编码在模和问题中优于 Slepian-Wolf 编码的解析条件，并证明该条件与已知 SW 最优充分条件的补集一致，具有紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模和问题是分布式源编码中的典型问题。已有 KM 结构化线性编码和 AH 混合编码可改进传统 SW 区域，但多字母 AH 编码何时能严格优于 SW 过去主要依赖数值验证，缺乏解析刻画。

Method: 作者利用类型方法将多字母率表达式的评估化简为单字母散度之间的比较，从而推导多字母扩展 AH 编码改进 SW 编码的解析条件。

Result: 给出了多字母扩展 AH 编码优于 SW 编码的充分且紧的条件；该条件与已知 SW 最优充分条件的补集相吻合，并解释了此前数值结果背后的理论原因。

Conclusion: 研究从解析层面证明并刻画了多字母 AH 编码相对 SW 编码的改进范围，同时提出的类型方法化简技巧可能对其他多字母信息论表达式分析具有独立价值。

Abstract: The modulo-sum problem, proposed by Körner and Marton (KM), is a representative problem in the field of distributed source coding. In the modulo-sum problem, two correlated sources are encoded separately, and the decoder decodes the modulo-sum of the sources. It is clear that the Slepian-Wolf (SW) coding rate region is achievable for the modulo-sum problem. Körner and Marton proved that the SW coding rate region can be improved by structured coding based on linear codes. Ahlswede and Han (AH) proposed AH coding, which combines structured coding and random coding, and expressed its rate using auxiliary random variables. However, it was conjectured that the minimum sum rate of AH coding cannot be smaller than the minimum of the sum rates achievable by KM coding or SW coding. Subsequently, Kakishima and Watanabe considered a multi-letter extension of AH coding, and designed the auxiliary random variables by taking the XOR of adjacent bits of the source sequences. Through numerical computation, they demonstrated the existence of source parameters for which multi-letter AH coding improves upon SW coding. However, this confirmation remained numerical, and the conditions under which multi-letter extended AH coding improves upon SW coding have not been analytically characterized.
  In this study, we analytically characterize the conditions under which multi-letter extended AH coding improves upon SW coding. Our condition is tight in the sense that it coincides with the complement of the known SW optimal sufficient condition. To obtain the conditions, we apply the method of types to reduce the evaluation of the multi-letter expression to a comparison of single-letter divergences, which might be of independent interest.

</details>


### [189] [Performance Analysis for Heterogeneous Air-Ground ISAC in Coordinated Multipoint Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25574)
*Yihang Jiang,Xiaoyang Li,Guangxu Zhu,Changsheng You,Xiaowen Cao,Dingzhu Wen,Bingpeng Zhou,Xinyi Wang,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向低空经济场景的空地异构网络级 ISAC 架构，利用 CoMP 与混合单/双基地协同感知提升通信与感知性能，并揭示二者在多基站协作和网络密度变化下的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空经济需要高可靠、强集成的无线系统同时支撑通信与感知功能。现有 ISAC 研究多集中于地面环境中的链路级或单小区架构，对网络级协同空地 ISAC 的潜力探索不足，因此需要面向低空场景设计可扩展的协同网络架构与性能分析框架。

Method: 论文提出基于协调多点 CoMP 的异构空地 ISAC 网络架构，引入协同混合单基地/双基地感知方案以增强空间分集和感知能力。网络采用两层基站部署：主基站按六边形格点分布，从基站按泊松点过程随机分布；同时服务地面用户通信与空中目标感知，并建立覆盖关键信道与网络参数的通信和感知整体性能分析框架。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在多基站协作和不同网络密度条件下，通信性能与感知性能之间存在内在权衡；网络协作和密度变化会显著影响 ISAC 系统的整体表现。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了网络级协同空地 ISAC 在低空经济场景中的应用潜力，并为可扩展、高效率 ISAC 网络的实际部署提供了架构设计和参数配置方面的参考。

Abstract: The emergence of the \textit{low-altitude economy} (LAE) calls for highly integrated and reliable wireless systems that can simultaneously support \textit{communication and sensing} (C\&S) functions. Although \textit{integrated sensing and communication} (ISAC) has been widely studied, most existing works focused on link-level or single-cell architectures in terrestrial environments, leaving the potential of network-level cooperative air-ground ISAC largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, a heterogeneous air-ground ISAC network architecture based on \textit{coordinated multipoint} (CoMP) is proposed, which incorporates a cooperative hybrid mono/bi-static sensing scheme to enhance spatial diversity and sensing capability. In the proposed architecture, a two-tier \textit{base station} (BS) deployment is adopted: master BSs are arranged in a hexagonal lattice, while slave BSs follow a Poisson point process distribution. This structure concurrently supports communication for terrestrial users and sensing for aerial targets. A holistic performance analysis framework for both C\&S is further developed, accounting for key channel and network parameters. Simulation results reveal inherent trade-offs between C\&S performance, especially under multi-BS cooperation and varying network density. These findings provide practical guidance for the deployment of scalable and efficient ISAC networks in LAE scenarios.

</details>


### [190] [The MDS or NMDS for Modified GRS codes with flexible hull dimensions and lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25662)
*Zhonghao Liang,Qunying Liao,Jun Zhang,Xiaoping Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究MGRS与EMGRS这类非GRS型码，刻画其MDS/NMDS性质、重量分布、LCD与hull结构，并证明其与椭圆曲线NMDS码的线性不等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非GRS型MDS、NMDS、LCD码以及线性码的hull在密码学与编码理论中具有重要应用。已有Wang等提出的MGRS和EMGRS码提供了一类新的非GRS构造，亟需系统分析其距离性质、重量分布、hull维数可控性及与已知码族的关系。

Method: 论文围绕MGRS及其扩展EMGRS码展开：证明若干子类必为MDS或NMDS；推导成为NMDS的充要条件；对一类NMDS MGRS/EMGRS码完全确定重量分布；构造满足欧几里得LCD或一维欧几里得hull的MGRS码；构造性证明Hermitian hull维数和长度可灵活调节；并利用Schur乘积分析其与椭圆曲线NMDS码的线性不等价性。

Result: 得到两类MGRS/EMGRS码的MDS或NMDS分类及NMDS判定条件；给出一类NMDS码的完整重量分布；构造了四类欧几里得LCD或一维欧几里得hull的MGRS码；证明存在长度与Hermitian hull维数可调的MGRS码；并展示NMDS MGRS码与椭圆曲线NMDS码线性不等价。

Conclusion: 该工作系统扩展了非GRS型MDS/NMDS码的理论，提供了具有可控LCD与hull性质的新型MGRS码构造，并通过不等价性分析说明这些码并非已有椭圆曲线NMDS码的简单变体。

Abstract: Non-generalized Reed-Solomon (in short, non-GRS) type maximum distance separable (in short, MDS), near MDS (in short, NMDS), and linear complementary dual (in short, LCD) codes, as well as the hull of linear codes have interesting practical applications in cryptography and coding theory. In this paper, we focus on a class of non-GRS codes and its extended codes, i.e., modified generalized Reed-Solomon (MGRS) codes and extended MGRS (EMGRS) codes introduced by Wang et al. in 2026. Firstly, we prove that two classes of MGRS codes and EMGRS codes are either MDS or NMDS, derive the necessary and sufficient conditions for these codes to be NMDS, and then completely determine the weight distributions for one class of these NMDS MGRS or NMDS EMGRS codes. Secondly, we construct four classes of MGRS codes which are either Euclidean LCD codes or one-dimensional Euclidean hull codes. Thirdly, we constructively prove that there exist MGRS codes with flexible Hermitian hull dimensions and lengths. In addition, we illustrate the linearly inequivalence of NMDS MGRS codes and elliptic curve NMDS codes by Schur product. Finally, some corresponding examples are given.

</details>


### [191] [MAP-Based Task-Oriented Precoding for Multiuser Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25722)
*Mohammad Javad Ahmadi,Rafael F. Schaefer,H. Vincent Poor*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向任务的多用户无线通信框架，用于信道受损条件下的分布式分类，通过MAP驱动设计直接提升接收端分类可分性，并降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线分布式分类方法多依赖协方差矩阵建模或重构目标，通常需要重复进行协方差求逆和特征分解，计算开销较高；同时，重构信号并不一定最优地服务于最终分类任务。因此需要一种更直接面向分类性能、且低复杂度的系统设计方法。

Method: 作者基于MAP准则建立任务导向的系统设计框架，推导出可处理的类均值分离目标；在该目标下联合设计学习式特征提取与无线预编码策略，使经过信道失真后的特征仍具有更好的类别可分性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法相比已有协方差型和重构型方案取得更高的分类准确率，同时显著减少计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，在无线感知/通信融合的分布式分类场景中，直接优化分类相关的类间可分性比传统重构导向或协方差导向方法更有效，并且更适合低复杂度实现。

Abstract: We propose a task-oriented multiuser wireless communication framework for distributed classification based on a MAP-driven system design under wireless channel impairments. By deriving a tractable class-mean separation objective, the proposed approach enables low-complexity design of both learning-based feature extraction and precoding strategies. Unlike existing covariance-based and reconstruction-oriented methods, the proposed formulation avoids repeated covariance inversions and eigen-decomposition operations while directly improving class separability after channel distortion. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher classification accuracy than existing schemes, while simultaneously reducing computational complexity.

</details>
